# Full finding set — ntnu-page audit
160 survived adversarial verification, 15 unverified (verifier lost to session limit), 34 refuted.
Severity mix: {"blocker":12,"major":60,"minor":82,"polish":21}
## BLOCKER (12)
### [conf-1] /emne/ and /emner/ print "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026" for ~30% of courses that ARE taught that semester — the same page renders their autumn timetable directly below
- Where: `src/components/site/planClash.ts:111` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Traced and reproduced. `semesterLectures` (planClash.ts:74) returns `lecturesOnly(entriesInSemester(entries, teachingWeeks))`; `planClash` line 111 then does `if (own.length === 0) return { kind: "off-semester" }`, which `clashSentence` line 154 renders as "Undervises ikke i {term} — ingen kollisjon å sjekke." That branch cannot distinguish "no entries this semester" from "entries exist, classifier recognised no lecture". Live Playwright against 127.0.0.1:8788 with np:plans={"26h":[TDT4100]} and np:lastSemester=26h: /emne/IIK4100/ → "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026 — ingen kollisjon å sjekke."; /emne/SPA1100/ → same; /emne/LKRO001E/ → same; /emne/TDT4136/ → "Ingen kollisjon i planen din for Høst 2026." (control). The full body dump of /emne/IIK4100/ contains, in this order: "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026 — ingen kollisjon å sjekke." … "TIMEPLAN / Viser Høst 2026." … a Tuesday block "IIK4100 · A154, B22 · 09:15 · Lecture and Lab exercise · 2 grupper · uke 34–46" … "Ingen aktiviteter er merket som forelesning i disse emnene — viser all undervisning." So the grid does the honest B7a thing on the same page where the sentence asserts the opposite. Independent scale check (my own, not the auditor's): I bundled the SHIPPED activity.ts/schedule.ts with esbuild and ran a deterministic 120-course sample from public/data/search-index.json against the live /api/course/:code/timetable?year=2026 — 41 courses had entries intersecting 26h teachingWeeks [34..47], and 12 of those 41 (29.3%) produced zero lecture-classified entries: AAR4896, BK3190, FI2002, IDG4210, LITT3500, MGLU1520, MIB4106, ODA3901, PPU4482, RAG2302, SYGF1001, TKP4101.
- Failure: Student has TDT4100 in their 26h plan and opens /emne/IIK4100/. The clash line says "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026 — ingen kollisjon å sjekke." while the timetable island two paragraphs down draws IIK4100 Tuesday 09:15–12:00, uke 34–46. IIK4100 would collide with any Tuesday-morning course in their plan; the student adds it believing the tool checked and found nothing to check.
- Fix: In planClash.ts, have `semesterLectures` return both the in-semester entries and the lecture subset (or return `{ inSemester, lectures }`). Add a `{ kind: "unclassified" }` verdict for "in-semester entries exist but none classify as a lecture" and render it as DR-6's honest gap (e.g. "Vi klarte ikke å lese undervisningstypen for dette emnet — sjekk timeplanen selv."), reserving `off-semester` for `entriesInSemester(...).length === 0`. The `error` branch already models exactly this shape, and grid.ts's `mutedLayerAutoRevealed` already ships the matching copy.
- Verifier: I tried to kill this three ways. (1) Guard elsewhere: /emne/[code].astro:466 passes `own = sameYear ? await timetable : []`, so a genuinely off-catalog course legitimately gets `[]` — but IIK4100 is offered:true, sourceYear===2026, so the real entries do reach planClash and the branch still fires. (2) Documented decision: activity.ts's header justifies under-classification as "silently degraded, not confidently wrong" — that argument is explicitly about the toggle layer and holds for grid.ts, but this surface has no toggle layer and turns the degradation into a false assertion. Nothing in PRODUCT/ROADMAP/REVIEW sanctions it. (3) Scale: I did not trust the auditor's 31% and re-measured independently with the shipped code over my own 120-course sample; got 29.3%. Survived. I stand behind blocker rather than the usual downgrade because the output is a factual falsehood that contradicts data rendered on the same page, on the surface PRODUCT §6 calls "the verb, everywhere".
### [cpc-1] planClash is not section-aware: it skips applyGroupSelection, so /emne/[code]/ and the add-course modal paint a red collision the planner grid never draws
- Where: `src/components/site/planClash.ts:112` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of S7
- Evidence: planClash.ts:88-93 claims parity with "the same section-aware filter the grid (groups.ts/grid.ts) already applies", and PRODUCT.md:246 states "all three surfaces now share the same section-aware collision path". Both are false. planClash.ts:112 and :117 apply only `entriesForProgram(...)`; `grep -n "groups\|applyGroupSelection\|defaultLectureKeys" src/components/site/planClash.ts` returns nothing, so neither `defaultLectureKeys` nor `PlanCourse.groups` (the shipped popover picker) is ever consulted. grid.ts:203 calls `applyGroupSelection(timetable, state.course.groups, state.programCode)`.

Traced with real modules against live data: TDT4110's three lecture parallels carry studyProgramKeys that never mention MTDT (`Forelesningsparallell 1` = BMAT,MTINGGEO,MTELSYS,MTBYGG d5 08:15; `2` = BMAT,MTNANO,… d3 08:15; `3` = MBIOT5,… d1 14:15), so `entriesForProgram(entries,"MTDT")` is a no-op and planClash diffs all three. An esbuild bundle of the REAL src/lib/planner modules (applyGroupSelection + entriesInSemester + lecturesOnly + findConflicts) over the live API prints: `TDT4110 grid lectures: Forelesningsparallell 1 d5 08:15` … `GRID conflicts involving TDT4110: (none)` / `ALL grid conflicts: (none)`.

Live Chromium, np:plans={26h:[HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300]} + np:profile MTDT: `/emne/TDT4110/` renders #emne-clash = "Kolliderer med TMA4412, onsdag 08:15." in the .np-note-clash red. TMA4412's Forelesning d3 08:15 collides only with TDT4110's Forelesningsparallell 2 — the parallel the grid discards.

The same defect is inside /planlegger/: addCourse.ts:150 calls planClash with `programCode` and no group state, so the add-course modal's hover note and the grid behind it disagree by construction.
- Failure: MTDT kull 2026 student (the flagship programme prefill) opens /emne/TDT4110/ or hovers TDT4110 in the planner's add-course modal. Both say "Kolliderer med TMA4412, onsdag 08:15." in collision red, so they don't add it. Add it anyway and the grid draws TDT4110 on Friday 08:15 with zero conflicts. DR-1: "a false red is the one failure mode DR-1 exists to prevent."
- Fix: In planClash.ts's `semesterLectures`, replace the bare `entriesForProgram` narrowing at :112/:117 with `applyGroupSelection(entriesInSemester(entries, weeks), selectedGroups, programCode)` before `lecturesOnly` — that is the single call grid.ts:203 makes, so the surfaces stop disagreeing by construction. Thread each partner's `PlanCourse.groups` (`others` already carries the full PlanCourse) and, for the candidate, the plan's own pick when it has one. Add a unit test in tests/site/planClash.test.ts asserting a multi-parallel course whose non-default parallel overlaps a plan course returns `clear`.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways. (1) Maybe entriesForProgram already covers it — no: TDT4110's parallels do not name MTDT, so it is a documented no-op (schedule.ts:90). (2) Maybe the grid is the one that's wrong (planClash being conservative) — the grid's parallel-1 default is a heuristic, but DR-1 explicitly names the false RED, not a false green, as the forbidden failure, and PRODUCT.md:246 asserts parity that does not exist. (3) Maybe it's unreachable — reproduced end to end in headless Chromium with the flagship MTDT prefill, and the grid-side comparison was run through the actual repo modules, not a reimplementation. Auditor's line/file/fix are correct; I add that addCourse.ts:150 puts the same disagreement inside the planner itself, which raises rather than lowers the severity.
### [edit-1] A manually-added course that is also in the programme prefill is stored twice: credits double-count, the exam list invents a same-day collision, and Dropp stops working
- Where: `src/lib/planner/store.ts:386` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: p4.mjs: fresh planner -> "Legg til emne" -> TDT4109 (hash `#26h;-;%2BTDT4109`, "7,5 av 30 sp") -> studieinfo -> MTDT kull 2026 -> Lagre. Result (screenshot O-duplicate-check.png, dump O-after-program-pick): hash `#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300,%2BTDT4109`; np:plans holds two TDT4109 objects (`source:"program"` and `source:"manual"`); the Emner rail lists TDT4109 twice ("Dropp / fra programmet" and "Fjern / lagt til selv"); the credit line reads "37,5 av 30 sp" with `#planner-credit-note` hidden; the exam list renders `lø 9. des TDT4109` twice with a red "samme dag" connector and the verdict `#planner-exam-status` = "2 eksamener samme dag". Cause: `setProgramPlan` keeps every `source:"manual"` course untouched (store.ts:386) and appends the freshly derived programme set beside it with no code-level dedupe, while `addCourse`'s own guard (store.ts:305) only sees codes already present. `sameProgramSet` (plannerApp.ts:234) compares only `courses.filter(source==="program")`, so the duplicate survives every reload. Follow-on, verified in p9.mjs (screenshot AE-after-dropping-duplicated-course.png): pressing "Dropp" on the programme TDT4109 row grays the row and moves the total to "30 av 30 sp" in accent green, but the grid block codes are unchanged (`["TMA4412","EXPH0300","TMA4400","TDT4109"]` before and after, blockCount 7 both times) and TDT4109's exam is still listed — the manual twin keeps feeding `orderedActiveStates`.
- Failure: A student who adds TDT4109 from /emner/ or /emne/TDT4109/ (both call `store.addCourse` with the default `source:"manual"`) and then picks MTDT kull 2026 — the site's own recommended funnel — is told their autumn is "37,5 av 30 sp" (overloaded by a course they have once) and that they have "2 eksamener samme dag" on 9 January, which is the product's flagship sanity check answering PRODUCT §1's primary question with a fabricated clash. If they then press "Dropp" on that course to fix the overload, the row grays out but the lecture stays in the week and the exam stays in the list, and the total lands on a green "30 av 30 sp" that is only correct by accident.
- Fix: In `setProgramPlan` (store.ts:376-403) build the incoming programme code set first and filter it out of `manual`: `const incoming = new Set(courses.map(c => c.code)); const manual = plan.courses.filter(c => c.source === "manual" && !incoming.has(c.code));` — the programme entry wins because it is the one that carries the study plan's credits and the Dropp/Legg tilbake semantics. Add a store unit test asserting `activeCourses` never contains two entries with the same code.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [exams-1] DR-3's kont filter is a no-op: deferred ("Utsatt") exams render as ordinary exam rows, inventing dates and false "tett" clashes
- Where: `crawler/transform.mjs:133` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `toSearchIndex` filters `course.exams.filter((exam) => !exam.continuation)` (crawler/transform.mjs:133), but my own histogram over data/catalog.json returns `{total: 2438, cont: 0}` — the flag is never true. Live worker cross-check: `/api/course/HBIOT2030` returns `[{occasion:"Ordinær eksamen", date:"2026-09-24"}, {occasion:"Utsatt eksamen", date:"2026-12-01"}]` while catalog.json carries both as `{season:"AUTUMN", continuation:false}`. `/api/course/MGLU1106` returns THREE `Utsatt eksamen | Høst 2026` (2026-11-30, 2026-12-07, undated) and three `Ordinær eksamen | Vår 2027` (all undated) — catalog carries only the two utsatt dates as AUTUMN. `/api/course/ENG1102`: 2026-10-06 is Utsatt, 2027-01-08 is Ordinær. `/api/course/AAR4225`: its only 26h-window date (2026-11-19) is Utsatt. Playwright at 1280x900 on `#26h;-;+MGLU1106,+HBIOT2030,+ENG1102` rendered exactly: `to 24. sep HBIOT2030 / om 59 dager` → `12 dager mellomrom` → `ti 6. okt ENG1102` → `55 dager mellomrom` → `ma 30. nov MGLU1106` → `1 dag mellomrom · tett` → `ti 1. des HBIOT2030` → `6 dager mellomrom` → `ma 7. des MGLU1106` → `32 dager mellomrom` → `fr 8. jan ENG1102`. Four of those six rows are deferred sittings, and the red `· tett` warning connects two of them.
- Failure: A student with MGLU1106 in a Høst 2026 plan sees two exams (ma 30. nov, ma 7. des) plus a red "1 dag mellomrom · tett" clash warning against HBIOT2030's phantom 1. des. All three dates are utsatt sittings for students who failed last year. This student's real MGLU1106 exam is Vår 2027 and undated — so the planner invents exams in the wrong semester, stays silent about the real one, and manufactures a spacing warning out of exams nobody in this plan sits.
- Fix: The catalog flag cannot carry this — do not try to fix it in the crawler. `plannerApp` already fetches `/api/course/:code` per plan course and `CourseBundle.details.exams` carries structured `date` + `occasion`. Thread `state.bundle?.details?.exams` into `collectExamInputs` (src/components/planner/examList.ts:38) and, when a catalog `[season, date]` matches a scraped exam by exact ISO `date`, drop or visibly label as "utsatt" any whose `occasion` does not start with "Ordinær". This joins on a structured date and reads `occasion` as a label, so it does not violate DR-3's "never re-parse scraped free-text into a date". Until it lands, PRODUCT.md:244's "Ordinary-only by default; kont filtered out. Shipped" is false and should be corrected.
- Verifier: Tried three ways to kill it. (1) Cherry-picked courses? I drew a fresh random sample of 12 from the 1298 catalog courses with a dated AUTUMN exam inside 26h's `fromDate`…`examFinalDate` window and queried 10 through the live worker — MGLU1507's only in-window date (2026-11-20) is `Utsatt eksamen`, ~1 in 8 of dated courses, matching the auditor's rate. (2) Downstream guard? I read `examsFromIndex`/`examBelongsTo` (data.ts:240-270), `collectExamInputs` (examList.ts:38-53) and `buildExamList` — the only filters are season and date window; nothing looks at occasion and nothing can, because the search-index tuple is only `[season, date]`. (3) Deliberate deferral? PRODUCT.md §11 build-order item 1 names DR-3 as part of "the correctness floor" and §6 marks it Shipped. It survived all three.
### [groups-1] Ticking any øving/lab checkbox in the popover deletes the course's lectures from the week
- Where: `src/lib/planner/groups.ts:157` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live at 1280x900 on /planlegger/#26h;-;+TDT4120. BEFORE: one block, aria-label "TDT4120, fredag 12:15 til 15:00, uke 34 til 47". Popover renders exactly two checkboxes ("Øvingsforelesning", "Øvingsveiledning") and zero radios. Clicking the first checkbox → AFTER blocks: ["TDT4120, onsdag 10:15 til 12:00, uke 34 til 47"] — the Friday lecture is gone; hash `#26h;-;%2BTDT4120~%C3%B8vingsforelesning`; localStorage np:plans `{"code":"TDT4120",…,"groups":["øvingsforelesning"]}`; grid note flips to "Ingen aktiviteter er merket som forelesning i disse emnene — viser all undervisning" (false — the popover's own note still says "fredag 12:15–15:00 · F1"); #planner-grid-status still reads "ingen kollisjoner", now over zero lectures. Mechanism traced: toggleOther (popover.ts:137-144) seeds `base` from `ctx.defaults`, but `defaultLectureKeys` returns `[]` whenever the course has ≤1 distinct lecture group (groups.ts:124), so the written selection is `["øvingsforelesning"]` and applyGroupSelection's explicit branch (groups.ts:157-163) drops every entry whose key isn't in that flat set. Bundled groups.ts/schedule.ts with esbuild and ran the same simulation against the live API for 26h-narrowed data: TDT4120 1→0, TDT4160 2→0, TDT4136 2→0, TFY4104 2→0, TFY4115 2→0, TTK4100 1→0, MA1101 2→0, TDT4258 1→0, TMA4400 3→0 lecture entries on the first checkbox click. The toggleOther doc comment describes this exact failure and claims the ctx.defaults seeding prevents it — the seeding is empty in precisely the common single-lecture-group case.
- Failure: An MTDT first-year with TDT4120 in the plan clicks their Friday Algoritmer lecture, ticks "Øvingsforelesning" to record which øving stream they follow, and the lecture disappears from the week. The page then asserts no activity in the course is marked as a lecture, the verdict line reads "ingen kollisjoner" over a week that lost its lectures (so a genuine clash against another course goes unreported), and the broken state is written to localStorage and to the share hash.
- Fix: Make the explicit branch per-kind instead of one flat allow-list. In applyGroupSelection (groups.ts:152-178) partition `selected` into lecture keys and non-lecture keys via classifyActivity over the entries; filter lecture entries only against the lecture half and non-lecture entries only against the non-lecture half, falling through to the existing default rule for a kind with no selected key. That also removes the need for popover.ts:137-144's ctx.defaults seeding, which cannot reach this case.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways. (1) Checked whether "Øvingsforelesning" classifies as lecture and would therefore be in the radio group — activity.ts:78 NON_LECTURE_KEYWORDS `/øving/i` matches, so it is "other" and rendered as a checkbox; the live DOM dump confirms two checkboxes, no radios. (2) Checked whether the toggleOther ctx.defaults seeding saves it — traced defaultLectureKeys over live 26h data for 9 courses, `[]` in every one. (3) Checked whether a guard downstream restores the lecture — drove Chromium against 127.0.0.1:8788 and watched the block, the hash, localStorage and the grid note all change. Also checked tests/planner/groups.test.ts and e2e/flows.pw.ts: no test covers ticking a checkbox on a single-lecture-group course. It survives everything.
### [pc-3] The week prints a green "ingen kollisjoner" verdict when one course's timetable fetch failed and its lectures are absent from the grid
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:1013` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Traced and reproduced. `GridRenderResult.partial` is `partial: loading` in all three return paths (grid.ts:714, :742, :1013) — it encodes "a fetch is still in flight", never "a fetch came back null". `plannerApp.ts:1159` (`if (grid?.state !== "grid" || grid.partial) return;`) is the only suppressor. The fallback that *does* know about failures (`const failed = states.some(s => s.bundle !== null && s.bundle.timetable === null)`, plannerApp.ts:1244) only reaches the user when `showFallback` is true, and `showFallback` requires `!published || empty` (line 1255) — i.e. ALL loaded bundles empty. A mixed outcome (4 ok, 1 failed) therefore renders a normal grid with `partial:false`. Live repro (my own Playwright run, `.../scratchpad/vpc/v2.mjs`): route-fulfilled `**/api/course/TMA4400/timetable*` with 503, loaded `/planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300`, waited 9 s → `.planner-block` matching TMA4400 = 0, while `#planner-grid-status` = "ingen kollisjoner" with `class="planner-section-sub is-clean"` (the accent-green Green-Means-Fits mark). The course row ("fikk ikke hentet timeplan: 503") and the provenance line ("Fikk ikke hentet timeplan for TMA4400") do admit the gap; the verdict — PRODUCT §1's one primary job — does not.
- Failure: NTNU's timetable endpoint (or the student's flaky mobile connection) fails for one of five courses. The grid draws 7 blocks and the verdict says, in green, "ingen kollisjoner". The student registers in Studentweb and then discovers the missing course's lecture sits on top of TMA4412. The composite answered "kan jeg ta disse emnene sammen?" with a confident yes on data it never had.
- Fix: Smaller than the auditor's version: `failed` is already computed in `plannerApp.ts:1244`. Thread it into `renderVerdict(gridResult, anyLoading, failed)` (plannerApp.ts:1151/1367) and, when `failed` is true, either return silently or print "kan ikke sjekkes — mangler timeplan for N emner" with the clash styling rather than `is-clean`. No change to grid.ts is required; if you prefer keeping the knowledge in the grid, set `partial: loading || courses.some(c => c.bundle !== null && c.bundle.timetable === null)` at grid.ts:1013 (the raw `timetable === null` survives `filteredStates`' `if (!s.bundle?.timetable) return s;` guard, so the check works there too).
- Verifier: I tried to kill this three ways. (1) Looked for a guard elsewhere: `renderGridAndExams`'s `failed` branch exists but is gated behind `showFallback`, which requires every loaded bundle to be empty — a mixed success/failure never reaches it. (2) Checked whether the verdict is suppressed by the loading flag long enough to matter — no, `anyLoading` is false once the failed fetch resolves. (3) Reproduced live with a 503 intercept and read the actual class list (`is-clean`), not just the text. All three failed to refute. I raised severity from major to blocker: this is the one sentence the whole product exists to say, and it says the confidently-wrong thing that D5/DR-8 name as worse than four correct single-column tools.
### [pd-1] A failed timetable fetch still produces a confident "ingen kollisjoner" verdict — the page answers PRODUCT §1's one job wrongly
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1367` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live twice with the same plan `#26h;-;+TDT4109,+TMA4400,+TMA4412`. Healthy run: `#planner-grid-status` = "2 kollisjoner denne uka", `#planner-grid-notes` = "Mandag 08:15–10:00 · TMA4400 og TMA4412 kolliderer …". With `**/api/course/TMA4400/timetable**` fulfilled as 500 `{"error":"Upstream boom"}`: `#planner-grid-status` = "ingen kollisjoner", `#planner-grid-notes` = "", `.planner-block` count 3 (down from the healthy set), and 0 "Prøv igjen" buttons anywhere in the page. Only trace is a meta span on the row: "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Upstream boom". Code trace matches: `renderVerdict(gridResult, anyLoading)` (plannerApp.ts:1367) suppresses the assertion only on `loading` or `grid.partial`, and grid.ts sets `partial: loading` at lines 714/742/1013 — never true for a *failed* bundle. plannerApp.ts:1244 already computes `const failed = states.some((s) => s.bundle !== null && s.bundle.timetable === null)` and uses it only for the all-courses-empty fallback card, which cannot fire while any other course has entries (`empty` at :1251 requires *every* loaded bundle to be empty). renderVerdict's own docstring says "nothing is asserted while a fetch could still change it" — so this is an oversight in the U4 fix, not a decision.
- Failure: Student plans TDT4109 + TMA4400 + TMA4412 in Høst 2026; NTNU's timetable endpoint 500s (or the worker maps an NTNUAPIError to 502, or 429 "Rate limited") for TMA4400 alone. The week draws two of three courses and the verdict beside the Ukeplan kicker reads "ingen kollisjoner". The true answer is 2 collisions, both involving the missing course. Combined with pd-5 the wrong verdict is permanent for the SPA session.
- Fix: plannerApp.ts:1367 — pass the already-computed failure set into `renderVerdict` and refuse the assertion when it is non-empty: `renderVerdict(gridResult, anyLoading, states.filter(s => s.bundle !== null && s.bundle.timetable === null).map(s => s.course.code))`, and in `renderVerdict` (line 1151) early-return with `.np-note-clash` text "kunne ikke sjekke {codes}" instead of "ingen kollisjoner"/"N kollisjoner". Three lines; do not widen grid.ts's `partial`, which is also read for other purposes.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways: (a) checked whether the state-3 "Fikk ikke hentet timeplanen" + "Prøv igjen" fallback catches it — it cannot, `showFallback` needs `!published || empty` and `empty` requires every loaded bundle to have zero entries, so a mixed success/failure plan never reaches it (verified live: 0 retry buttons); (b) checked whether `grid.partial` covers failed bundles — it is literally `partial: loading` at grid.ts:714/742/1013; (c) checked whether the grid notes or provenance compensate — notes were empty and provenance never re-renders (pd-2). Nothing rescues it. Severity kept at blocker: the primary product question is answered with a false negative and there is no other correcting signal on the surface.
### [plan-1] classifyPeriod never descends into nested waypoints, so picking a direction deletes the semester's courses — CMED/medisin resolves to zero, BSPL/sykepleie loses half
- Where: `src/components/planner/programPlan.ts:406` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: The docstring at programPlan.ts:376-380 asserts "Nested waypoints *inside* a chosen direction are not descended into. No programme in the crawled data has them". I killed that claim against the live API: `GET /api/program/CMED/plan?year=2026` period 1 has `courseGroups: []` at top level, one waypoint "Medisinstudiet - Veivalg - By", and each of the four city directions (CMED26-V-T/-G/-L/-Å) has `courseGroups: []` plus a nested waypoint "…Veivalg - Klasse" whose two Gruppe directions BOTH carry EXPH0400 (O, 7,5 sp) + MD4012 (O). `collectGroups(chosen, …)` at line 407 adds nothing and the function returns `pendingChoice: null`. My own independent scan of 11 programmes x 2 years found nested waypoints carrying courses under a direction with zero own courses in: CMED (48 pairs), MGLU1-7 (42), MLSAM (40), BIELEKTRO (12, period 3), BHIST (4, periods 3-5), MSENVIENG (4), BMUSP (4) — MTDT and BIT have none, which is exactly why the docstring's sample missed it. Browser check at /planlegger/#26h;CMED.2026.CMED26-V-T; : credit line "0 av 30 sp", zero course rows, question panel hidden, banner "kull 2026 · Medisinstudiet - Trondheim · Høst 2026". Without the direction (#26h;CMED.2026;) the student at least gets the city question — so answering it makes the week strictly worse. BSPL kull 2025 Trondheim (#26h;BSPL.2025.BSPL25-V-TRONDHEIM;) renders "15 av 30 sp" from SYT2100 alone; the nested "valg av praksisløp, Trondheim" holds SYT2101HJE/MED/KIR/PSY, each 15 sp and studyChoice "O", and none of them reaches obligatory, choice or the question. tests/planner/programPlan.test.ts has no nested-waypoint case at all.
- Failure: CMED kull 2026, Trondheim, Høst 2026: the planner shows "0 av 30 sp", no rows, no question and no grid — EXPH0400 and MD4012 are in the fetched document one level deeper. BIELEKTRO kull 2026 period 3 and MGLU1-7 period 5 behave the same. BSPL kull 2025 Trondheim, Høst 2026: exactly one course and "15 av 30 sp"; the 15 sp obligatory practice course that is the other half of the semester is never shown or offered.
- Fix: Make the waypoint pass in `classifyPeriod` recursive: after folding a chosen direction in via `collectGroups` (line 407), re-run `gatingWaypoint`/`obligatoryIntersection`/`collectPool` on that direction. Where the nested directions agree (CMED's two Klasse groups) the existing intersection rule prefills them; where they differ (BSPL's four praksisløp) the courses land in `choice` and the nested waypoint becomes `pendingChoice` — the shape the top level already produces. Fix the docstring at line 376 too; it is why this was never checked.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways and failed. (1) Checked whether the intersection rule rescues the unanswered case — it does for the question, but the finding is about the answered case, which I reproduced in Chromium: picking the direction yields zero courses and hides the question. (2) Checked whether some consumer re-descends — loadPeriodCourses (plannerApp.ts:1548) and commit (studieinfo.ts:612) both call resolvePeriodFor and take resolved.courses.obligatory verbatim; nothing else looks at waypoints. (3) Checked whether this is a documented decision — PRODUCT.md/ROADMAP have nothing on it, and the only statement anywhere is the docstring's factual claim, which is false. Independent programme scan reproduced the pattern in 7 programmes I picked myself.
### [ux-fail-1] B7 regression: the auto-revealed muted layer draws zero blocks, and the page stamps "ingen kollisjoner" on the blank week
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:764` · correctness · UNVERIFIED · regression of REVIEW.md B7
- Evidence: Live, 1280×900, `/planlegger/#26h;-;+BI1006` (BI1006 "Dyrenes struktur og funksjon", 11 real entries in 26h teaching weeks, every title classified "other": "Forelesning og øving", "Laboratorieøvelse 1–6"). Screenshot N/H-B7-BI1006.png shows a fully ruled 08:00–20:00 week with NOT ONE block, headed by the green verdict "ingen kollisjoner" and the margin note "Ingen aktiviteter er merket som forelesning i disse emnene — viser all undervisning." plus "BI1006 har 7 grupper — velg din". Playwright block count before picking a group: 0. I then clicked the note, checked one of the 7 group checkboxes in the popover, and the count went to 18 blocks (grid text: "BI1006 EL6 · 14:15 Forelesning og øving uke 34, 36, 38, …"). The data was there the whole time. Cause is exact: grid.ts:756 sets `mutedLayerAutoRevealed = !showOthers && lectureEntries.length === 0`, then grid.ts:764-767 does `const shown = revealOthers ? rawEntries.filter(e => e.isLecture || e.groupPicked) : lectureEntries` — and collectEntries (grid.ts:235) sets `groupPicked = showAllGroups || key === null || soleGroup || picked.has(key)`, which is false for every entry of a course with ≥2 unpicked groups. B7's auto-reveal survives; the group filter added on 2026-07-25 empties it. I found 3 more such courses in a 1-in-137 sample of the 5 470-course index (HMSG1001, KUH1011, SYT3500).
- Failure: A biology student adds BI1006 for Høst 2026 → the week is completely empty, labelled "ingen kollisjoner", under a sentence promising it is "viser all undervisning". They conclude the course has no scheduled teaching and plan another course on top of a slot that is in fact occupied 7 weeks of the term.
- Fix: In grid.ts:764-767, when `mutedLayerAutoRevealed` is true the pick filter must not apply — use `rawEntries` (or `e.isLecture || e.groupPicked || mutedLayerAutoRevealed`) for `shown`, keeping the "har N grupper — velg din" note as the narrowing invitation. Add a regression test: a course whose entries are all "other" with ≥2 distinct group keys must yield `blockCount > 0`.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [week-1] Ticking an øving group in the block popover deletes that course's lectures from the week and persists it
- Where: `src/components/planner/popover.ts:137` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced end-to-end on the live server. /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026; default week = 7 blocks including two `TMA4412 / R1 · 08:15 / Forelesning / uke 34–47` blocks (Mon + Wed). Pressed "Vis øvinger og labber" (grid notes then render `TMA4412 har 3 grupper — velg din`, `TMA4400 har 5 grupper — velg din`, `TDT4109 har 3 grupper — velg din`), opened the TMA4412 block popover (its Grupper section shows one non-pickable lecture "Forelesning" plus 3 checkboxes), checked "Mattelab 1". Resulting DOM block list: both TMA4412 Forelesning blocks are GONE, replaced by `TMA4412 / S7 · 12:15 / Mattelab 1` and `TMA4412 / S7 · 14:15 / Mattelab 1`. hash becomes `#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400,TMA4412~mattelab-1,EXPH0300` and localStorage np:plans records `"groups":["mattelab-1"]`. Traced: TMA4412 has one distinct lecture key, so groups.ts:124 `defaultLectureKeys` returns `[]`; popover.ts:138-139 `base = selection.length>0 ? selection : ctx.defaults` = `[]`; setSelection writes `['mattelab-1']`; applyGroupSelection's explicit branch (groups.ts:157-162) keeps only entries whose key is in the set, and the lecture entries' key is `forelesning`, so they are filtered out. The docstring at popover.ts:127-136 anticipates exactly this failure and believes ctx.defaults prevents it — it does not for the single-lecture-group case, which is the common case.
- Failure: MTDT kull 2026 student loads /planlegger/, presses the one §0.4 toggle, follows the grid's own note "TMA4412 har 3 grupper — velg din", ticks "Mattelab 1". Both weekly Matematikk 2C lectures (Mon + Wed 08:15, R1, uke 34–47) disappear from the week and from the conflict engine, and the broken plan is written to the URL hash and localStorage so it survives reload and sharing. Same for TDT4109 and any other course with a single lecture group.
- Fix: popover.ts:137-143 — when `ctx.defaults` is empty, seed `base` from every lecture-kind option instead: `ctx.groups.filter(g=>g.kind==='lecture').map(g=>g.key)` (the `lectureKeysOf` helper already exists at popover.ts:100). Belt-and-braces in groups.ts:157-162: make the explicit-selection branch kind-aware so a selection naming only øving/lab keys never filters lecture entries.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by checking whether `ctx.defaults` is ever non-empty for the affected courses (it is not — distinctLectureKeys length 1 ⇒ groups.ts:124 returns []), whether the popover hides the checkbox for such courses (it does not — pickableGroups counts kinds separately, so 3 øving checkboxes render with no lecture radio), and whether any unit/e2e test guards it (grep over tests/planner/groups.test.ts, store.test.ts, e2e/*.pw.ts finds nothing covering an øving-only selection). Then reproduced the whole flow in Chromium against the live server and read back the hash and localStorage. Survives.
### [week-2] A course's two different weekly lecture slots are treated as mutually exclusive parallels, so real lectures are silently deleted from the week
- Where: `src/lib/planner/groups.ts:85` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of UX-STUDY S5
- Evidence: Live /api/course/IT2805/timetable?year=2026 returns `{title:'Forelesning 2', dayNumber:1, 10:15–12:00, R7, studyProgramKeys:[MLREAL,BIT,MTDESIG]}` and `{title:'Forelesning 1', dayNumber:2, 10:15–12:00, A1, same programmes}` — two weekly slots, same programme set, i.e. not parallels. `looksLikeNumberedParallels` (groups.ts:85-87, `/\d+\s*$/`) matches both labels, so groups.ts:130-135 picks the alphabetically first. Live: /planlegger/#26h;BIT.2026; renders exactly one IT2805 block, `IT2805 / A1 · 10:15 / Forelesning 1 / uke 34–47`; /emne/IT2805/ on the same site renders BOTH `R7 · 10:15 Forelesning 2` and `A1 · 10:15 Forelesning 1`. Worse for TMR4106, whose labels end in clock times so every one of them matches the digit heuristic: /planlegger/#26h;MTIØT.2026;TMR4106 renders ONE block, `TMR4106 / T2 - Havrommet · 08:15 / Forelesning introuke tirsdag kl. 08:15- 11:00 / uke 34`, while /emne/TMR4106/ renders five, including `Forelesning morgen tirsdager kl. 08:15-10:00 · uke 35–48`. So the planner shows this course for uke 34 only and an empty week for uke 35–48.
- Failure: BIT kull 2026 student opens /planlegger/ and sees IT2805 only on Tuesday; the Monday 10:15 Webteknologi lecture in R7 is absent from the week and invisible to the conflict engine, so a clash against it is never reported and the verdict still reads "ingen kollisjoner". MTIØT student adding TMR4106 sees one intro-week block and nothing for the rest of the semester. The site's own /emne/ pages show the missing sessions, so the product contradicts itself.
- Fix: groups.ts:120-136 — add a disjointness guard before narrowing: never drop a lecture group whose entries occupy a different day/start-time (or a disjoint week set) from the kept group; true parallels are simultaneous alternatives. Tightening `looksLikeNumberedParallels` to `/parallell\s*\d+\s*$/i` fixes IT2805/TMR4106 too and keeps TDT4110/TMA4245 narrowing, but the disjointness guard is the semantically correct one and should land regardless.
- Verifier: Attacked it by pulling the raw upstream timetable through the live worker to check the auditor had not misread the programme tagging (both IT2805 lectures really do carry the same studyProgramKeys, so entriesForProgram cannot separate them), and by rendering the same two courses on /emne/ pages to confirm the data exists and only the planner drops it. Also checked whether a grid note warns the student — `#planner-grid-notes` is empty in both default views, so there is no affordance at all. Survives.
### [worker-1] CODE_RE rejects `/` and `+`, so 4 programmes the planner itself offers 400 on every plan request — MSØK/5 has a real 9-period plan upstream and the UI tells the user NTNU has none
- Where: `worker/src/routes.ts:25` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of B1
- Evidence: Reproduced end to end. (1) `CODE_RE = /^[A-ZÆØÅ0-9_-]{2,16}$/i` at routes.ts:25 admits neither `/` nor `+`. Running that exact regex over `data/programs.json` (403 programmes) fails exactly four: EMNE/HF, EMNE/SU, MSECT+OH, MSØK/5. (2) `src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:17-25` maps *every* programme into the inlined `programOptions` with no filter — `curl -s /planlegger/ | grep -o 'MSØK/5'` hits. (3) Live: `curl 'http://127.0.0.1:8788/api/program/MS%C3%98K%2F5/plan?year=2026'` → `400 {"error":"Invalid program code"}`. (4) Upstream really has the plan: `new NTNUClient().programs.studyPlan('MSØK/5', 2026)` through the same ntnu-api the worker uses returned `Samfunnsøkonomi - master (5-årig) periods=9`. (5) The intent is documented in the caller: `src/components/planner/programPlan.ts:137-140` comments "the handful of codes carrying a literal '/' (\"MSØK/5\") would otherwise become a 400 ... (B1)" — the front-end author believed the worker's parseCode handled this; it does not. `tests/planner/store.test.ts:654` also uses `MSØK/5` as a first-class programme code.
- Failure: A Samfunnsøkonomi 5-årig (or MSECT+OH) student opens /planlegger/, types the programme name, picks it from the typeahead. `fetchPlan` gets 400 → `!res.ok` → `"error"` → `{kind:"error"}` → `studieinfo.ts:361-368` sets `programMissing = true` and the hint "Fant ingen studieplan for dette programmet. Du kan fortsatt legge til emnene dine selv." No kull chips, no obligatory prefill. The flagship promise (programme + kull → week) is 100% unavailable for that programme, and the site asserts a falsehood about upstream — the exact inverse of the "honest join" differentiator.
- Fix: worker/src/routes.ts:25 → `const CODE_RE = /^[A-ZÆØÅ0-9_+/-]{2,16}$/i`. Both downstream uses are escape-safe (`courses.details` wraps the code in `encodeURIComponent`; `programs.studyPlan` goes through `URLSearchParams`), and `.` is still excluded so no traversal shape can form. Then repoint `tests/worker/routes.test.ts:381-386` ("still rejects a decoded code that is not a code", currently asserting `TDT%2F4100` → 400) at something genuinely invalid such as `TDT%204100`, add a `MS%C3%98K%2F5` → 200 case, and update `docs/SPEC.md`'s documented regex (line ~325) to match.
- Verifier: I attacked this three ways and failed to kill it. (a) Tried to find a front-end filter that would keep these four codes out of the picker — `planlegger/index.astro` maps `programsData.programs` with no predicate, and I grepped the served HTML and found `MSØK/5` present. (b) Tried to show upstream has no plan anyway (which would make the 400 harmless) — a real ntnu-api call returned a 9-period plan. (c) Tried to call it a documented decision: `docs/SPEC.md` does document the regex verbatim, but SPEC is authority level 5 and the caller's own comment plus `tests/planner/store.test.ts` show the intended behaviour is the opposite, so the spec line is part of the bug, not a waiver. I did downgrade my confidence in the auditor's Playwright detail only in that the exact hint text comes from studieinfo.ts:364, not plannerApp — verified in source. Severity kept at blocker: the core flow is totally dead for identifiable real users and the UI states something false about NTNU's data.
## MAJOR (60)
### [a11y-3] "Dropp" from the block popover restores focus to a node that no longer exists, dumping focus to <body> outside any dialog — next Tab restarts at the skip link
- Where: `src/components/planner/popover.ts:353` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live (my own script, BIT/2025/26h): focus `#planner-block-0` ("IT1901, mandag 14:15 til 16:00…") → Enter opens the popover and focus correctly moves to `.planner-popover-close` → Tab → `.planner-popover-action | Dropp` → Enter. After: `document.activeElement` = BODY, `document.getElementById("planner-block-0")` = null, and the next three Tabs are `.skip-link`, `.site-brand`, `.np-navlink|Planlegger`. Trace: the action handler (popover.ts:232-243) calls `store.dropCourse()` then `close()`; the store change re-renders the grid and destroys the anchor; the `close` listener then runs `invoker?.focus?.()` on the detached node, a silent no-op. The popover is deliberately non-modal (`dialog.show()`, line 330), so unlike the two modal cases there is no focus scoping to catch the fall.
- Failure: Keyboard student opens a colliding block, drops the course to resolve the clash — the exact remedial action the product exists to enable — and is silently teleported to the top of the document with no focus ring and no announcement. Getting back to the week costs a Tab past skip link, wordmark, two nav links, the studieinfo chip and the theme toggle, every single drop.
- Fix: In popover.ts's `close` listener (352-356) guard the restore: `if (invoker?.isConnected) invoker.focus(); else fallbackFocus();` where `fallbackFocus` targets the surviving semantic neighbour — the course row's own Dropp/Legg tilbake toggle for that code, or the grid frame given `tabindex="-1"`.
- Verifier: Attacked by checking whether the grid re-render might reuse the same DOM node (it does not — getElementById returns null post-drop), whether the dialog is modal so focus would be scoped (it is not, `dialog.show()` at line 330 with a comment explaining the choice), and whether any e2e test guards focus restoration (grepped e2e/*.pw.ts for `activeElement`/`toBeFocused` — zero hits, nothing covers this). Reproduced end-to-end. Severity kept at major: unlike a11y-1/a11y-2 there is no containment, so the cost is the whole chrome tab run on every drop.
### [a11y-4] Collision verdict never renders in collision red — the scoped .planner-section-sub rule outranks .np-note-clash, so "1 kollisjon denne uka" prints in --muted in both themes
- Where: `src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:269` · design-system · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live, both themes, with a real BIT/2025/26h clash (IT1901 × TDT4160 Monday 14:15). `#planner-grid-status` carries class `planner-section-sub np-note-clash` and text "1 kollisjon denne uka", but computed color is rgb(87,86,83) in light (--clash is #af3029) and rgb(159,157,150) in dark (--clash is #d14d41) — i.e. --muted in both. Built CSS confirms the cascade: dist/_astro/index.lDteG2S4.css has `.planner-section-sub[data-astro-cid-slvc6ckq]{…color:var(--muted)}` at specificity (0,2,0); dist/_astro/Layout.6o6mCF-a.css has `.np-note-clash{…color:var(--clash)}` at (0,1,0). The clean state works only because `:global(.planner-section-sub.is-clean)` (index.astro:282) is also (0,2,0) and later — measured srgb(0.627,0.674,0.413) = --accent-ink on the same element. plannerApp.ts:1165 (grid verdict) and :1185 (exam verdict) both hit this.
- Failure: Student on /planlegger/ with a real Monday 14:15 collision. The sentence answering PRODUCT §1's primary question renders in the page's quietest gray, identical to the decorative "UKEPLAN" kicker beside it, while the no-collision state gets a confident green — the emphasis is inverted exactly where it matters. index.astro:279-284's own comment asserts "--clash when it does not [hold together]", which does not happen. Same for "N eksamener samme dag".
- Fix: Add beside the existing is-clean rule in planlegger/index.astro's style block: `:global(.planner-section-sub.np-note-clash) { color: var(--clash); }` — same (0,2,0), later in file, exactly how is-clean already wins. Cleaner long-term: drop `color: var(--muted)` from `.planner-section-sub` (line 272) and let the primitive carry the ink.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways: (a) checked whether the element even receives the scoped attribute — it does (`data-astro-cid-slvc6ckq` present in the live attribute list); (b) checked whether some later unscoped rule re-asserts --clash — grepped the built CSS, none exists; (c) checked whether this is a deliberate decision — index.astro:279-284's comment and DESIGN.md's Red-Is-Collision rule both mandate the opposite. Measured live in light AND dark. Survives.
### [app-1] syncHash() writes history.replaceState(null, …), wiping Astro ClientRouter's entry state — Back (and Forward) onto /planlegger/ changes the URL but never swaps the page
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:443` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Code: plannerApp.ts:441-444 `function syncHash(){ lastWrittenHash = formatPlanHash(plan); history.replaceState(null, "", lastWrittenHash); }`, called on first paint (1675) and in every onPlanChange (1614); the ?studieinfo strip at 1683-1688 passes null too. node_modules/astro/dist/transitions/router.js:389 `if (ev.state === null) { return; }` in onPopState, and :39 seeds `{index,scrollX,scrollY}`. Live at 127.0.0.1:8788, my own runs: (1440x950) /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135 -> history.state null; click topbar Emner -> /emner/, state {"index":1,...}; goBack -> url http://127.0.0.1:8788/planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135 but title "Emner — Semesterplan", #planner-grid-frame count 0, body text "…EMNER Emnekatalog Gjøvik Levanger Trondhei…". Worse than the auditor reported: at 390x844, / -> /planlegger/ -> /emner/ -> Back gives emner-under-planner-URL, a second Back correctly reaches / (state index 0), and then **Forward** to the planner entry also no-ops (url /planlegger/#26h;-; while the homepage stays rendered, title "Semesterplan — planlegg semesteret ved NTNU"). Reload recovers (#planner-grid-frame 1). COUNTERFACTUAL I ran to try to blame Astro instead: with an addInitScript wrapping `history.replaceState` so a null state falls back to `history.state`, the very same click path gives planner state {"index":1,...} and Back -> url /planlegger/#26h;-;, title "Planlegger — Semesterplan", #planner-grid-frame 1. So the three-line fix is verified to work and the cause is unambiguously our replaceState.
- Failure: Student on /planlegger/ taps "Emner" in the topbar, then presses the browser/Android Back button. The address bar returns to /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024;… while the emnekatalog stays on screen — URL and content disagree, and the same entry is also dead going Forward. Only a fresh nav click or a reload recovers the week. Fires for every visitor, including one with no plan (the empty-plan syncHash write is enough).
- Fix: plannerApp.ts:443 -> `history.replaceState(history.state, "", lastWrittenHash);` and the identical change at plannerApp.ts:1686 (the ?studieinfo strip). Add an e2e case (e2e/navigation.pw.ts) doing planner -> /emner/ -> goBack -> assert #planner-grid-frame is present; nothing in e2e/ currently calls goBack (grep: zero hits).
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways and failed: (a) reproduced independently on desktop and 390x844 mobile viewports; (b) control from / (an untouched entry) Backs correctly, so it is not the environment or Astro generally; (c) ran a counterfactual init-script that preserves state — Back then works perfectly on the identical path, which isolates the cause to syncHash. Severity downgraded from blocker to major: the planning flow itself still works and a reload/click recovers, so no mandate flow is permanently broken and no data is wrong — but it hits every visitor on the primary surface and breaks both Back and Forward onto that entry.
### [app-2] Picking a lecture parallel or øving group teleports the block popover to (8,8): the synchronous renderAll inside the store event detaches the anchor before position() re-measures it
- Where: `src/components/planner/popover.ts:115` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Path: popover.ts:110-119 setSelection() calls store.setCourseGroups first, the store dispatches PLAN_CHANGE_EVENT synchronously, plannerApp.ts:1606-1615 runs renderAll() in the same tick, renderGridAndExams -> grid.ts:943 frame.replaceChildren(...) detaches every block; control then returns to popover.ts:115 `if (invoker) position(invoker)` with a detached invoker, whose getBoundingClientRect is all zeros, so position() (popover.ts:300-322) clamps to ANCHOR_MARGIN. Reproduced live at 1440x950 with #26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4110: anchor block box {x:642.6,y:782.4,w:161.2,h:168}; popover on open top:427px/left:642.594px (correctly pinned to the block's column); after clicking one group input top:8px/left:8px, rect x=8,y=8. Mechanism nailed by stashing the anchor node before the pick: after the pick `window.__anchor.isConnected === false` and its rect is {x:0,y:0,w:0,h:0}. Mobile (390x844) is unaffected — position() early-returns below 60rem and the bottom sheet stayed at rect 0,497 before and after.
- Failure: Desktop student clicks their TDT4110 block, the card opens beside it, they tick "Øving" to record their group — the card instantly jumps to the top-left corner over the page heading and the studieretning question, and stays there for every further radio/checkbox. They must re-find both the card and the block. First click of the primary group-picking interaction (PRODUCT §0.3 "editing is trivial").
- Fix: Smallest correct change: guard popover.ts:300 with `if (!anchor.isConnected) return;` so the card keeps its last good coordinates instead of snapping to (8,8). Better: in setSelection (popover.ts:115) re-resolve the anchor out of #planner-grid-frame by its stable block id (grid.ts:653 `planner-block-${entry.ordinal}`) before calling position().
- Verifier: Reproduced from scratch with my own Playwright script (found the first block that yields group inputs, measured dialog.style before/after) and additionally proved the mechanism rather than inferring it, by holding a reference to the anchor across the pick and reading isConnected=false / zero rect. Tried to kill it as mobile-only or as a CSS artifact: it is desktop-only (>=60rem) and mobile is genuinely fine, but desktop is the reported viewport in the finding and the jump is real. Severity kept at major (desktop-only, but first-click on the primary edit interaction); file/line moved from plannerApp.ts:1615 to popover.ts:115 where the fix belongs.
### [astro-1] /emne/[code]/ and /emner/ hard-code semestersData.current while the plan store follows np:lastSemester — the clash verdict names and checks the wrong semester
- Where: `src/pages/emne/[code].astro:434` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Traced: `src/pages/emne/[code].astro:434` `const semester = semestersData.current;` feeds `term` (435), `mountCourseTimetable({semester})` (444), `createPlanStore(semester.id)` (450) and `clashSemester` (451-455). `src/lib/planner/store.ts:284-289` `loadPlan()` reads `np:lastSemester` first and only falls back to the constructor arg when that key is absent/empty, so the course list being checked is the *selected* semester's while the teaching weeks and all copy are the build-time default's. `src/pages/emner/index.astro:226-233` is the identical hard-code (plus `term` at 227, used verbatim in the stale row's tooltip at 398). Reachability proven end-to-end, no seeded storage: Playwright clicked `#studieinfo-chip` on /planlegger/, read the semester select (`['Høst 2026','Vår 2027 — timeplan publiseres ~desember','Høst 2027 — timeplan publiseres ~august']`), selected `27v`, pressed Lagre with no programme chosen — `localStorage.np:lastSemester` became `"27v"` (studieinfo.ts:577-581 commits the semester unconditionally). Navigating to /emne/TDT4100/ then rendered clash slot = "Ingen andre emner i planen din for Høst 2026." A second run with a valid `np:profile` + a 27v plan reproduced the full contradiction (screenshot /tmp/.../scratchpad/emne27v.png): chip = "MTDT · 2024 · Vår 2027", clash slot two lines below = "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026 — ingen kollisjon å sjekke.", timetable note = "Viser Vår 2026 — ikke undervist i Høst 2026." above a grid full of TDT4100 spring lecture blocks. Zero console errors. /planlegger/ itself does this correctly (`plannerApp.ts:431` resolves the semester from `plan.semesterId`), so the two static pages are the outliers.
- Failure: Student opens the studieinfo chip from any page, picks "Vår 2027", saves (a semester-only edit is enough — it commits unconditionally). On /emne/TDT4100/ — a course that IS taught in spring — the page says "Undervises ikke i Høst 2026 — ingen kollisjon å sjekke." directly under a chip reading "Vår 2027" and directly above a grid of the course's lectures. No collision was ever computed against the 27v plan; the verdict ran against Høst 2026's teaching weeks 34-47. On /emner/, adding a course writes into `np:plans["27v"]` while the add button's tooltip promises a verdict "for Høst 2026".
- Fix: In `src/pages/emne/[code].astro:434` and `src/pages/emner/index.astro:226-233`, resolve the planned semester before building anything from it: `const planned = createPlanStore(semestersData.current.id).loadPlan().semesterId;` then `const semester = semestersData.semesters.find(s => s.id === planned) ?? semestersData.current;` and derive `term`/`clashSemester`/`mountCourseTimetable({semester})` from that. Re-derive inside the existing `onPlanChange` handler too (emne/[code].astro:500-506, emner/index.astro:493) since `setSemester` can fire from another tab.
- Verifier: I attacked reachability first — hoping the semester picker was unreachable without a programme, or that `loadPlan` respected the constructor default. Both attacks failed: the select is one click from the chip on every page and `setSemester` commits before any programme validation (studieinfo.ts:572-581 with its own comment saying so), and store.ts:284-286 provably prefers `np:lastSemester`. I also checked whether the planner has the same bug (it does not — plannerApp.ts:431), which rules out "deliberate single-semester scope". Reproduced twice in a real browser, once through the actual UI flow with no storage seeding. Severity kept at major: it is the honesty differentiator producing a confident, wrong-semester verdict.
### [conf-2] "Problembasert læring" is hand-labeled a lecture, but the live data shows it is a parallel-group activity — producing the false red DR-1 exists to forbid
- Where: `src/lib/planner/activity.ts:68` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: LECTURE_KEYWORDS line 68 is `/problembasert\s+l(?:æ|ae)ring/i` commented "PBL plenary, medicine/health faculties", and tests/planner/activity.test.ts:33 asserts ["Problembasert læring","DISAM","Dialog- og samarbeidsbasert undervisning"] → lecture with the comment "the health faculties' PBL plenary". Live data refutes "plenary": BI1001's 2026_VÅR timetable publishes 29 rows titled exactly "Problembasert læring" across FIVE distinct slots — 10x Mon 14:15–16:00, 4x Mon 10:15–12:00, 5x Tue 10:15–12:00, 2x Wed 12:15–14:00, 8x Thu 12:15–15:00 (weeks 5-13,16-17 / 3-13,15-16 / 5-13,15-17). No student attends five weekly plenaries of the same activity; the repeated-identical-row multiplicity is exactly the signature conflicts.ts:37-39's own comment calls "parallel groups (øvinger/labs) of a course repeat the same slot many times". MDT4030 spells the same activity "PBL-gruppe 1…16" with name "Gruppe", where `\bgruppe` correctly sends it to "other" — one pedagogical activity, opposite verdicts on spelling alone. I reproduced the damage with the SHIPPED engine (esbuild bundle of activity.ts/schedule.ts/conflicts.ts) over live 26v data, teachingWeeks [2..18]: `groupConflicts(findConflicts(lecturesOnly(BI1001) + lecturesOnly(TKT4116)))` = 2 groups — "day1 855-960 BI1001+TKT4116 uke 5-17" and "day4 735-840 BI1001+TKT4116 uke 5-17". Excluding only the PBL rows: 0 groups. BI1001's lecture-classified titles are ['1-1Forelesning','Problembasert læring','1-2Forelesning'], so dropping PBL leaves the course two honest lecture blocks, not an empty week.
- Failure: A BBI student with BI1001 in their spring plan adds TKT4116 (Forelesning Mon 14:15–16:00 and Thu 12:15–14:00). plannerApp's verdict line prints "2 kollisjoner denne uka" and two red margin notes appear ("mandag 14:15–16:00 · BI1001 og TKT4116 kolliderer · uke 5–17"). The true count is 0 — the student attends one PBL group, not all five slots. They drop a course they could have taken. The same false red renders on /emne/TKT4116/ as "Kolliderer med BI1001, mandag 14:15".
- Fix: activity.ts: delete `/problembasert\s+l(?:æ|ae)ring/i` from LECTURE_KEYWORDS (line 68) and add `/problembasert|\bpbl\b/i` to NON_LECTURE_KEYWORDS, so the three observed spellings agree with MDT4030's "PBL-gruppe N" and land in the muted toggle layer where DR-1's asymmetry says unknown group work belongs. Move the ["Problembasert læring", …] row in tests/planner/activity.test.ts from `lectures` to `other` and correct the "PBL plenary" comment — the fixture is where the mislabel originates.
- Verifier: I attacked the premise, not the arithmetic. First I checked whether any doc adjudicates PBL as a lecture: `grep -rn 'Problembasert|PBL' docs/*.md` returns nothing, so this is a code comment and a hand label, not a PRODUCT decision — it is not protected. Then I checked whether downstream machinery already neutralises it: `mergeParallelSlots` collapses the 10 identical Mon 14:15 rows to one representative but leaves all five distinct slots standing, and groups.ts only offers a picker for entries the classifier calls "other", so a lecture-classified PBL can never be narrowed to the student's own group. Then I verified the false red end-to-end with the shipped modules against live 2026_VÅR data and confirmed 2 → 0 groups. The only surviving defence would be that PBL really is plenary at some faculty; five mutually exclusive weekly slots in one course kills that. Severity major (not blocker): it is confined to courses that spell PBL this way, and the flagship programme-plan flow is unaffected.
### [copy-4] DR-8 provenance line is never re-rendered after `loadBundles()`, so it freezes at "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå" and stays silent about the fetch failure it exists to admit
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1487` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `renderProvenance()` (1383-1429) composes from live state: the "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå" branch (1394) fires while `states.some(s => s.loading)`, the honest branch "Timeplan hentet direkte fra NTNU nå" (1396) once bundles land, and the failure clause `Fikk ikke hentet …` (1411-1421) from `state.bundle.errors`. `grep -n renderProvenance` gives call sites 1460 (renderAll), 1506 (renderPlanDependents), 1668 (loadPlannerIndex callback) — but `loadBundles()`'s completion block (1483-1486) re-renders only `renderCreditLine / renderCourseRows / renderGapLine / renderGridAndExams`. Live on /planlegger/#26h;-;+TDT4100,+TMA4100,+TDT4110,+EXPH0300,+IT2805 the line read, byte-identically at t=4 s, t=5 s and t=11 s with the week fully drawn and the TMA4100 row already showing its failure: `"Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026). Uoffisiell."` Adding one course via the dialog (which re-runs renderAll) flipped the very same page to `"Timeplan hentet direkte fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026). Fikk ikke hentet detaljer for TMA4100. Uoffisiell."` — proving the composer is right and only the re-render is missing.
- Failure: Any first visit to /planlegger/ with a stored or shared plan: the page's single provenance sentence claims data is still being fetched right now, permanently, and omits the per-course failure ("Fikk ikke hentet detaljer for TMA4100.") that a course row two columns away already displays — until the student happens to edit the plan.
- Fix: Add `renderProvenance();` to `loadBundles()`'s completion block (plannerApp.ts:1483-1486), alongside the other four re-renders.
- Verifier: Attacked by (a) enumerating every `renderProvenance` call site and checking whether any fires after bundles resolve on first load — 1460 is renderAll (plan mutation only), 1506 is renderPlanDependents, 1668 is the `loadPlannerIndex().then()` callback which resolves from the static index fetch and races ahead of the course bundles; (b) waiting 11 s live to rule out a slow-but-eventual re-render — the string was unchanged; (c) forcing an edit to confirm the composer itself is correct, which it is. Corrected the auditor's anchor: line 1394 is the branch, the defect is the missing call in the `loadBundles()` completion block at 1483-1487. Severity kept at major: the sentence is factually false on every unedited load and suppresses a real failure disclosure on the surface PRODUCT.md calls the moat.
### [copy-5] `.np-btn { display: inline-flex }` defeats the `hidden` attribute — the add dialog paints "Legg til" + "Fjern" simultaneously, and the suppressed "Velg fra studieplanen" button is still live
- Where: `src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:121` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `setAddState` (addCourse.ts:118-123) drives visibility purely through `button.hidden`. `.np-btn` declares `display: inline-flex` at class specificity (0,1,0) (src/styles/primitives.css:64-66), which beats the UA `[hidden] { display: none }`; the only counter-rule in the repo is scoped to one component — `.studieinfo-dialog [hidden] { display: none }` (src/styles/site.css:172-174), whose comment spells out this exact specificity reasoning. The add dialog is `.add-course-dialog`, not `.studieinfo-dialog`, so it is not covered. Live Playwright on 127.0.0.1:8788, /planlegger/#26h;-;+TDT4100, add dialog: query "TDT4100" (in plan) -> row innerText `"TDT4100\nObjektorientert programmering\n\nLegg til\nLagt til ✓\nFjern"`, children `{Legg til, hidden:true, display:flex, 69x36}`, `{Lagt til ✓, hidden:false}`, `{Fjern, hidden:false}`. Query "TDT4120" (NOT in plan) -> `"Legg til\nFjern"`, children `{Legg til, hidden:false, w:69}`, `{Lagt til ✓, hidden:true, display:none, w:0}`, `{Fjern, hidden:true, display:flex, w:58}` — the plain span hides correctly (no CSS display), both `.np-btn`s do not. Same defect at plannerApp.ts:645 `elements.gapButton.hidden = pool.length === 0`: measured `{text:"Velg fra studieplanen", hidden:true, display:flex, 160x36}` on a plan with no programme, and `document.getElementById("planner-gap-btn").click()` opened `#planner-add-dialog` — precisely the "two identical buttons a line apart" that the comment at plannerApp.ts:640-644 says was fixed by hiding it.
- Failure: (a) Student opens "Legg til emne", searches TDT4120 (not in plan): the row offers both "Legg til" and "Fjern" for a course they never added; pressing "Fjern" is a no-op labelled as a destructive action. (b) Searches TDT4100 (already in plan): the row states three contradictory things at once — "Legg til", "Lagt til ✓", "Fjern" — and "Legg til" is a no-op. (c) Student with no programme sees "Mangler 7,5 sp" next to a live "Velg fra studieplanen" button with no study plan behind it; clicking it opens the same generic catalog dialog the "Legg til emne" button one line below opens, and it keeps its bare markup label because the `(N)` suffix is only written when `pool.length > 0`.
- Fix: Make the attribute authoritative for the primitives rather than per-component: add `.np-btn[hidden], .np-toggle[hidden] { display: none; }` to src/styles/primitives.css next to the `.np-btn` block (or a global `[hidden] { display: none !important; }`). The `.studieinfo-dialog [hidden]` rule at site.css:172 can then be folded into it.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways. (1) Looked for a global `[hidden]` rule — `grep -rn '\[hidden\]' src/styles src/pages src/components src/layouts` returns only the studieinfo-scoped one plus `:not([hidden])` display rules; nothing covers `.add-course-*` or `.planner-gap-btn`. (2) Checked whether the not-taught early return (addCourse.ts:196-199) or a disabled state makes the row unreachable — no, that return only applies to not-taught rows; ordinary rows always build all three controls. (3) Checked whether this restates an already-fixed REVIEW/UX item — the site.css comment shows the class of bug was found and fixed ONLY inside the studieinfo dialog; addCourse.ts and the gap button are 2026-07-25-era surfaces that never got the fix, so these are live unfixed instances. Reproduced in two independent Playwright runs. Severity kept at major: a real user is offered no-op and mislabelled actions in the add flow, and a deliberately-suppressed duplicate door is fully clickable.
### [course-1] 703 course pages render an empty Nøkkeltall (and no exam enrichment, no "Mer om emnet") because the details fetch drops the source year the page already computed
- Where: `src/components/site/courseDetails.ts:193` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live with Playwright at 1280x900. /emne/TMA4100/: `#details-section [data-role=status]` = "Vi har ingen emnedetaljer for dette emnet.", `.details-fact` count = 0, console error "Failed to load resource: 404". curl confirms the split for six sampled last-year-only courses — `bare=404 year2025=200` for TMA4100, AAR4334, AAR4500, KJ2095, KLH3103, KLH3201. worker/src/routes.ts:102-129 (`handleCourseDetails`) already parses `yearParam`, validates it and passes it to `deps.client.courses.details(parsedCode, year)`, and keys its cache on `["details", code, year]`. [code].astro:29 computes `sourceYear = offeredYears[0]` and threads it into the timetable island (line 443 `year: sourceYear`) but calls `mountCourseDetails(code)` bare at line 437; courseDetails.ts:193 fetches `/api/course/${code}` with no year. data/catalog.json: 703 of 5470 courses have offeredYears excluding 2026. The same fetch also feeds `renderExamDetails` (courseDetails.ts:250) and the "Mer om emnet" disclosure, so on those pages the Eksamensdetaljer disclosure never un-hides and the prose section never appears either.
- Failure: A student opens /emne/TMA4100/ (NTNU's largest course — the page's own grade figure shows 1368 candidates in Høst 2024). The Nøkkeltall block reads "Vi har ingen emnedetaljer for dette emnet." — no studiepoeng, no vurderingsform, no karakterregel, no "Mer om emnet", and the Eksamensdetaljer disclosure stays hidden. All of it is available one query parameter away: `/api/course/TMA4100?year=2025` returns credits 7,5, level, assessmentScheme and full prose. Same on all 703 last-year-only pages — exactly the population the two-catalog-year union (CLAUDE.md) exists to serve.
- Fix: Thread the year through: `mountCourseDetails(code, sourceYear)` at src/pages/emne/[code].astro:437, and in courseDetails.ts:193 append `?year=${year}` only when `year !== catalogYear`, so offered courses keep sharing the bare cache key. The worker route already validates and honours `?year=`.
- Verifier: I tried to kill this by checking whether the worker actually accepts `?year=` (it does — routes.ts:107-113), whether the 404 was a TMA4100 quirk (it is not — 6/6 sampled courses from data/catalog.json's 703-course off-year set show the same bare=404 / year2025=200 split), and whether some other code path back-fills facts from data/catalog.json (it does not; #details-section has exactly one data source). Nothing rescues it. Kept at major: the page's decision facts are entirely absent on 703 pages.
### [course-3] The course-page week unions two semesters into one grid, and the layout engine packs cross-term sessions into an overlap cluster as if they were simultaneous
- Where: `src/components/site/courseTimetable.ts:149` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `curl '/api/course/EXPH0300/timetable?year=2026&version=1'` → 84 entries, `{"2026_VÅR":51,"2026_HØST":33}`. Live /emne/EXPH0300/ at 1280x900, measured block rects against the day-header rects (man x=104-328, tir x=328-552, ons x=552-776): tirsdag 08:15-10:00 draws TWO side-by-side blocks in one 2-column cluster — x=329 "Forelesningsparallell 3 Ålesund · Fogdegården · uke 3-13, 16" (spring, ended in May) and x=441 "Forelesningsparallell 1 Trondheim · R1 · uke 34-45" (autumn). Seven lecture blocks are drawn in total; only three carry autumn weeks. courseTimetable.ts:146-151 builds the one-course state from the raw `entries` array and hands it to `renderGrid` (line 159) with no term/week filter, while the clash path two files away clamps with `entriesInSemester(entries, semester.teachingWeeks)` (planClash.ts:74). src/lib/planner/layout.ts is time-only and week-blind, so a cluster deeper than MAX_COLUMNS=2 collapses whole into ONE pile block. REVIEW.md:369 and PRODUCT.md §3.4 both specify "the timetable as a ruled grid **for the planned semester**".
- Failure: A student checking EXPH0300 (ex.phil. — taken by essentially every NTNU student) for Høst 2026 sees seven lecture parallels of which only three run in Høst 2026, and a Tuesday 08:15 where a Trondheim autumn lecture is drawn beside an Ålesund spring lecture as a simultaneous pair. The only discriminator on the block is the week range ("uke 34-45" vs "uke 3-13, 16"), which is dropped entirely on compact blocks. A third cross-term entry in the same slot would collapse all three into one pile block presenting non-coexisting sessions as one simultaneous group.
- Fix: In courseTimetable.ts, before building `state` at line 146, filter `entries` to the planned semester's teaching weeks with `entriesInSemester` from lib/planner/schedule.ts; fall back to the newest available term only when that leaves nothing, and have `termNote` (line 78) name the single term actually on screen instead of listing both.
- Verifier: I attacked this three ways. (1) Is the mixing disclosed? Partly — the term line honestly reads "Viser Høst 2026, Vår 2026." and termNote's doc comment anticipates plural terms, so the union is a knowing choice; but PRODUCT §3.4 / REVIEW.md:369 both specify the grid is for the *planned* semester, so the union is still a conformance gap. (2) Does the conflict engine already guard it? conflicts.ts:59-62 IS week-aware and correctly refuses to flag cross-term overlaps as collisions — so no false red — but layout.ts is week-blind and still packs them into one cluster, which is the visual claim. (3) I could not reproduce the auditor's Monday example: both Monday 10:15 blocks carry uke 34-45 (both autumn). The Tuesday 08:15 example is exact and I measured it. Corrected the evidence accordingly; the finding survives at major.
### [cpc-3] courseDetails fetches /api/course/:code without ?year=, so Nøkkeltall is empty on all 703 off-year course pages
- Where: `src/components/site/courseDetails.ts:193` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: courseDetails.ts:193 is `fetch(`/api/course/${encodeURIComponent(code)}`)` with no year, and mountCourseDetails takes only `code`. [code].astro:29 computes `sourceYear` from `offeredYears[0]`, :431 reads it into the script, :439-445 threads `year: sourceYear` into mountCourseTimetable — but :437 calls `mountCourseDetails(code)` with the code alone. Curl: `/api/course/TMA4100` → 404 {"error":"Not found"}; `/api/course/TMA4100?year=2025` → full payload `{"courseCode":"TMA4100","courseName":"Matematikk 1","credits":7.5,"level":"Grunnleggende emner, nivå I",…}`. Live in Chromium the whole #details-section reads exactly "NØKKELTALL\n\nVi har ingen emnedetaljer for dette emnet." `node -e` over data/catalog.json: 703 of 5470 courses have offeredYears excluding 2026 (AAR4334, AAR4500, AAR4510, AAR4515, AAR4525, … TMA4100).
- Failure: A student following a 2025 syllabus link opens /emne/TMA4100/. The page honestly says "ikke undervist i 2026 · sist undervist 2025" and shows the 2025 exam date, then reports no studiepoeng, no nivå, no undervisningsspråk, no vurderingsform, no karakterregel, no ansvarlig enhet, no "Mer om emnet" prose, no studiepoengreduksjon and no Eksamensdetaljer — every one of which the worker returns for `?year=2025`. CLAUDE.md's two-catalog-year rule exists precisely so these pages render the honest state rather than a hollow one.
- Fix: Give `mountCourseDetails` a `year` parameter, call it as `mountCourseDetails(code, sourceYear)` from src/pages/emne/[code].astro:437, and append `?year=${year}` at courseDetails.ts:193. worker/src/routes.ts's handleCourseDetails already accepts `year` and folds it into the cache key.
- Verifier: Attacked it as possibly already handled: checked whether the worker falls back to a previous catalog year (it does not — bare /api/course/TMA4100 404s), whether the page hides Nøkkeltall for off-year courses (it does not — it renders the 404 copy), and whether the empty state is a deliberate honesty decision (CLAUDE.md's two-catalog-year note says the opposite: render the honest state, not an empty page). Reproduced live and counted the affected courses from the real catalog.
### [cpc-4] With øvinger on, the course-page week degenerates into 17 unopenable "1 emner" piles, and every block on the page is an inert button
- Where: `src/components/site/courseTimetable.ts:159` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: courseTimetable.ts:159 calls `renderGrid(frame, notes, [state], showOthers, { showAllGroups: true })` and passes no `onBlockClick`. Live on /emne/EXPH0300/ after clicking "Vis øvinger og labber": 24 `.planner-block`, of which 17 are `.planner-block-pile`. Their entire inner text is "1 emner · 08:15 | EXPH0300" (…10:15, …12:15, …15:15) with aria-label "1 aktiviteter samtidig: EXPH0300, mandag 08:15–10:00". grid.ts:607 hardcodes `${courses.length} emner` and :621 `${courses.length} aktiviteter samtidig` off the DISTINCT-COURSE map built at :598-602, which on a one-course page is always 1 — both strings are ungrammatical bokmål and both undercount (Monday 08:15 piles several seminargrupper). Screenshot read back: the piled slabs carry no group number, no room and no week range, while the non-piled blocks beside them show "Seminargruppe 22 Trondheim · uke 4–5, 7–10, 13, 16–17". grid.ts:575 documents "Clicking still opens the popover with every entry" — false here: clicking the first pile left `document.querySelectorAll('*').length` at 602 before and after, 0 popovers, because :624 is `if (onBlockClick)`. buildBlock (grid.ts:526) and buildPileBlock (:583) both emit `<button type="button">`, so all 24 blocks are focusable, cursor:pointer, hover-highlighted controls with no handler.
- Failure: A student on /emne/EXPH0300/ (mandatory for nearly every technology programme) turns the øving layer on to find out when the seminargrupper run. 17 of 24 blocks collapse into slabs reading "1 emner · 10:15 / EXPH0300" — group number, room and week range destroyed — and clicking or tabbing into one does nothing, so the information is unrecoverable on that surface. Before HEAD the same cluster showed a "+N til" chip on top of two readable columns.
- Fix: Two independent changes. (a) grid.ts:607/621: when `courses.length === 1`, label the pile from the entry count with correct grammar ("3 aktiviteter · 08:15") and list distinct activity names (`groupLabel`) instead of repeating the one course code. (b) courseTimetable.ts:159: pass an `onBlockClick` that opens the read-only popover, or make renderGrid emit non-interactive elements when no handler is supplied so 24 dead buttons leave the tab order.
- Verifier: Tried to file it under the brief's pre-excused polish ("the read-only grid renders an inert group-picker affordance") — it is not that; that note is gone and this is a different defect. Tried to blame the deliberate `showAllGroups: true` Task 7 ruling — showing every group is the deliberate part, but "1 emner", "1 aktiviteter samtidig" and a pile that swallows its own content while advertising a click it never handles are not. Measured the click as a DOM-length delta rather than trusting the absence of a visible popover, and read the full-page screenshot back to confirm the piles really are information-free.
### [cpc-6] Kont/resit cohorts are drawn as peer semesters in the grade figure and, being newest, lead it with a 60 % fail rate
- Where: `src/lib/planner/grades.ts:100` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `buildGradeSemesters` buckets purely on `${row.year}|${season}` (grades.ts:108) with no notion of which season the course is taught in, and `mountGradeChart(code, signal)` ([code].astro:438) receives only the code, so the teaching season the page already computed for `termNote` never reaches the model. Live /emne/TDT4102/ — whose own timetable line reads "Viser Vår 2026 — ikke undervist i Høst 2026." — renders as its first, top-left, newest chart: "Høst 2025 · 70 kandidater" with bars B 0,0 C 11,4 D 5,7 E 22,9 F 60,0, immediately left of "Vår 2025 · 646 kandidater · F 17,8". Same shape on /emne/TDT4100/. Three of TDT4102's six charts (Høst 2023/2024/2025, n=125/79/70) are autumn sittings of a course the site itself says is not taught in autumn. PRODUCT.md:383 (DR-3) already sets the principle for exams: catalog `ExamDate` with "kont filtering"; the grade figure has no equivalent.
- Failure: A student choosing between TDT4102 and an alternative opens /emne/TDT4102/. The first and visually leading chart, labelled as the most recent semester, says 60,0 % F. That is the resit population — 70 candidates who had already failed — not the course's 646-candidate spring cohort where F is 17,8 %. The figure hands them a fail rate 3,4× the real one as its headline number, on a page whose whole premise is the honest join.
- Fix: Pass the course's teaching season(s) into `mountGradeChart` from [code].astro:438 (the page already derives them for `termNote`) and in grades.ts either drop off-season buckets or label them "Høst 2025 · konte" so they are not read as an ordinary semester. Minimum viable: never let an off-season bucket be the first chart.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as an honest label — the caption does show "70 kandidater" next to "646 kandidater", but nothing on the page tells a student that an autumn sitting of a spring course is a resit, and no doc concedes this. Tried to find a filter elsewhere: grades.ts has none, the worker relays DBH table 308 unchanged, and mountGradeChart's signature makes the season physically unavailable. Reproduced the exact leading chart text in Chromium ("Høst 2025 | 70 kandidater | 0,0 | B | 11,4 | C | 5,7 | D | 22,9 | E | 60,0 | F") and re-derived the same buckets from a raw curl of /api/course/TDT4102/grades.
### [crawler-1] Four crawled programme codes cannot pass the worker's own code grammar — MSØK/5's flagship flow dies with copy blaming NTNU
- Where: `worker/src/routes.ts:25` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Verified independently, end to end. (1) Data: a node pass over `data/programs.json` gives 403 programmes, of which exactly 4 fail the worker's `CODE_RE = /^[A-ZÆØÅ0-9_-]{2,16}$/i` — `EMNE/HF`, `EMNE/SU`, `MSECT+OH`, `MSØK/5` (all `hasAdmission: true`). (2) API: `curl 'http://127.0.0.1:8788/api/program/MS%C3%98K%2F5/plan?year=2026'` → `400 {"error":"Invalid program code"}`; `/api/program/MTDT/plan?year=2026` → 200. (3) Reachability: `src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:17-25` maps every programme in `programs.json` into `programOptions`, no filter. Playwright against the live server, typing "MSØK/5" into `#studieinfo-program-input`, yields exactly one option ("MSØK/5 | Samfunnsøkonomi | master 5 år, trondheim"); clicking it fires exactly one request — `400 /api/program/MS%C3%98K%2F5/plan?year=2026` — and the modal then renders no KULL section at all plus "Fant ingen studieplan for dette programmet. Du kan fortsatt legge til emnene dine selv." (`programPlan.ts:143` maps any non-404 !ok to "error", so the 404 step-back ladder never runs — hence the single request). (4) The data really exists: one polite upstream call with our own UA, `client.programs.studyPlan("MSØK/5", 2026)` → `{name: "Samfunnsøkonomi - master (5-årig)", periods: 9}`. (5) `src/components/planner/programPlan.ts:137-139` asserts the opposite in a comment: the encodeURIComponent round-trip it credits does happen, but CODE_RE rejects the decoded value anyway, so the comment documents a fix that does not work.
