- Failure: A student on Samfunnsøkonomi master (5-årig) opens /planlegger/, types their programme, gets exactly one match (MSØK/5), clicks it — and the modal shows no kull chips and "Fant ingen studieplan for dette programmet", i.e. the site tells them NTNU has no plan for a programme NTNU publishes a 9-period plan for. PRODUCT §0's core promise (programme + kull → your week) is 100% unreachable for MSØK/5, MSECT+OH, EMNE/HF and EMNE/SU.
- Fix: Give programme codes their own grammar rather than widening the shared course regex: add `const PROGRAM_CODE_RE = /^[A-ZÆØÅ0-9_+\/-]{2,16}$/i` in worker/src/routes.ts:25 and use it in the programme-plan handler's parseCode call (the decode already runs first and the cache key is built from the decoded value, so no key collision). Course codes keep the tighter regex. Add an assertion (in tests/crawler.test.mjs or the artifact test from crawler-5) that every code in `data/programs.json` matches the worker's programme regex, and correct the now-false claim in programPlan.ts:137-139.
- Verifier: Attacked three ways. (a) Maybe the picker filters these codes out — it does not; planlegger/index.astro maps all 403 with no predicate, and Playwright showed MSØK/5 as a real, clickable option. (b) Maybe they are junk pseudo-programmes with no upstream plan — true by name for EMNE/HF and EMNE/SU, but I made a live `programs.studyPlan("MSØK/5", 2026)` call and got a real 9-period plan, so at least MSØK/5 (and plausibly MSECT+OH) is a genuine loss. (c) Maybe the encoded path breaks worker routing rather than CODE_RE — no: the 400 body is `Invalid program code`, i.e. it reached parseCode with the segment intact, so widening the regex is sufficient. I corrected the auditor's fix: widening the shared CODE_RE would also start accepting `/` and `+` in course codes, so the regex should be split.
### [crawler-3] S13's raw "fikk ikke hentet detaljer: Not found" is still reachable — /emner/ and /emne/[code]/ both let you add a not-taught course the planner cannot resolve
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:954` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of S13
- Evidence: Reproduced live with Playwright. On /emner/, searching TMA4100 gives the row `TMA4100 | Matematikk 1 | eksamen høst | Trondheim | ikke undervist i 2026 | Legg til` with the add button `disabled: false`; clicking flips it to "Lagt til". Navigating to /planlegger/ then renders, verbatim: "Ingen av emnene dine undervises i Høst 2026.", "0 av 30 sp (+1 emne uten oppgitt sp)", and the course row `TMA4100 / Matematikk 1 / Fjern / lagt til selv / fikk ikke hentet detaljer: Not found`. Network during that render: `200 /api/course/TMA4100/timetable?year=2026&version=1` (body `[]`) and `404 /api/course/TMA4100`. The leaked English string is produced by `plannerApp.ts:954` — `meta.append(el("span", undefined, `fikk ikke hentet ${error}`))` — which interpolates raw upstream error text into Norwegian copy. `src/pages/emner/index.astro:360` already computes `const stale = !offeredYears.includes(index.year)` and uses it at :361-366 and :397, but the add button at :371-394 is built unconditionally. `src/pages/emne/[code].astro` has the same hole: `curl /emne/TMA4100/` returns a live `#emne-plan-btn` "Legg til i planen" even though the page header says "ikke undervist i 2026 · sist undervist 2025". Only `addCourse.ts:193-199` guards. UX-STUDY.md:179-181 (S13) describes exactly this planner row and asks to reuse the friendly copy; ROADMAP.md:54 lists S13 as "folded in".
- Failure: A student searches TMA4100 — the canonical course the two-catalog-year union exists to keep addressable — on /emner/ (or opens /emne/TMA4100/), reads "ikke undervist i 2026", presses the still-live add button, and lands on a planner claiming their whole plan has no teaching, "0 av 30 sp", and a raw English API error string in the course row.
- Fix: Fix it at the leak, not only at one entry point: in src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts around :950-955, when the course's `offeredYears` (already in the runtime index) excludes the selected semester's year, render the friendly stale copy ("ikke undervist i {year} · sist undervist {lastYear}", matching /emne/[code].astro:59-65) instead of iterating `bundle.errors`; never interpolate a raw upstream error string into user copy. Then make the three add affordances agree — src/pages/emner/index.astro:371-394 and src/pages/emne/[code].astro's `#emne-plan-btn` should adopt the same policy as addCourse.ts:193-199.
- Verifier: I tried to kill this as a restatement of an already-fixed S13 and failed: ROADMAP.md:54 explicitly claims "S13 (the raw 'Not found' row copy)" was folded in, but the fix landed only in addCourse.ts's dialog guard, and I reproduced the exact leaked string in a real browser via the /emner/ path in under a minute. I also looked for any other guard (store.addCourse, plannerApp filtering) — none exists. I widened the finding: /emne/[code].astro has the identical unguarded add button (curl-verified on /emne/TMA4100/), so the auditor's proposed one-file fix would have left the hole open, and I re-anchored file/line to the actual leak site.
### [ds-2] Red-Is-Collision is defeated on the flagship verdict: an Astro-scoped selector outranks .np-note-clash, so "1 kollisjon denne uka" renders muted gray
- Where: `src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:272` · design-system · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live. Seeded a 26h plan (IT1901 + TDT4160 + TDT4120, MTDT/2025 profile), loaded http://127.0.0.1:8788/planlegger/ headless: `#planner-grid-status` = {cls:"planner-section-sub np-note-clash", text:"1 kollisjon denne uka", color:"rgb(87, 86, 83)"} — that is `--muted` #575653, not `--clash` #AF3029. `#planner-exam-status` = {cls:"planner-section-sub np-note-clash", text:"2 eksamener samme dag", color:"rgb(87, 86, 83)"} — same defect. Cause verified in the shipped CSS: `dist/_astro/index.lDteG2S4.css` emits `.planner-section-sub[data-astro-cid-slvc6ckq]{font-family:var(--font-sans);font-size:var(--text-sm);color:var(--muted)}` (specificity 0,2,0) while `dist/_astro/Layout.6o6mCF-a.css` emits `.np-note-clash{...;color:var(--clash)}` (0,1,0). Source: src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:269-273 sets `color: var(--muted)` on the scoped class; plannerApp.ts:1165 and :1185 add `np-note-clash` to those two hosts. The positive verdict escapes only by accident — it was written `:global(.planner-section-sub.is-clean)` (index.astro:283), which ties at 0,2,0 and wins on source order.
- Failure: Student plans IT1901 + TDT4160 (both Monday 14:15). The week shows red-edged blocks and a red margin note, but the verdict beside the Ukeplan kicker reads "1 kollisjon denne uka" in exactly the same gray as "henter timeplan …". Same for "2 eksamener samme dag" beside Eksamener. The one headline statement of the tool's verdict is styled as neutral status text, and only the negative verdict is — "ingen kollisjoner" IS green, so the two states are indistinguishable-by-severity in the same slot.
- Fix: src/pages/planlegger/index.astro:269-273 — drop `color: var(--muted)` from the scoped `.planner-section-sub` rule and give it at zero specificity instead: `:where(.planner-section-sub){color:var(--muted)}`, so any `.np-*` state class composed onto the element wins. Then delete the `:global()` workaround on `.is-clean` (it exists only to beat the same rule). Add an e2e assertion on colour, not just text — e2e/flows.pw.ts:140 asserts `toContainText(/\d+ kollisjon/)` and nothing about `--clash`, which is why this shipped.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways and failed. (1) Checked whether the class is added at all — plannerApp.ts:1164-1167 does `host.className = "planner-section-sub"` then `classList.add("np-note-clash")`, so both are present, confirmed in the live DOM dump. (2) Checked whether some later rule re-inks it — grepped every `.np-note-clash` definition in dist; only primitives' 0,1,0 rule sets colour. (3) Checked whether a test already guards it — e2e/flows.pw.ts:140 asserts text only. Reproduced the muted colour live on the real running build for both the grid verdict and the exam verdict. Severity kept at major (not blocker): the collision is still legible on the grid itself via red block edges and the red margin note, so no student is left with zero red signal.
### [ds-3] The course page's plan button hard-deletes programme courses under the label "Fjern fra planen", and the planner's prefill silently resurrects them on the next visit
- Where: `src/pages/emne/[code].astro:491` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced end to end live. Seeded only `np:profile` = MTDT/2025 with an empty 26h plan, loaded /planlegger/ → prefill wrote `np:plans` = IT1901, TDT4120, TDT4160, TMA4240 all `source:"program"`. Opened /emne/IT1901/: button = {text:"Fjern fra planen", aria-pressed:"true", backgroundColor:"rgb(102, 128, 11)"} (= `--accent` #66800B, the "I planen" fill). Clicked it → `np:plans` became TDT4120/TDT4160/TMA4240, i.e. the entry was **deleted**, not marked dropped. Navigated back to /planlegger/ → `np:plans` was back to all four including IT1901, undropped. Code path: [code].astro:461 `planBtn.textContent = inPlan ? "Fjern fra planen" : "Legg til i planen"`, :491 `store.removeCourse(code)` with no `source` check; store.ts:318-323 `removeCourse` filters the entry out, while store.ts:334-346 `dropCourse` sets `dropped: true`; store.ts:376-401 `setProgramPlan` rebuilds the programme list from the study plan and only carries over entries that still exist with `dropped: true`, so a hard-deleted programme course returns undropped. `hasCourse` (store.ts:325-327) also ignores `dropped`, so a course dropped in the planner shows the accent "Fjern fra planen" state on the course page. Violates DESIGN §7 on both counts: the in-plan label should be "I planen", and a programme course's reversible removal is "Dropp" → "Legg tilbake".
- Failure: Student's plan is prefilled from MTDT. They open /emne/IT1901/, see a green button reading "Fjern fra planen", click it to drop the course. Button flips to "Legg til i planen" and the entry is deleted from storage. They go back to the planner and IT1901 is there again — the removal did not stick, the destructive verb both over-promised (it said irreversible) and under-delivered (it was undone), and the same course now offers two different removal verbs on two pages.
- Fix: src/pages/emne/[code].astro — read the plan entry, not just membership: in `syncPlanBtn` (line 458-462) look up `store.loadPlan().courses.find(c => c.code === code)` and branch on `entry.source`. For `source === "program"` use the reversible pair ("Dropp" / "Legg tilbake") backed by `store.dropCourse`/`store.restoreCourse` in the click handler at line 489-495, and drive `aria-pressed`/accent off `!entry.dropped`. For a manual add keep `removeCourse` but relabel the in-plan state "I planen" per §7 with removal as a separate quiet control.
- Verifier: Attacked the resurrection claim specifically, since that is what makes it more than a copy nit. Checked whether the prefill is gated so it cannot re-run: plannerApp.ts:1579-1587 guards on `programDerivedFor !== derivationKey()`, but `programDerivedFor` is closure state created on each planner mount, so a fresh page load always re-derives — and `sameProgramSet(plan.courses, toAdd)` is false once a code is missing, so `setProgramPlan` fires. Confirmed by observation, not just reading: deleted IT1901 from the course page and it was back in `np:plans` after one navigation to /planlegger/. Also checked whether this is the known-deferred `removeProgram` prune item in the brief — it is not, that one is about `removeProgram` and other semesters. Severity kept at major: a user-initiated edit is silently reverted.
### [edit-2] Every `.np-btn` the planner hides with the `hidden` property stays visible: the add dialog shows "Legg til" + "Lagt til ✓" + "Fjern" at once and the gap rail shows two identical buttons
- Where: `src/styles/site.css:172` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: p12.mjs measured the live DOM (MTDT kull 2026, one course dropped so the gap line shows). `#planner-gap-btn: hiddenAttr=true display=flex box=160x36 text="Velg fra studieplanen"`. Add dialog, course already in the plan (TDT4109): `.add-course-add: hiddenAttr=true display=flex box=69x36`, `.add-course-added: hiddenAttr=false display=block`, `.add-course-remove: hiddenAttr=false display=flex` — all three controls on screen (screenshot AJ-add-dialog-programme-course.png: "Legg til | Lagt til ✓ | Fjern"). Add dialog, course NOT in the plan (TDT4100): `.add-course-remove: hiddenAttr=true display=flex box=58x36` — a live "Fjern" button for a course the student does not have (screenshot E-add-dialog.png). Cause: `.np-btn { display: inline-flex }` (src/styles/primitives.css:65-66) is an author declaration that beats the UA `[hidden] { display: none }`. This exact hazard is documented and patched for one dialog only, at src/styles/site.css:167-173 (`.studieinfo-dialog [hidden] { display: none }`); the add-course dialog and the planner page have no equivalent, so `setAddState` (addCourse.ts:115-124) and `renderGapLine` (plannerApp.ts:645) are both inert. The gap-line comment at plannerApp.ts:640-646 explicitly says the button is hidden with an empty pool "so the rail carried two identical buttons a line apart" — that regression is live: screenshot AN-gap-button-visible.png and M5-mobile-after-drop.png both show "Velg fra studieplanen" directly above "Legg til emne", both opening `addCourseDialog.open()`.
- Failure: A student opens "Legg til emne", types TDT4110, and sees "Legg til" next to "Fjern" — there is no way to tell from the row whether the course is in the plan. After adding it the row shows "Legg til", "Lagt til ✓" and "Fjern" simultaneously; clicking "Legg til" again does nothing (store.addCourse returns early), so the primary editing surface has a dead control and no readable state. Separately, an MTDT student who drops one course sees "Mangler 7,5 sp · Velg fra studieplanen" over "Legg til emne" — two buttons a line apart that open the same dialog, even though MTDT period 1 has no study-plan choice pool at all.
- Fix: Add one origin-beating rule in src/styles/base.css: `[hidden] { display: none !important; }` (or, if the `!important` is unwanted, mirror the existing patch: `.add-course-dialog [hidden], .planner-gap-line [hidden] { display: none; }` next to site.css:172) and drop the now-redundant `.studieinfo-dialog [hidden]` rule. Add an e2e assertion that `#planner-gap-btn` is not visible for a programme whose period has no choice pool.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [edit-3] The add dialog's "Fjern" deletes a programme course outright instead of dropping it — and the deletion silently undoes itself on the next page load
- Where: `src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:217` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: p10.mjs, MTDT kull 2026 in 26h. Opened "Legg til emne", searched TDT4109 (a `source:"program"` course), clicked the row's "Fjern". Dump AK-after-add-dialog-fjern: hash `#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300`, credit "22,5 av 30 sp", TDT4109 gone from the rows, from the grid blocks and from the exam list — no "droppet — fortsatt en del av programmet" row anywhere. Then `page.reload()` + 9 s: dump AL-after-reload shows hash `#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300` and "30 av 30 sp" — TDT4109 is back (screenshots AK/AL). Cause: the handler is `deps.store.removeCourse(code)` (addCourse.ts:217-220) regardless of source, and `removeCourse` (store.ts:318-323) filters purely by code; because the deletion leaves no `dropped` marker, `setProgramPlan`'s `previousDrops` map (store.ts:378) has nothing to preserve, and the next mount's `loadPeriodCourses` sees `sameProgramSet === false` and re-adds the course undropped (plannerApp.ts:1580-1587). PRODUCT §0.3 / D3 reserve the irreversible verb "Fjern" for manual adds; the Emner rail correctly renders "Dropp" for the same course (plannerApp.ts:925).
- Failure: A student who removes a programme course from the add dialog (the natural place to look, since it is the surface that lists everything) sees it vanish from the week, the credit line and the exam list, copies the link or just closes the tab — and on the next visit the course is back in the plan, back in the week, and the total is back at 30 av 30 sp, with no message explaining why. Two surfaces disagree about what removing a programme course means.
- Fix: In addCourse.ts, look the plan course up before acting: `const inPlan = deps.store.loadPlan().courses.find(c => c.code === code);` then call `deps.store.dropCourse(code)` when `inPlan?.source === "program"` and `removeCourse` otherwise, and label the button "Dropp"/"Legg tilbake" accordingly — the same three-state logic `renderCourseRows` already uses (plannerApp.ts:923-935). `sync()` must read the drop state too, not just `hasCourse`.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [edit-4] Programme-cluster lecture parallels are unresolved: MTIØT's week draws TMA4400 nine times with no note, and the only fix on offer deletes a lecture the student attends
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:775` · correctness · UNVERIFIED · regression of UX-STUDY.md S5 ("parallel groups make real schedules unreadable" — fixed for digit-suffixed parallels like TDT4110's "Forelesningsparallell 1/2/3", still live for cluster-named ones like TMA4400's)
- Evidence: p7.mjs, MTIØT kull 2026 / Høst 2026 (two courses, 15 av 30 sp): `document.querySelectorAll('.planner-block')` filtered on TMA4400 returns **9** blocks — five of them overlapping at 10:15 on Mon/Tue/Wed/Thu (screenshot W-after-paste.png), two on Thu squeezed to ~55 px wide — while `#planner-grid-status` reads "ingen kollisjoner" and `#planner-grid-notes` is the empty string. The popover shows `input:checked` count **0**: `defaultLectureKeys` (groups.ts:120-136) bailed because `entriesForProgram(entries,"MTIØT")` leaves 6 lecture keys and `looksLikeNumberedParallels` (groups.ts:85-87) is false — the labels end in programme codes ("Forelesning 1 MTBYGG, MTING, MTIØT, …"), not digits. No margin note points at the picker either: `unpickedGroups` skips every lecture entry (grid.ts:775 `if (entry.isLecture || entry.groupPicked) continue;`) and is only populated at all when `revealOthers` is true (grid.ts:773). Worse, using the picker breaks the week the other way: selecting the radio "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" collapses TMA4400 to **1** block (dump Y-after-lecture-pick, screenshot Y-after-lecture-pick.png) — the same cluster's second weekly lecture "Forelesning 2 MTIØT, MTKOM, MTDT" (Thu 10:15) and "Plenumsregning" (Wed 14:15) both disappear, because `pickLecture` (popover.ts:121-124) treats every lecture-kind key as mutually exclusive while the group key is derived from the whole title (groups.ts:41-49), which conflates "which stream" with "which of the week's two lectures".
- Failure: An MTIØT kull 2026 student picks their programme and sees Matematikk 1 nine times in one week, five of them stacked at 10:15 on four different days, with the verdict line asserting "ingen kollisjoner" and nothing on the page saying the week is showing alternative streams or how to narrow it. If they discover that blocks are clickable and pick their own cluster, the week shrinks to a single Monday lecture — their Thursday lecture and Wednesday plenumsregning are silently deleted, and the tool still says "ingen kollisjoner, 15 av 30 sp". Either way the week they are told is theirs is not the week they attend.
- Fix: Two changes in the same pass. (a) grid.ts: populate `unpickedGroups` for lecture groups too — drop the `entry.isLecture` skip at line 775 and the `revealOthers` gate at 773 when a course has more than one distinct lecture key with no explicit pick, so the existing "TMA4400 har 12 grupper — velg din" note (grid.ts:975-1002) opens the picker. (b) popover.ts: render lecture options as checkboxes rather than one exclusive radio (i.e. route them through `toggleOther`), or key lecture options on the programme-code list inside the title so "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" and "Forelesning 2 MTIØT, MTKOM, MTDT" collapse into one selectable cluster.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [grid-1] A piled cluster erases every session time and the pile popover adds nothing back — on the prefilled BERGO kull 2026 week, four of five days are identical featureless slabs
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:598` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live at http://127.0.0.1:8788/planlegger/#26h;BERGO.2026; (1280x900, default lecture-only mode). Four piles render, innerText identical on all four: "2 emner · 08:15 | ETT1101 | ETT1102"; aria-labels "2 aktiviteter samtidig: ETT1101 · ETT1102, tirsdag|onsdag|torsdag 08:15–12:00" and "…fredag 08:15–10:00". The Tuesday cluster really contains ~9 distinct lecture slots (curl /api/course/ETT1101/timetable?year=2026 → dayNumber 2 Forelesning at 08:15–10:00, 09:15–10:00, 10:15–11:00, 10:15–11:15, 11:15–12:00; ETT1102 → 08:15–11:00, 08:15–10:00, 08:15–12:00, 10:15–12:00). grid.ts:600-604 builds `byCode` — one row per DISTINCT COURSE — and :607 prints only `minutesToTime(start)`, the cluster's earliest start. Clicking the pile opened #planner-popover reading exactly "ETT1101 · ETT1102 · Person, aktivitet og deltakelse i hverdagslivet · Aktivitetsanalyse / tirsdag 08:15–12:00 · 02-034, 04-066, 04-025, 03-059 · uke 33–42, 44–46" — one merged span, zero per-session times. I additionally confirmed the pile popover is a dead end: popover.ts:286 `if (ctx.kind !== "course") return;` runs before renderActions, so a pile gets no "Gå til emnesiden →" link (popover.ts:247-249) and no group picker.
- Failure: A BERGO kull 2026 student opens /planlegger/, gets the prefilled week, and tirsdag/onsdag/torsdag/fredag each show one grey slab captioned "2 emner · 08:15". Clicking it yields a popover with a single merged 08:15–12:00 span. She cannot learn from the planner that ETT1101 lectures 08:15–10:00, 10:15–11:00 and 11:15–12:00 on Tuesday — the primary surface withholds the schedule it exists to deliver, on the majority of her week.
- Fix: In grid.ts:600-616 key the pile list on the SESSION, not the course: one `.planner-pile-row` per entry carrying dot + code + `HH:MM–HH:MM` (list scrolls when it overflows). Independently — and cheaper — make `pileDetail` (grid.ts:629-646) hand the popover the per-entry rows so the popover always lists every session's time and room even when the block itself has no room for them.
- Verifier: I reproduced the pile and the popover live, and traced the byCode collapse in code. I attacked the auditor's strongest sentence and it is WRONG: "Nowhere in the product … can she learn that ETT1101 lectures 08:15–10:00 and 10:15–11:00 and 11:15–12:00 on Tuesday" — /emne/ETT1101/ renders day 2 as SIX separate column blocks (08:15 ·2 grupper, 09:15, 10:15, 10:15, 11:15, 13:15), i.e. exactly those times, because ETT1102 is not present there to push the cluster past MAX_COLUMNS. So a recovery path exists for that specific example. What survives: /emne/ETT1101/ still piles onsdag and torsdag ("1 emner · 08:15"), so those days' times are unrecoverable anywhere in the product, and the pile's own popover offers no link to the course page at all. I downgraded blocker→major on that basis plus scope (the auditor's own scan found piles in ~10 of 57 programmes, and the week/exams/rest of the flow still render).
### [grid-3] The mobile week is illegible: a 56 px day column shatters "ETT1101" over three lines and clips the guaranteed second line; a 2-column cluster gives 27 px blocks with one character per line
- Where: `src/styles/planner-week.css:66` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Measured live at 390x844. /planlegger/#26h;ÅFRAN.2026; with "Vis øvinger og labber" on: three Wednesday blocks report `--planner-col-count: 2`, width 27 px, and their `.planner-block-code` bounding box is 1 px wide x 146 px tall — "FRA1010" rendered F/R/A/1/0/1/0 down seven lines. That is verbatim the failure layout.ts:18-21 says MAX_COLUMNS=2 exists to prevent ("~35 px per block, at which width `.planner-block-code`'s `overflow-wrap: anywhere` broke course codes one character per line"). The one-column case is broken too: /planlegger/#26h;BERGO.2026; at 390 px, every block is 56 px wide, `.planner-block-code` measures 30x62 px (three lines) and the 45-min blocks are 72 px tall, so `.planner-block-meta` (grid.ts:552, the "guaranteed second line" room·start) is clipped away by `.planner-block{overflow:hidden}`. My screenshot shows those blocks reading "ETT / ▪110 / 1" with nothing under them. The 56 px column comes from planner-week.css:66-70 dropping `.planner-grid` to `min-width: 21rem` below 40rem; half of that is 27 px.
- Failure: A student opens the planner on a phone — the primary device — and the week reads as a column of "ETT/110/1" fragments with no room or start time; turn on øvinger and any two-deep cluster becomes two 27 px slivers of vertically stacked single characters. The week's two-line minimum contract (grid.ts:547-552) is not met at the width the surface is most used at.
- Fix: Do NOT simply raise the mobile `min-width` — planner-week.css:66-70's 21rem is REVIEW.md A4's adjudicated fix ("drop min-width enough to fit five day headers") and reversing it re-opens the silent Thu/Fri truncation. Instead add a `@media (max-width: 40rem)` type step for the grid: drop `.planner-block-code` (planner-week.css:238-243) to `var(--text-xs)`, and clamp `MAX_COLUMNS` to 1 below 40rem so narrow clusters pile instead of splitting into 27 px.
- Verifier: Independently measured both cases with getBoundingClientRect at 390x844 and read the screenshots back — the numbers match the auditor's exactly (27 px block / 1x146 px code; 56 px block / 30x62 px code). I tried to kill it as a known-deferred item: the brief's deferred list and UX-STUDY §P2 do not cover it, and REVIEW A4 covers only the silent truncation, not legibility. I also checked whether it merely restates UX-STUDY S12 (grid chips truncating to "TMA44") — it does not: S12's fix was to WRAP instead of truncate, and this is the wrap's own failure mode at phone width. I corrected the auditor's fix, which would have reversed REVIEW A4.
### [grid-4] Pile course codes are hard-clipped mid-code at phone width — every pile row reads "ETT" — reintroducing exactly the symptom UX-STUDY S12 was fixed for, and contradicting the CSS comment that promises no code is clipped mid-stroke
- Where: `src/styles/planner-week.css:292` · ux · CONFIRMED · regression of UX-STUDY S12
- Evidence: planner-week.css:307-312 states the contract — "The codes are the point of the pile, so they stay whole: a pile that has more courses than it has room for clips the LIST, never a code mid-stroke" — and enforces `overflow-wrap: normal; white-space: nowrap` on `.planner-pile-row .planner-block-code`. But `.planner-pile-list` (:292-298) sets `overflow: hidden`, which clips horizontally as well as vertically. Measured live at 390x844 on /planlegger/#26h;BERGO.2026;: all four piles are 56 px wide and every one of the eight code spans reports clientWidth 24 px against scrollWidth 56 px. The screenshot shows the four days as "2 em… / ▪ ETT / ▪ ETT". UX-STUDY S12 recorded the identical measurement shape for the old grid chips (clientWidth ~40 px vs scrollWidth 64–74 px, "TMA44", "EXPH0") and was fixed by wrapping; the pile row opts out of that fix by re-disabling wrapping without giving the list any horizontal escape.
- Failure: On a phone, a BERGO student's tirsdag/onsdag/torsdag/fredag each show a pile whose two rows both read "ETT", distinguishable only by hue dot. The pile — the construct introduced specifically so nothing hides behind a count — carries no other content, so nothing readable survives: it is strictly worse than the "+N til" chip it replaced.
- Fix: In planner-week.css:292-298 replace `.planner-pile-list { overflow: hidden }` with `overflow-x: auto`, or (better) under `@media (max-width: 40rem)` shrink `.planner-pile-row .planner-block-code` to `var(--text-xs)` so a 7-character mono code fits the 56 px column. Do not leave a rule whose own comment claims an invariant a sibling rule breaks.
- Verifier: Reproduced with my own measurement (clientWidth 24 / scrollWidth 56 on all eight pile code spans across four piles) and by reading the screenshot. I tried to refute it as a duplicate of grid-3 — it is a different code site and a different mechanism (hard clip vs wrap), and it is the one place where the stylesheet's own documented invariant is provably violated. Also checked it is not on the brief's deferred list, in REVIEW.md, or in UX-STUDY §P2.
### [groups-2] Lecture-kind groups are rendered as mutually exclusive radios, but on TMA4400 they are complementary weekly sessions for the same programme cluster
- Where: `src/components/planner/popover.ts:200` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live on /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026;. TMA4400 draws 3 blocks: tirsdag 10:15–12:00, onsdag 14:15–16:00, torsdag 10:15–12:00. Its popover renders 13 radios and 8 checkboxes, none checked. Clicking the radio labelled "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" leaves exactly one TMA4400 block (tirsdag 10:15); hash becomes `TMA4400~forelesning-1-mtdt-mtiøt-mtkom`. The three lost/kept titles come straight from the live API: `curl /api/course/TMA4400/timetable?year=2026` returns among others "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" AND "Forelesning 2 MTIØT, MTKOM, MTDT" AND "Plenumsregning" — the same programme cluster appears in both Forelesning 1 and Forelesning 2, so they are two weekly sessions, not alternatives. classifyActivity puts all three in "lecture" (LECTURE_KEYWORDS `/\bplenum/`, activity.ts:66), pickableGroups (popover.ts:156-166) puts every lecture-kind option in one radio group, and pickLecture (popover.ts:122-125) replaces every lecture key with the one picked.
- Failure: An MTDT first-year opens their Matematikk 1 block, picks the parallel labelled with their own programme code out of thirteen radios, and loses two thirds of the course's week — the Thursday lecture and the Wednesday plenumsregning vanish from the grid and from the collision engine. Nothing on the page says anything was filtered out, and (per groups-6) there is no way back.
- Fix: Stop equating `kind === "lecture"` with "mutually exclusive parallel" in pickableGroups (popover.ts:156-166). Offer radios only when the lecture options are genuinely alternatives — the same signal defaultLectureKeys already uses, `looksLikeNumberedParallels` over the programme-narrowed survivors (groups.ts:85-87); when several lecture groups survive `entriesForProgram` and do not look like numbered parallels, they are complementary sessions and must be rendered as additive checkboxes.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as a deliberate decision. groups.ts's header does state the intent ("exactly one lecture parallel"), and e2e/flows.pw.ts:190-224 asserts that picking "Forelesning 2 MTBYGG" on TMA4400 leaves a Wednesday block — but that test only asserts `.first()`, never a count, so it does not endorse dropping the student's other lectures. Then checked whether the data really is complementary rather than parallel: the raw API titles show MTDT listed in both "Forelesning 1 …" and "Forelesning 2 …", and the untouched default branch correctly draws all three, which is only consistent with them being three real sessions. Reproduced the 3→1 collapse live. Downgraded from the auditor's blocker to major: the default week is correct, the loss requires a deliberate radio click, and the fix is a heuristic judgement about ambiguous upstream data rather than a one-line correction.
### [groups-3] The popover computes "din parallell" on the full-year timetable while the grid resolves it on the semester-narrowed one, so the picker denies a choice the week made
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:339` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: buildPopoverContext passes the raw bundle timetable — `groups: groupOptions(timetable)` / `defaults: defaultLectureKeys(timetable, state.programCode)` (plannerApp.ts:337-339) where `timetable = state.bundle.timetable`, the whole 2026 fetch — while the grid is fed `semesterWeekEntries` (plannerApp.ts:467-471, 1231-1233). Traced against the live API + data/semesters.json 26h weeks: EXPH0300 year-wide has 44 group options / 5 lecture options and `defaultLectureKeys(all, "MTTK") === []`; 26h-narrowed has 33 options / 3 lecture options and `defaultLectureKeys(sem, "MTTK") === ["forelesningsparallell-1-trondheim"]`. Confirmed live at /planlegger/#26h;MTTK.2024;+EXPH0300: the grid draws "Forelesningsparallell 1 Trondheim" (tirsdag 08:15–10:00, R1) and the popover for that very block returns 5 radios, anyChecked false, zero "(din parallell)" labels. The plannerApp comment at 325-343 states the purpose is that "`defaultLectureKeys` marks the programme's own parallel" — so this is the code failing its own stated intent, not a design choice.
- Failure: An MTTK student clicks the ex.phil lecture the planner picked for them and sees five parallels with none selected and no "(din parallell)" marker — the picker denies the week made a choice it did make. Clicking any radio then reads as a change from "no choice", and picking one of the four they were not scheduled for silently swaps their week to another campus. The same mismatch also empties the `ctx.defaults` that toggleOther seeds from, so it makes groups-1 fire on courses where the semester-narrowed default would have saved the lectures.
- Fix: In buildPopoverContext (plannerApp.ts:325-343) build the context from `semesterWeekEntries(state.bundle)` rather than the raw `state.bundle.timetable`, so groupOptions and defaultLectureKeys see exactly the entry set collectEntries runs applyGroupSelection over.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as the documented deliberate breadth of the picker ("Built from the *unfiltered* bundle timetable … the picker lists ALL of a course's groups"). That comment justifies the `groups` list being wide; it does not justify computing `defaults` on the wide set, and the same comment claims defaults marks the programme's own parallel — which I showed empirically fails. Then checked whether the discrepancy is theoretical: computed year vs semester defaults for 7 (course, programme) pairs against the live API and found EXPH0300/MTTK diverging ([] vs [forelesningsparallell-1-trondheim]), then reproduced the unchecked-radio state in the browser. Kept at major.
### [groups-5] When the programme's own lecture parallel can't be resolved, the week silently draws two campuses' lectures with no default marked and no note
- Where: `src/lib/planner/groups.ts:120` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live at /planlegger/#26h;BDIGSEC.2026;+EXPH0300 (1280x900). The grid draws TWO ex.phil lectures: "EXPH0300 / K105 · 10:15 / Forelesningsparallell 3 Gjøvik / uke 34–45" and "EXPH0300 / R1 · 08:15 / Forelesningsparallell 1 Trondheim / uke 34–45". Grid notes: empty. Status line: "ingen kollisjoner". Opening either block shows five radios, ALL unchecked, none labelled "(din parallell)". Traced: BDIGSEC is listed on both parallels' studyProgramKeys so entriesForProgram cannot narrow; looksLikeNumberedParallels (groups.ts:85-87) fails because the labels end in a city not a digit, so defaultLectureKeys returns `[]` and applyGroupSelection's default branch (groups.ts:176, `keys.length === 0 ||`) keeps both. Sibling programme BPROG resolves cleanly to `["forelesningsparallell-3-gjøvik"]`, so the failure is per-programme and invisible. grid.ts:775 skips `entry.isLecture` when building the "X har N grupper — velg din" margin note (grid.ts:975-987), so there is no equivalent affordance for an unresolved lecture parallel — the one case where the week is actually wrong.
- Failure: A BDIGSEC (Gjøvik) student sees two ex.phil lectures in their week, one in a Trondheim auditorium they will never enter, with nothing telling them which one the planner believes is theirs — the picker shows five unchecked radios and no marker. When the Trondheim slot overlaps another course the planner reports a collision the student does not have, which is exactly the false red DR-1 exists to prevent.
- Fix: Emit the ambiguity instead of swallowing it. In grid.ts, extend the unpicked-groups margin note (grid.ts:772-783 / 975-987) to cover the case where a course draws more than one lecture-kind group with no explicit pick — "EXPH0300 har 2 forelesningsparalleller — velg din", opening the same popover — and in popover.ts state it above the radios when `ctx.defaults` is empty while more than one lecture option is drawable.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as a restatement of the already-fixed UX-STUDY.md S5 ("parallel groups make real schedules unreadable"). S5's fix was the group engine + picker, and it works for the numbered-parallel and programme-partitioned cases (verified: TDT4110 and BPROG both resolve to one parallel). But S5's exact symptom — several parallels drawn simultaneously while the verdict line reads "ingen kollisjoner" — is still live for BDIGSEC, which I reproduced in the browser rather than inferring. Also checked whether the margin-note machinery already covers it: grid.ts:775's `if (entry.isLecture || entry.groupPicked) continue;` proves it does not. This is a genuine residual of S5, not a new theory; kept at major.
### [groups-6] Once a lecture radio is picked, the selection cannot be cleared — no control restores the programme default
- Where: `src/components/planner/popover.ts:122` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live on /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026;. TMA4400 starts with no `groups` key and 3 blocks. After clicking the "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" radio, np:plans holds `groups: ["forelesning-1-mtdt-mtiøt-mtkom"]` and 1 block. I then programmatically clicked each of the 8 Mattelab checkboxes on and off in turn — stored groups stayed `["forelesning-1-mtdt-mtiøt-mtkom"]`, blocks stayed at 1. Full control inventory of the popover: `["×", "Dropp", "Gå til emnesiden →"]`. Code path: pickLecture (popover.ts:122-125) always writes an array of length ≥1; toggleOther (popover.ts:137-144) filters against `base = selection`, which still carries the lecture key; an HTML radio cannot be unchecked by the user; and popover.ts:180-184 records that the only control that ever called `setSelection([])` was removed. store.ts:451-464's `groups.length === 0` branch (which deletes the property and returns to defaults) is therefore unreachable from the UI once a radio is touched.
- Failure: A student experiments with TMA4400's parallel picker, loses their Thursday lecture and Wednesday plenumsregning (groups-2), and finds no way back: no reset control, unchecking every checkbox does nothing, and for a programme course "Dropp" + "Legg tilbake" preserves the stored `groups`. The wrong week persists across reloads and in the share hash.
- Fix: Render a single explicit reset in renderGroupsSection (popover.ts:186-222) when `selection.length > 0` — e.g. a "Bruk programmets valg" link calling `store.setCourseGroups(detail.code, [])`. Copy names the actual effect, unlike the removed "Vis alle grupper" button whose label was the bug.
- Verifier: Attacked the claim by testing whether unchecking clears the selection at all: it DOES in the no-radio case — on TDT4120 I unchecked the one ticked checkbox live and localStorage went back to `{..."source":"manual"}` with no `groups` key and the Friday lecture returned. So the auditor's blanket "a group selection cannot be undone" is too broad; I narrowed the finding to what actually survives — once a lecture radio has been picked the key is permanently stuck, which I reproduced live on TMA4400 by toggling all 8 checkboxes with no effect. Also checked whether Dropp/Legg tilbake resets groups (store.ts dropCourse/restoreCourse leave `groups` untouched) and whether any other surface offers a reset (grepped grid.ts/plannerApp.ts for setCourseGroups — only popover.ts calls it). Severity kept at major, not blocker, because it is the amplifier of groups-1/groups-2 rather than an independent data-wrong state.
### [mob-1] Topbar theme toggle collapses to a 7px sliver at 360px (15px at 390px) and the studieinfo chip is cut mid-glyph with no ellipsis — worst in the cold state every first-time visitor sees
- Where: `src/styles/primitives.css:93` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced independently (Chromium, isMobile+hasTouch, DPR 2; `matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)")` true, `--control-h` = 44px). On a COLD `/planlegger/` (no plan) at 360x740: `.theme-toggle` getBoundingClientRect = 6.98 x 44, computed `flex-shrink: 1`, `min-width: auto`, `width: 6.98438px`; `#studieinfo-chip` clientWidth 44 vs scrollWidth 150, textContent "Velg studieprogram". At 390x844: toggle 14.75 x 44, chip clientWidth 66 vs scrollWidth 150. Same numbers on `/` (homepage, 360): toggle 6.98 x 44. Screenshot crops v-top-360.png / v-top-390.png show the chip reading "Velg s" / "Velg stud" chopped mid-character with NO ellipsis (`.np-btn` is `inline-flex`, so `text-overflow: ellipsis` from site.css:95-100 never applies to a flex item's text) and the crescent-moon glyph reduced to a vertical sliver. With a plan loaded (v-wk-390.png) the chip reads "MTDT · 2" with the 2 cut. Cause traced: `.np-icon-btn` (primitives.css:93-97) declares `width: var(--control-h)` but no `flex-shrink: 0`, and ThemeToggle.astro:33-35 only overrides `display: grid` — so as a direct flex child of `.site-topbar` it shrinks below its 44px width. No e2e test uses a mobile viewport (grep for `viewport|isMobile|360|390` across `e2e/*.pw.ts` returns nothing), so nothing guards this.
- Failure: Any visitor on a 360px Android (the single most common phone width) loads any page: the theme toggle is a 7px-wide sliver — not a usable tap target, so dark mode is unreachable on that device class. Alongside it, the sitewide studieinfo control — the only opener for the §0 "velg studieprogram" flow outside /planlegger/ — reads "Velg s" cut mid-letter, so the product's entry-point CTA looks like a rendering bug rather than a button.
- Fix: Add `flex-shrink: 0` to `.np-icon-btn` in src/styles/primitives.css:93 (it already fixes `width: var(--control-h)`; nothing about it should shrink). For the chip, in the `@media (max-width: 480px)` block at src/styles/site.css:102-106 give it a floor (`flex: 0 1 auto; min-width: 6rem`) and let `.site-brand` be the shrinking element instead; wrap the chip's label in a `<span>` in src/layouts/Layout.astro:155-160 so `text-overflow: ellipsis` has an inline box to act on.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways: (1) grepped for any `flex-shrink: 0` on `.theme-toggle`/`.np-icon-btn` — none exists, ThemeToggle.astro's scoped style only sets `display: grid`; (2) checked whether `min-width: auto` would floor it at content size — measured 6.98px, so it does not (the inner `<svg>` has a 0x0 client rect); (3) checked whether it is a plan-loaded-only artifact — it is worse cold, where the chip carries the 18-character "Velg studieprogram". Also checked `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` == 360, so the topbar 'fits' only in the sense site.css:72-74's comment claims, by crushing two of four children. Survived.
### [mob-3] Every week block renders its course code as three mid-token fragments at phone width ("TMA / ■441 / 2"), with the hue dot sitting between fragment 1 and 2
- Where: `src/styles/planner-week.css:238` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced at BOTH 390x844 and 360x740 on `/planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026;TDT4109.1,TMA4400.1,TMA4412.1,EXPH0300.1`: every `.planner-block-code` measures 29.6 x 62.5 px with `Range.selectNodeContents(...).getClientRects().length === 3` (three line boxes), computed `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, `font-size: 13.44px`. Day columns are 57.6px, blocks 55.6px. Screenshot v-wk-390.png reads, in three adjacent 08:15 blocks: "TMA" / "■441" / "2", "EXP" / "■H03" / "00", "TMA" / "■441" / "2", and below "TMA" / "■440" / "0" twice — the colour dot renders between the first and second fragment. The 62.5px code then squeezes the rest of a two-hour block so every remaining line ellipsises: "R1 ·…", "Forel…", "1 MTD…", "EL5 …". planner-week.css:235-237 documents the intended behaviour as wrapping to TWO rows ("'TDT4110' → two rows"); three rows of ~3 characters exceeds it. The same file at :276-282 already records this exact failure mode ("`.planner-block-code`'s `overflow-wrap: anywhere` broke a course code one character per line") and fixes it — but only for pile blocks, at :309-312 (`overflow-wrap: normal; white-space: nowrap`).
- Failure: A student with the standard MTDT kull-2026 autumn plan opens /planlegger/ on a 390px iPhone. Monday 08:15 and Tuesday 10:15 both read "TMA / ■440-something"; telling TMA4400 from TMA4412 requires reading a single trailing digit on a third line, and the room and activity lines are all truncated to "R1 ·…" / "Forel…". The primary surface's primary identifier is not readable at a glance.
- Fix: In src/styles/planner-week.css:238-243, under `@media (max-width: 40rem)`, apply the pile-block treatment scaled down: `white-space: nowrap; overflow-wrap: normal; font-size: var(--text-xs); letter-spacing: -0.02em` so a 7-8 character code fits one line in a 57.6px column; or hoist the hue dot out of the `.np-tag` flow at that width so the code gets the full ~39.6px.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as (a) a restatement of UX-STUDY S12 and (b) a documented decision. Neither holds: S12's fix menu was "wrap/shrink or front-truncate" and wrap was implemented, but planner-week.css:235-237 documents the intended result as TWO rows for a 7-char code, and I measured three line boxes for every block at both 390 and 360; and the repo's own comment at :276-282 already classifies mid-token fragmentation as a defect, fixing it for piles only. Also checked whether the fragments were a measurement artifact by reading the screenshot back — they are literally on screen with the dot interleaved. Not a regression of S12 (S12 was truncation, this is the state of its fix), so I dropped the auditor's `regression_of`. Survived.
### [modals-1] Add-course rows render "Legg til" and "Fjern" at once — `hidden` is defeated by `.np-btn { display: inline-flex }` in the add dialog
- Where: `src/styles/site.css:332` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `setAddState` (src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:115-124) toggles only the `hidden` *attribute*; both buttons carry `.np-btn`, which declares `display: inline-flex` at src/styles/primitives.css:65-66 (author origin, beats the UA `[hidden] { display: none }`). `.studieinfo-dialog` carries the antidote at site.css:172-174 with a comment explaining exactly this; the `.add-course-dialog` block (site.css:332-342) does not. I reproduced it live at 127.0.0.1:8788/planlegger/, query "TDT4100": before add `{add:{hidden:false,disp:"flex"}, added:{hidden:true,disp:"none"}, remove:{hidden:true,disp:"flex"}}`; after add `{add:{hidden:true,disp:"flex"}, added:{hidden:false,disp:"block"}, remove:{hidden:false,disp:"flex"}}`. Row textContent after add: "TDT4100Objektorientert programmeringLegg tilLagt til ✓Fjern". I also confirmed the mechanism in-page: a bare `<div hidden>` computes `display:none`, and the same node with `class="np-btn"` computes `inline-flex`.
- Failure: Student opens "Legg til emne" and searches TDT41. Every result row shows both a "Legg til" and a "Fjern" button for courses that are not in the plan. After pressing "Legg til" on TDT4100 the same row reads "Legg til | Lagt til ✓ | Fjern" — three mutually exclusive states at once, so the only feedback that the add took is a badge sitting next to a still-clickable add button. The phantom "Fjern" is also what makes the destructive path in modals-3 reachable from search results.
- Fix: Add `.add-course-dialog [hidden] { display: none }` to src/styles/site.css beside the `.add-course-dialog` block (mirror of site.css:172-174 and its comment). Add an e2e assertion that `.add-course-add` is hidden after an add — e2e/flows.pw.ts:401-402 only asserts `.add-course-added` is visible, which is why this shipped green; there are no unit tests for addCourse.ts at all.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by checking whether Chrome's UA sheet uses `display: none !important` for `[hidden]` (it does not — verified in-page: `.np-btn` on a `hidden` div computes `inline-flex`), and whether the auditor's claimed side effect of the phantom "Fjern" was real. That part is WRONG and I corrected the failure scenario: clicking "Fjern" on a course not in the plan changed neither `location.hash` nor `np:plans` (removeCourse filters nothing out; savePlan writes the identical state). The visual/state defect itself reproduces exactly, on every row, on the product's only add surface — major stands.
### [modals-2] Studieinfo cannot save a programme NTNU publishes no study plan for (MTPROD, MTPETR, BALIT, BFRA…) — Lagre answers with an instruction the UI makes impossible to follow
- Where: `src/components/planner/studieinfo.ts:585` · product-conformance · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `commit()` returns early at studieinfo.ts:585-589 with `hintText = "Velg kull for å lagre studieprogrammet."` whenever a programme is staged and `stagedCohort === null`, while `renderKull` (studieinfo.ts:466-470) hides the entire kull section when `programMissing` — so there is no kull to choose. Reproduced live from a cleared localStorage: picked "BALIT · Allmenn litteraturvitenskap" → hint "Fant ingen studieplan for dette programmet. Du kan fortsatt legge til emnene dine selv.", `kullHidden:true`, `kullText:""`. Pressed Lagre → `{open:true, hint:"Velg kull for å lagre studieprogrammet.", kullHidden:true, profile:null}` and the topbar still reads "Velg studieprogram". The honest hint is overwritten by the unfollowable one and `np:profile` is never written. `findProgramPlan` tries guessYear, -1, -2 (programPlan.ts:133-164 `MAX_STEP_BACK_TRIES = 3`); I curled 2026/2025/2024 for BALIT, MTPROD, MTPETR, BFRA — 404 on all nine, while MTDT is 200. All four are in data/programs.json and offered by the typeahead (MTPROD = "Produktutvikling og produksjon, Master 5 år, Trondheim"). A deterministic 58-programme sample of the 403 gave 10 with no plan at any of the three years (~17 %), consistent with the auditor's 74/403.
- Failure: An MTPROD student opens the studieinfo modal (the only programme picker, §0 addendum 2), types "Produktutvikling", picks their own programme, presses Lagre. The modal replies "Velg kull for å lagre studieprogrammet" with no kull control anywhere on screen and refuses to close; their programme is never recorded, so the topbar chip stays "Velg studieprogram" on this and every future visit and the planner keeps prompting them to pick a programme they just picked.
- Fix: In studieinfo.ts `commit()`, gate the early return on `!programMissing`. When the programme has no study plan there is nothing to classify, so store the programme identity with no prefill and close, leaving the honest "Fant ingen studieplan … legg til emnene dine selv" hint standing (`PlanProgram.cohort` is currently required — either allow it to be optional or keep the last selected/current year). Minimum viable: when `programMissing`, replace the guard's copy with something actionable and let the dialog close.
- Verifier: Attacked it three ways. (1) Is the state reachable? Yes — reproduced end to end in Chromium against the live worker. (2) Is it just data honesty we already document? Partly: the pre-Lagre hint is honest and the manual-add fallback is real and signposted, which is why I nearly refuted it — but the defect is that pressing the primary button *replaces* that honest hint with an impossible instruction and blocks the modal's completion, and the programme identity (used for the topbar chip and for `entriesForProgram` section filtering) is silently unrecordable. (3) Is it a known-deferred item? Nothing in ROADMAP/PRODUCT §9/§12/REVIEW/UX-STUDY covers plan-less programmes in the studieinfo modal. Severity kept at major on scale (MTPROD/MTPETR-sized programmes) plus front-door position.
### [modals-3] Add-course "Fjern" hard-deletes a programme course — PRODUCT's data model says a programme course is "never deleted" — and the deletion silently reverts on the next real page load
- Where: `src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:218` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: addCourse.ts:217-220 calls `deps.store.removeCourse(code)` unconditionally; store.ts:318-323 filters the code out of `plan.courses` regardless of `source`. PRODUCT.md:316 defines `dropped` as "programme course grayed out, **never deleted**" and the hash grammar (PRODUCT.md:337-339) gives `-` for a dropped programme course versus `+` for "a manual add (removable outright)". The rail does it correctly — plannerApp.ts:923-925 `const label = isProgram ? (isDropped ? "Legg tilbake" : "Dropp") : "Fjern"` with the comment "§0.3's verb for the reversible one is 'Dropp' (D3)". Live on `#26h;MTDT.2026;` (rail HMS0002, TDT4109, TMA4400, TMA4412, EXPH0300): opening the add modal, searching TDT4109 and clicking "Fjern" left rail HMS0002, TMA4400, TMA4412, EXPH0300 and hash `#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300` — plain deletion, not the `-TDT4109` dropped form. A *fresh document load* of that exact URL restores TDT4109 within ~1,5 s (rail back to 5 rows, hash back to `…HMS0002,TDT4109,TMA4400…`): plannerApp.ts:1580-1587 re-derives with `programDerivedFor === null` and `sameProgramSet` (plannerApp.ts:234-246) fails on the length mismatch, so `setProgramPlan` re-adds it.
- Failure: MTDT-2026 student opens "Legg til emne", searches TDT4109 to check it, presses the "Fjern" that every row now shows (modals-1). The course disappears from the rail, grid, credit total and exam list, and the rail's "Legg tilbake" is gone with it, so there is no undo. On the next visit (or when they open the shared link they just copied) TDT4109 is back with no explanation — and a course they genuinely meant to drop is therefore not durable either.
- Fix: In addCourse.ts's remove handler, branch on the stored course's `source`: `deps.store.dropCourse(code)` for `source: "program"`, `deps.store.removeCourse(code)` only for manual adds — and label the button "Dropp" in the programme case, matching plannerApp.ts:925.
- Verifier: My first attempt to reproduce the re-add FAILED (the course stayed deleted) and I nearly refuted that half — but the failure was my own: navigating to a different `#hash` on the same document is a same-document navigation, so plannerApp never re-mounts. Re-run as a genuine load of `…#26h;MTDT.2026;HMS0002,TMA4400,TMA4412,EXPH0300`, TDT4109 was back at the 1,5 s sample and stayed for 9 s; a plain `/planlegger/` visit off localStorage did the same. Both halves therefore hold. Also checked that the destructive click is reachable without an add first — it is, because of modals-1.
### [pc-2] The Karakterer figure draws kont/utsatt sittings as ordinary semester distributions on a shared y-scale, manufacturing a difficulty signal
- Where: `src/lib/planner/grades.ts:110` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `buildGradeSemesters` buckets on `${row.year}|${season}` alone (grades.ts:110) with no notion of ordinary vs. re-sit sitting, and `gradeChart.ts:44/65` draws every bucket as an equal-sized small multiple against a shared `peakPercent`. Live-verified on the running server: `curl /api/course/TDT4100` → `teachingStart: "Vår 2027"`, exams `[Ordinær eksamen / Vår 2027, Utsatt eksamen / Sommer 2027]` — spring-taught, spring-examined. Aggregating `/api/course/TDT4100/grades` myself: Vår buckets run n=508–596, Høst buckets n=62–85, and DBH labels the same population "Sommer" before 2010 (2005 Sommer n=97, 2006 Sommer n=73) — the Høst rows *are* the utsatt cohort. On http://127.0.0.1:8788/emne/TDT4100/ the figure renders exactly 6 charts / 36 bars with headings ["Høst 2025 · 68 kandidater", "Vår 2025 · 508 kandidater", "Høst 2024 · 62", "Vår 2024 · 596", "Høst 2023 · 85", "Vår 2023 · 572"] — three of six charts are re-sit cohorts, unlabelled. It is systemic, not a TDT4100 quirk: TMA4115 shows Vår 2024 n=697 F=6 % beside Høst 2024 n=26 F=54 %; IT2805 produces a "Vår 2025 · 6 kandidater" chart the same size as its 252-candidate autumn one. DR-3 explicitly filters kont out of the exam surface ("Ordinary-only by default; kont filtered out"); this surface does the opposite with no label and no minimum-n.
- Failure: A student comparing TMA4115 opens /emne/TMA4115/ and sees six equal charts; the second-newest is "Høst 2024 · 26 kandidater" with a full-height F bar at 54 %, next to "Vår 2024" at 6 %. They conclude the course has become brutal. The 26 candidates are the utsatt cohort — students who already failed once. The site has fabricated a difficulty signal out of a self-selected re-sit population, which is the §9 no-fabricated-signals line.
- Fix: Two independent guards in `src/lib/planner/grades.ts`: (a) carry the course's teaching term (already on `/api/course/:code`, `teachingStart`) into `buildGradeSemesters` and either drop off-term buckets the way DR-3 drops kont, or label them "utsatt eksamen" and exclude them from `peakPercent`'s shared scale; (b) drop buckets under a minimum candidate count (~20) rather than drawing a 6-candidate chart at the same visual weight as a 500-candidate one. If pc-1 is resolved by deleting the figure, this is moot.
- Verifier: I attacked this as "the data is literally true, so the auditor is over-reading". It survives: the numbers are true but the *label* ("Høst 2025") and the equal-weight small-multiple form both assert "this is that semester's distribution", which is false for a spring-only course. I then tried to refute it as a one-course anomaly by pulling grades for TMA4100, TFY4104, TDT4110, TDT4120, TDT4109, TDT4180, IT2805, EXPH0300, TMA4115 — TMA4100/TFY4104/TDT4120/TDT4180 have no off-term bucket at all (so the auditor's implied universality is wrong), but TDT4100, TMA4115 and IT2805 reproduce it, and TMA4115's Høst 2024 (n=26, 54 % F) is worse than the example the auditor gave. Kept at major; not a blocker only because it needs the student to draw the inference rather than being handed a false verdict.
### [pd-2] `loadBundles` never re-renders the provenance line, so DR-8's moat sentence is stale or missing on every first load — and the per-course failure clause is structurally unreachable
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1486` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of U9 (REVIEW.md) — fix landed but is never re-invoked after loadBundles
- Evidence: `loadBundles` ends with `renderCreditLine(); renderCourseRows(); renderGapLine(); renderGridAndExams();` (lines 1483-1486) — `renderProvenance()` is absent, unlike `renderPlanDependents()` (line 1500-1507) and the index `.then` (line 1668) which both call it. Live: (a) healthy 3-course hash plan, sampled at +2.5 s and +6.5 s after settle — `#planner-provenance` = "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026). Uoffisiell." at both samples while `#planner-grid-status` already read "2 kollisjoner denne uka" and the week was fully drawn; (b) the flagship flow `#26h;MTDT.2026;`, sampled at +2.5/+6.5/+11.5 s — "eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026) · studieplan for kull 2026. Uoffisiell.", i.e. **no timetable clause at all**, because the last `renderProvenance()` ran while every bundle was still `null` and `loading` was still false; (c) with one timetable 500ing, provenance = "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen …" and never contains the "Fikk ikke hentet timeplan for TDT4109." sentence that the `failures` loop at lines 1421-1427 exists to build.
- Failure: Any first load of /planlegger/ — including the flagship "programme + kull → your week" path. The line PRODUCT §3/DR-8 calls the moat either claims a fetch is still running when the week is drawn, or omits where the timetable came from entirely, and never names a per-course failure. REVIEW.md U9's fix explicitly required "fikk ikke hentet timeplan for TDT4109" in this line; the composition code landed but is never invoked after bundles resolve, so the U9 fix is only half-live.
- Fix: Add `renderProvenance();` after line 1486 in `loadBundles` (it is idempotent and cheap, and every sibling orchestrator already does it).
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by finding another caller that re-runs after bundles land: `renderAll` (line 1462) only runs on store change, `renderPlanDependents` only on study-plan resolution, and the `loadPlannerIndex().then` (1668) fires before the bundles do. Verified live on both a hash plan and the MTDT programme prefill; in the MTDT case the timetable clause is permanently missing, which is worse than the auditor's "stale" framing for the healthy case. Kept at major rather than blocker: no wrong *data* is shown, but DR-8's stated MUST surface is inert.
### [pd-3] A failed `/data/search-index.json` is memoised as a rejection forever: exam column spins permanently, add-course dialog is unusable, and the provenance line asserts a false fact about NTNU
- Where: `src/lib/planner/data.ts:162` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live probe aborting `**/data/search-index.json` on `#26h;-;+TDT4109,+TMA4400,+TMA4412`. Six seconds after load, with the week fully drawn ("2 kollisjoner denne uka", real collision notes): `#planner-exam-status` = "henter eksamensdatoer …", `#planner-exam-list-host` = "" (nothing rendered), `#planner-provenance` = "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · **eksamensdatoer ikke publisert for Høst 2026**. Uoffisiell." Mechanism traced: `loadPlannerIndex` (data.ts:162-166) assigns the rejected promise to `indexMemo` and never clears it, so no retry is possible for the session; plannerApp.ts:1670-1672 swallows it in an empty `.catch()` whose comment claims "the rest of the page still works", leaving `plannerIndex === null` forever; `examLoading = anyLoading || (states.length > 0 && plannerIndex === null)` (line 1338) is then permanently true, and `examUncovered` (line 1346) requires `!examLoading`, so the honest C3 message can never fire either. `indexCoversSemester(null, "26h")` returns `false` (data.ts:206), which is what drives the false "ikke publisert" branch at line 1412. addCourse's own docs (addCourse.ts:48) confirm a `null` index renders "henter emner …" indefinitely.
- Failure: A student behind a proxy/ad-blocker/quota-exceeded cache that drops the index JSON (while /api/* succeeds) opens /planlegger/ with courses. The exam column spins forever with no error and no retry, the add-course dialog is permanently stuck on "Henter emner …", and the provenance line states NTNU has not published exam dates for Høst 2026 — false; they are in the build artifact we failed to download. A fetch failure is reported to the student as an upstream publication fact.
- Fix: data.ts:162-166 — clear the memo on rejection so a retry is possible (`const p = fetchJson(...); indexMemo = p.catch(e => { indexMemo = null; throw e; })`). plannerApp.ts:1670 — replace the empty catch with a `plannerIndexFailed` flag; gate `examLoading` on it, render "Fikk ikke hentet eksamensdatoer" + a "Prøv igjen" that re-calls `loadPlannerIndex()`, and split the provenance branch at 1410-1414 so "ikke publisert" is not printed for a download failure.
- Verifier: Attacked the reachability (is a same-origin static asset failing realistic while /api works?) — it is narrower than an API failure, but the memoised rejection means one transient failure is terminal for the whole session, and the empty catch's own comment documents an intent ("the rest of the page still works") that the live probe disproves. Also checked whether C3's `examUncovered` message rescues it — it cannot, it is gated behind `!examLoading` which can never become true. Kept at major: two surfaces are left permanently unusable and one prints a false statement about NTNU.
### [pd-5] A per-course fetch failure is memoised for the whole SPA session with no retry affordance — it survives in-site navigation after the network heals
- Where: `src/lib/planner/data.ts:123` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `bundleMemo.set(key, promise)` (data.ts:123) stores the promise of a *resolved* `CourseBundle` including its `errors[]`, at module scope, with no TTL; CLAUDE.md's ClientRouter rule means the module is not re-evaluated on in-site navigation. Live probe: `**/api/course/TDT4109/timetable**` fulfilled 500 once (route hit count 1), row reads "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Upstream boom"; the route is then healed and the student navigates via real topbar links /planlegger/ → /emner/ → /planlegger/ (ClientRouter swap, url back to `…/planlegger/#26h;-;%2BTDT4109,%2BTMA4400`). Route hit count still **1** — no refetch attempted — row still reads "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Upstream boom" and `#planner-grid-status` still reads "ingen kollisjoner". The only recovery, `retryBundles` (plannerApp.ts:1197-1206, which does call `clearCourseBundleMemo()`), is mounted exclusively inside the `showFallback` state-3 branch (lines 1291-1299), unreachable when only some courses failed (0 "Prøv igjen" buttons observed in both failure probes).
- Failure: A six-course plan loses one timetable to a transient blip. That course is missing from the week and from the collision verdict for the rest of the session; clicking around the site and coming back changes nothing. Only a hard reload — which nothing on screen suggests — recovers.
- Fix: data.ts `fetchCourseBundle`: inside the async IIFE, `bundleMemo.delete(key)` before returning when `errors.length > 0` (leaving the promise in the map while in flight preserves in-flight dedup). Plus, in `renderCourseRows` (plannerApp.ts:952-955), render the "fikk ikke hentet …" meta as a per-course "Prøv igjen" button that resets that state and re-runs `loadBundles`.
- Verifier: Attacked it by checking (a) whether `retryBundles`/`clearCourseBundleMemo` is reachable in a partial-failure plan — it is not, verified live with 0 retry buttons; (b) whether an in-site ClientRouter navigation re-evaluates the module and so drops the memo — it does not, proven by the route hit counter staying at 1 across a full nav round trip with the network healed; (c) whether some TTL or cache-busting exists — `bundleMemo` is a bare module-level `Map` with no eviction. Survives.
### [pd-6] The block popover offers groups from the other term (the timetable response carries both), and picking one silently removes the course from the week
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:337` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `curl '/api/course/EXPH0300/timetable?year=2026&version=1'` returns 84 entries spanning `2026_VÅR` (51) and `2026_HØST` (33) in one array; `TimetableEntry` (data.ts:9-21) does not model the `term` field the API actually sends, and nothing in the planner filters on it. `buildPopoverContext` deliberately builds `groups: groupOptions(timetable)` and `defaults: defaultLectureKeys(timetable, …)` from the **raw** bundle (plannerApp.ts:337/339). Grouping the live response by title shows 11 groups with zero entries in 26h's teaching weeks (34-47), all from the spring half — incl. "Forelesningsparallell 3 Ålesund", "Forelesningsparallell 4 Gjøvik", "Seminargruppe 27/28 Trondheim", 5 Ålesund and 2 Gjøvik seminar groups. Isolated live with a *lecture* parallel (so the øving-visibility toggle cannot confound it), plan `#26h;-;+EXPH0300,+TDT4109`: picking "Forelesningsparallell 3 Gjøvik" (a real autumn group) leaves 1 EXPH0300 block; picking "Forelesningsparallell 3 Ålesund" (spring-only, offered by our own popover) drops EXPH0300 to **0 blocks**, with no note in `#planner-grid-notes`, no "undervises ikke i valgt semester" on the course row, the credit line still counting it, and the choice written into the shareable hash (`…%2BEXPH0300~forelesningsparallell-3-alesund…`).
- Failure: Student plans Høst 2026 with EXPH0300, clicks the exphil block to pick their parallel/seminar group, and selects one of the 11 options the tool itself lists that only exist in spring. Exphil disappears entirely from the week — no block, no note, no error — and the broken selection is encoded in the URL they may share.
- Fix: plannerApp.ts:337/339 — build `groups`/`defaults` from the semester-narrowed entries (`semesterWeekEntries(state.bundle)`, defined at line 467) rather than the raw `timetable`. That keeps the intended "all cross-programme parallels" behaviour (semesterWeekEntries does not narrow by programme) while dropping the other term's groups. Also add `term: string | null` to `TimetableEntry` (data.ts:9-21) so the field the API already sends is modelled.
- Verifier: I tried hard to kill this and partly did: the auditor's demo ("Seminargruppe 27 Trondheim" → 3 blocks → 0) does NOT isolate the term problem — I reproduced the same disappearance with "Seminargruppe 20 Trondheim", which *does* have autumn entries (weeks 35-46), because a seminar-only selection is hidden by the muted øving layer (`mutedLayerAutoRevealed = !showOthers && lectureEntries.length === 0` is global across courses, grid.ts:756). That part is a grid/group-layer bug, not this one. So I re-ran the experiment with lecture parallels, where the øving toggle is irrelevant, and the term effect is clean and confirmed: autumn parallel → 1 block, spring parallel → 0 blocks. The 11-orphan-group count in the popover is confirmed directly against the live API response. Severity kept at major (silent loss of a course from the primary surface, encoded into the shared URL); evidence and mechanism corrected.
### [perf-1] Course page grows 844 px → 3 181 px after first paint — CLS 0.26 on localhost, 0.61 on a realistic network
- Where: `src/pages/emne/[code].astro:91` · perf · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced independently with my own Playwright probe at 390x844 against the live server, PerformanceObserver('layout-shift', buffered:true), waiting 3.5 s after domcontentloaded:
  delay=0   h0=844 h=3181 CLS=0.2625  shifts=[{t:74,v:0.2625}]
  delay=900 h0=844 h=3181 CLS=0.6103  shifts=[{t:950,v:0.2590},{t:964,v:0.3513}]
So the shift is not an artefact of the auditor's injected 900 ms delay — even with the API answering from a warm local worker in ~2 ms the page still scores 0.26, already past the 0.25 "poor" threshold; a real 4G RTT just moves the shift a second later and doubles it. Code confirmed: the four `.emne-section` blocks at lines 91/97/131/139 ship a single-line `Henter … …` placeholder plus `<div data-role="body" hidden>`, and `.emne-section` in the scoped `<style>` sets only `margin-bottom` — no reserved height anywhere. The author demonstrably knows the pattern: `.emne-clash-slot` carries `min-height: 1.6em` with the comment "Reserved so the verdict landing does not shove the week down the page".
- Failure: Student on 4G opens /emne/TDT4100/ from a search hit, starts reading the Eksamen line at y≈428 px and taps it ~1 s later. The Timeplan grid has expanded ~514 px underneath in the meantime, so the tap lands on a week-grid cell and the sentence they were reading is off-screen. Repeats on every one of the 5 470 course pages.
- Fix: In `src/pages/emne/[code].astro`'s scoped CSS reserve a box for the two big growers instead of letting them collapse to one line: `min-height` on `#timetable-section [data-role="body"]` sized to the rendered week grid and on `#grades-section [data-role="body"]` sized to `--grades-plot-h`. `.emne-clash-slot`'s existing `min-height` is the precedent to copy.
- Verifier: I tried to kill this two ways. (a) Is the 900 ms delay artificial? I re-measured with no route delay at all against the live worker — CLS still 0.2625, so the finding survives without the auditor's instrumentation. (b) Is it a documented deferred decision? grepped DESIGN.md / PRODUCT.md / ROADMAP.md for min-height / reserved / skeleton / shift — the only hit is an unrelated `--radius-lg` line, and REVIEW.md/UX-STUDY.md never raised layout stability, so it is not a restatement or a regression of a prior item either. Nothing excuses it. Severity kept at major: 500–1 800 px of movement on the primary read surface is a real mis-tap, and it is not on the deferred-polish list.
### [perf-2] No `_headers` file — every content-hashed asset is served `max-age=0, must-revalidate`, costing a 304 round-trip on every navigation including in-site ClientRouter swaps
- Where: `wrangler.jsonc:11` · perf · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `find . -maxdepth 3 -name _headers -not -path ./node_modules/*` → nothing; `public/` contains only `data/`. Live curl confirms `Cache-Control: public, max-age=0, must-revalidate` on `/_astro/Layout.6o6mCF-a.css`, `/_astro/schibsted-grotesk-latin.Bb8VGrTG.woff2`, `/_astro/studieinfo.D5p2DPur.js`, `/data/search-index.json` and the HTML. The literal `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate` and `_headers` handling both exist in `node_modules/wrangler/wrangler-dist/cli.js`, so this is the bundled asset-server default, not a dev-only quirk. My own Playwright probe (single context, hard navigation TDT4100 → TDT4109): first load 19×200; second load **15 × HTTP 304**, every one with `transferSize: 300, encodedBodySize: 0` — both variable fonts (46 752 B + 36 476 B), the 19 391 B shared stylesheet, `planner-week.css` and all 12 JS chunks. Stronger than the auditor claimed: an **in-site ClientRouter navigation** (/emner/ → link click to /emne/TDT4100/) still fired 3 × 304, one of them the render-blocking `/_astro/planner-week.BABxxX4K.css` — and Astro's swap waits on new stylesheets, so that revalidation sits directly in the navigation critical path.
- Failure: Returning student on 4G (~100 ms RTT) taps a course link from /emner/. Astro's swap blocks on a conditional request for `planner-week.css` that will return a 304 with a zero-length body, adding a full RTT of nothing before the week grid is styled; a hard load or a link from a search engine pays that 15 times over, on every visit.
- Fix: Add `public/_headers` (Astro copies `public/` verbatim into `dist/`; the Workers asset server reads `_headers` from the assets directory):
```
/_astro/*
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable
/data/search-index.json
  Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400
```
Leave HTML on the default `max-age=0, must-revalidate` — that part is correct.
- Verifier: Attacked it as "wrangler dev is not production" — refuted by finding both the default constant and `_headers` support inside wrangler's bundled asset worker, i.e. the same code path Cloudflare runs. Attacked it as "ClientRouter keeps assets in memory so real users never re-request them" — refuted by measuring an actual in-site link click, which still issued 3 conditional requests including the render-blocking stylesheet. I considered downgrading to minor (a 304 is cheap, body is 300 B) and decided against it: it is one RTT in the critical path of every navigation, it hits the flagship planner too, and the fix is a five-line file that simply does not exist.
### [plan-3] The 404 step-back substitutes another cohort's curriculum invisibly while the provenance line asserts the student's own kull
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1406` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `findProgramPlanUncached` returns {plan, year} with the year actually found after up to 3 step-backs (programPlan.ts:155-164); `loadPeriodCourses` destructures only result.plan (plannerApp.ts:1536-1547) and discards result.year, and the response's own plan.year is never read either. `renderProvenance` then unconditionally appends `studieplan for kull ${plan.program.cohort}` (plannerApp.ts:1406). Live: MPPR year=2026 → 404, year=2025 → 404, year=2024 → 200 (plan.year === 2024). Chromium at /planlegger/#26h;MPPR.2026; renders "Emnekatalogen hentet 24. jul 2026 fra NTNU · studieplan for kull 2026. Uoffisiell." MTMT is the damaging version: 2026 → 404, 2025 → 404, 2024 → 200, and the 2024 document's period 1 is EXPH0300 + HMS0001 + TDT4110 + TMA4100 + TMT4171 — a full confident 30 sp prefill labelled "studieplan for kull 2026". I reproduced the same class of substitution live for MTDT kull 2027 at Høst 2027: the modal even says "Fant ingen studieplan for kull 2027", and after Lagre the week is a full "30 av 30 sp" with provenance "studieplan for kull 2027".
- Failure: A 2026 MTMT student picks programme + kull, gets a full green 30 sp week, and the provenance line — the product's stated honesty surface (DR-8, PRODUCT §0's "we show you exactly what we couldn't verify") — tells them it is their own cohort's plan when it is the 2024 cohort's. If NTNU changed the 2026 first-year set (the reason no 2026 document exists yet), every course is wrong and nothing on screen says so.
- Fix: In `loadPeriodCourses` keep the found year (result.year, or equivalently result.plan.year which the API already returns) in a module-scope variable, and in `renderProvenance` (plannerApp.ts:1406) emit `studieplan for kull ${foundYear} (ingen egen plan for kull ${plan.program.cohort})` when they differ.
- Verifier: Tried to refute by looking for the disclosure elsewhere: the banner/context line ("kull 2026 · Høst 2026"), the credit note, the question panel and the plan panel were all dumped in Chromium for MPPR and MTDT-2027 and none of them mentions a substituted cohort. Also checked whether the step-back is bounded enough to be harmless — MAX_STEP_BACK_TRIES is 3, so it can reach back two years, and MTMT/MSCHEM/MPPR all actually do. Not covered by any test and not a documented decision.
### [search-1] No relevance ranking: results are raw catalog order, so TMA4100 is 77th for "matematikk" and the 200-cap systematically deletes Trondheim T-codes
- Where: `src/pages/emner/index.astro:315` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Traced: `render()` does `index.courses.filter(...)` (315-319) then `.slice(0, RESULT_CAP)` (323) — no scoring, no sort, and `public/data/search-index.json` is code-ordered (AAR1025 first, ~alphabetical). Live at /emner/?q=matematikk (1280x900): status "112 treff"; measured in-page, TMA4100 "Matematikk 1" is index 76 (row 77) at document offsetTop 4355 px = 4.8 viewports; the visible top ten are AR100919, BMA1010, BMA1020, BR100121, BR100131, DCST1004, DID3940, FO001321, IMAA1002, IMAA2012 — two of them (DCST1004, DID3940) are "ikke undervist i 2026" rows ranked above the live canonical course. Live /emner/?q=teknologi: "viser 200 av 242 treff", 200 rows, last row TMAT3020. I enumerated the 42 dropped rows offline over the same index: 39 of 42 are Trondheim (TMM4550, TMT4900, TPK4190, TTM4250 …), 2 Gjøvik, 1 Ålesund — the cap is a campus filter nobody asked for. No doc adjudicates ranking or the cap: grep for "relevan"/"sorter"/RESULT_CAP across docs/ returns nothing about /emner/ ordering, and no e2e test covers result order.
- Failure: First-year student types "matematikk" to add Matematikk 1: the first screen is Ålesund/Gjøvik service courses plus two not-taught-in-2026 rows, and TMA4100 is 4.8 screens down. A student searching "teknologi" for TMM4550 gets "viser 200 av 242 treff" with TMM4550 absent — and the 42 that were cut are 93 % Trondheim, so the biggest campus is the one silently truncated.
- Fix: In `render()` (src/pages/emner/index.astro:315), score before slicing: folded code === query (4) > code startsWith (3) > name startsWith (2) > query at a word boundary in name (1) > substring (0), stable-sort descending, then `slice(0, RESULT_CAP)`. Optionally demote `stale` rows one step. Add one `<li>` after the last row when `filtered.length > RESULT_CAP` ("42 flere treff — presiser søket") so the truth is also where the list ends, not only in the status 200 rows above.
