- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways: (a) looked for a documented decision — PRODUCT.md §6/§9, REVIEW.md U15/D6, ROADMAP say nothing about ordering or the cap, so it is not an adjudicated trade-off; (b) checked whether the sibling planner search compensates — addCourse.ts:242-245 does the same unranked `filter().slice()`, so this is systemic, not a one-page slip; (c) re-measured the two headline numbers myself live (row 77 / offsetTop 4355; "viser 200 av 242", last row TMAT3020) and re-derived the dropped tail offline (42 rows, 39 Trondheim). All held. Kept at major: for the over-cap case the wanted course is unreachable, not merely low.
### [sec-2] Planner renders as a permanently blank shell when `window.localStorage` access throws — no try/catch, no fallback, no message
- Where: `src/lib/planner/store.ts:100` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `defaultStorage()` (store.ts:100-102) returns `window.localStorage` unguarded, so the property access itself throws in a storage-denied context; `createPlanStore` (store.ts:246-251) calls it before the `nullStorage` fallback can engage, so that fallback is unreachable for this failure mode. Layout.astro:89-96 and 115-119 guard the identical API with try/catch for the theme, so the hazard is known here — store.ts just doesn't handle it. Live Playwright probe (context `addInitScript` redefining `window.localStorage` to throw `SecurityError: Access is denied for this document.`, i.e. Chrome "Block all cookies") against http://127.0.0.1:8787/planlegger/: pageerrors = ["Access is denied for this document.", "Access is denied for this document."]; `#planner-grid-frame` innerHTML length = 0; `#planner-course-rows` innerText = ""; chip stuck at "Velg studieprogram". Screenshot v-nostorage.png: topbar, H1 "Semesterplan", the UKEPLAN and EKSAMENER kickers, "0 av 30 sp", then ~600 px of empty page — no grid, no empty state, no error. Same probe on /emne/TDT4100/ leaves the page stuck on "Sjekker planen din for Høst 2026 …". No test injects a throwing `StorageLike` (tests/planner/store.test.ts injects only a working fake), and no doc records this as an accepted limitation.
- Failure: A student with Chrome's "Block all cookies", or inside a storage-partitioned/sandboxed embed, opens /planlegger/. `createPlanStore` throws inside `onPage`'s setup, so `mountPlannerApp` never completes: the week grid, exam list and course rail are all empty, the chip never updates, and the flagship surface is a blank page with only a console error to explain it.
- Fix: In `src/lib/planner/store.ts`, wrap `defaultStorage()`'s `window.localStorage` access (lines 100-102) in try/catch returning `undefined` so the existing `nullStorage` fallback engages, and route the four `storage.setItem` calls (lines 280, 297, 299, 441) through a `safeSet` helper that swallows write failures. Add a vitest case injecting a throwing `StorageLike`. Ideally also surface a one-line Norwegian note when storage is unavailable, since the plan then cannot persist.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by finding a guard: `nullStorage`/`nullEvents` exist (store.ts:106-124) and look like the intended fallback, but `defaultStorage()` throws *before* the `??` can pick them up, so they never engage — I reproduced the blank planner and read the screenshot back to confirm it is genuinely empty rather than merely unstyled. Also checked PRODUCT.md §9/§12 and ROADMAP for an accepted-limitation note: none. Severity kept at major (total loss of the flagship surface, no message), while noting the affected population is narrow (Chrome block-all-cookies / sandboxed embeds; Firefox strict and modern Safari private both allow first-party localStorage). The auditor's second half (QuotaExceededError on `setItem`) also reproduced (pageerror "quota", empty rail, `#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136` never rendered), but I rate that scenario speculative — the planner stores a few hundred bytes, so a real 5 MB quota hit is implausible; it is worth fixing only because the same `safeSet` helper covers it.
### [store-1] A blocked or failing localStorage throws out of createPlanStore/savePlan and kills the planner and the course pages — empty week, no message
- Where: `src/lib/planner/store.ts:100` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Re-verified live on a healthy :8788 with my own probe (scratchpad/v2/a.mjs). `defaultStorage()` (store.ts:100-102) evaluates `window.localStorage` with no try/catch and `nullStorage` (line 109) is only reachable when `window` is undefined, so a denied-storage document throws before any fallback. With an addInitScript making the `localStorage` getter throw `SecurityError`, `/planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136` produced: 2 uncaught pageerrors (`SecurityError: Access is denied for this document.`), `#planner-title` stuck at the static "Semesterplan", `#planner-course-rows` empty, 0 `.planner-block` elements, `#planner-credit-line` "0 av 30 sp". The same probe on `/emne/TDT4100/` also threw 2 uncaught SecurityErrors, so the course page's "Legg til i planen" button is dead too. Second run with `Storage.prototype.setItem` throwing QuotaExceededError on `/planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,%2BPSY1000`: 1 uncaught pageerror, empty course rail, "0 av 30 sp". store.ts's own docstring promises the store "falls back to inert no-op storage/events rather than touching `window`" — it delivers that only for the non-DOM case, not the denied case.
- Failure: A student with Chrome's "Block all cookies" (or in a sandboxed embed / a webview with DOM storage off) opens a friend's shared plan link. `mountPlannerApp` throws at `createPlanStore(defaultSemesterId)` before the first render: title stays "Semesterplan", the week is blank, the credit line reads "0 av 30 sp", and nothing says storage is blocked. The only available reading is "this site is broken".
- Fix: src/lib/planner/store.ts: wrap `defaultStorage()`'s `window.localStorage` access in try/catch returning `undefined` (so `nullStorage` engages), and route the four `storage.setItem` calls (lines 280, 297, 299, 441) through a local `safeSet()` that swallows write errors. Reads are already fresh-from-storage, so the session degrades to "works, does not persist" instead of dying.
- Verifier: I tried to kill this three ways: (a) looked for a try/catch or guard on any caller of `createPlanStore` — none in plannerApp.ts, popover.ts, addCourse.ts, index.astro, emne/[code].astro, emner/index.astro or Layout.astro; (b) checked whether `nullStorage` could catch it — it cannot, the ternary at line 101 evaluates the throwing getter itself; (c) re-ran the probe myself rather than trusting the auditor's, against a server I verified healthy (`/api/health` 200), so this is not the brief's outage artifact. It survived. Downgraded blocker → major: the failure is total but confined to users who have denied DOM storage; incognito/private mode is unaffected.
### [store-2] A study-plan fetch started for the previous programme resurrects it seconds after a program-less shared link cleared it — rewriting the plan, the storage and the URL with no user action
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1527` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `loadPeriodCourses` bumps `studyPlanFetchToken` only on the with-programme path (plannerApp.ts:1535); the `if (!program)` early return at 1527-1533 returns without bumping it, so an in-flight fetch for the old programme is never invalidated and its `token !== studyPlanFetchToken` guard passes. It then runs `store.savePlan({ ...plan, program: namedProgram })` (1578) and, because `programDerivedFor` was reset to null at 1531 while `derivationKey()` (1490-1493) now returns the program-less `-|26h`, falls into `store.setProgramPlan(namedProgram, toAdd)` (1585) with the captured old programme. Reproduced live myself (scratchpad/v2/f.mjs) delaying only `**/api/program/**/plan**` by 6 s: after `#26h;MTDT.2024;` then `location.hash = '#26h;-;%2BPSY1000'`, state at T+2.4 s was correct — `np:profile` `{}`, `np:plans` `[{PSY1000, manual}]`, title "Semesterplan", hash `#26h;-;%2BPSY1000`. At T+11 s with no interaction: `np:profile` `{"program":{"code":"MTDT","name":"Datateknologi - master (5-årig)","cohort":2024}}`, `np:plans` `[TDT4136(program), TMA4135(program), PSY1000(manual)]`, hash rewritten to `#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135,%2BPSY1000`, title "Datateknologi - master (5-årig) MTDT". The window is not hypothetical: `findProgramPlanUncached` (programPlan.ts:155-163) does up to `MAX_STEP_BACK_TRIES` sequential upstream round trips when a cohort's plan 404s.
- Failure: Student A has MTDT stored. She opens /planlegger/ on a slow connection, or for a cohort whose plan 404s (three sequential upstream fetches), and within that window pastes student B's program-less shared link — or commits "ingen studieprogram" in studieinfo (studieinfo.ts:630 calls the same `removeProgram`). The page correctly shows the pasted plan. A second or two later the banner, topbar chip, course rail, localStorage and the address bar all flip back to MTDT with MTDT's prefilled courses spliced in front of the pasted ones. She now holds — and may re-share — a hybrid plan nobody made.
- Fix: src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts: add `++studyPlanFetchToken;` inside the `if (!program)` branch (line 1527) before returning, and after the `await` at 1536 re-read `plan.program` and bail unless its `code`/`cohort`/`direction?.code` still match the captured `program`.
- Verifier: I tried to kill it by hunting for a second guard: the `token !== studyPlanFetchToken` check at 1537 is the only one and the early return provably bypasses it; `programDerivedFor = null` at 1531 actively guarantees the resurrect branch is taken rather than preventing it. I also checked whether the resurrect depends on the name-backfill path (`namedProgram !== program`) — it does not, `setProgramPlan` at 1585 fires regardless. Then I re-ran the race myself and got the exact flip. The one soft spot is that without an artificial delay the window is the real fetch duration, which is why I did not raise it to blocker.
### [store-3] `removeCourse` is not source-aware, so "Fjern fra planen" on a programme course's own page hard-deletes it — and the planner silently resurrects it un-dropped on the next visit
- Where: `src/lib/planner/store.ts:318` · correctness · CONFIRMED · regression of REVIEW.md D3 (partially applied — fixed in plannerApp.ts/popover.ts, never applied to the three off-planner call sites)
- Evidence: `removeCourse` (store.ts:318-323) filters purely on code with no `source` check, while `dropCourse` (334-346) refuses anything that is not `source:"program"`. plannerApp.ts:929-935 and popover.ts:233-239 branch on `course.source` before choosing drop vs remove; the three off-planner call sites do not — src/pages/emne/[code].astro:491, src/pages/emner/index.astro:387, src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:218. Reproduced live myself (scratchpad/v2/e.mjs): with MTDT/2024 derived for 26h (`np:plans` = TDT4136 + TMA4135, both `source:"program"`, credits 7.5), `/emne/TDT4136/` renders the button as "Fjern fra planen" (emne/[code].astro:461); one click flips it to "Legg til i planen" and `np:plans` becomes `[{TMA4135 …}]` — deleted, not dropped. Navigating to /planlegger/ then prints rows "TDT4136 … Dropp … fra programmet" and `np:plans` is back to both courses, because `programDerivedFor` starts null on every mount (plannerApp.ts:1497) and `sameProgramSet` now differs, so `setProgramPlan` re-adds it with no `dropped` flag. The planner's own "Dropp" writes `dropped:true` and survives, verified in the same session.
- Failure: A student opens /emne/TDT4136/ to read about a programme course, decides she is not taking it, and presses "Fjern fra planen". The button confirms "Legg til i planen". She opens the planner and TDT4136 is back in her week, her credit total and her share URL, with no message. Her removal is silently undone, and the reversible drop state PRODUCT §0.3 specifies is unreachable from that surface.
- Fix: src/lib/planner/store.ts: make `removeCourse` enforce the documented semantics itself — if the matched entry has `source === "program"`, delegate to the `dropCourse` body (mark `dropped: true`) and only splice out `source: "manual"` entries. That fixes all three unguarded call sites at once and leaves the already-correct planner/popover branches unchanged. Then label the buttons "Dropp"/"Legg tilbake" at emne/[code].astro:461 and emner/index.astro:254 when the matched course is a programme course.
- Verifier: I attacked reachability rather than the code: is a programme course ever reachable from /emne/ or /emner/ with the programme already derived? Yes — I did it end to end in a real browser against the real MTDT 2024 study plan, no seeded hash. I also checked whether this merely restates a fixed item: REVIEW.md D3 fixed exactly this verb/semantics split but only inside plannerApp.ts (the code comment at plannerApp.ts:924-926 cites D3), so this is an incompletely-applied fix, not a restatement. It is also not on the brief's deferred list (that covers /emne/'s inert group-picker and removeProgram's semester pruning, neither of which is this).
### [store-6] The same course can end up in the plan twice — `setProgramPlan` never dedupes the programme prefill against existing manual adds — inflating the credit total and inventing a red "samme dag" exam conflict with itself
- Where: `src/lib/planner/store.ts:386` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `setProgramPlan` (store.ts:376-403) builds `manual = plan.courses.filter(c => c.source === "manual")` and concatenates it with the freshly derived `program_` list with no code-level dedupe, while `addCourse` (305) does guard. Reproduced live with real clicks only (scratchpad/v2/d.mjs, screenshot scratchpad/v2/d1.png): on /emne/TDT4136/ press "Legg til i planen" (`np:plans` = one manual TDT4136), then on /planlegger/?studieinfo search MTDT, pick kull 2024, press Lagre. Result: `np:plans` = `[{TDT4136,program,7.5},{TMA4135,program,7.5},{TDT4136,manual}]`; the rail renders TDT4136 twice ("Dropp / fra programmet" and "Fjern / lagt til selv"); `#planner-credit-line` reads "22,5 av 30 sp" for 15 sp of actual courses while the panel says "Mangler 7,5 sp"; the exam section reads "2 eksamener samme dag" and draws TDT4136 vs TDT4136 with the red "samme dag" note — a fabricated collision against itself. The hash becomes `#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135,%2BTDT4136`, so the wrong state is shareable. The auditor's hash-only variant also holds (`#26h;-;%2BTDT4136,%2BTDT4136` → "15 av 30 sp", two rows) because `parsePlanHash`'s token loop (store.ts:676-687) pushes unconditionally, but that one needs a hand-edited link; this one needs two ordinary clicks.
- Failure: A student browses /emner/ or a course page first, adds TDT4136, then picks her programme and kull in studieinfo — the ordinary "I found a course, now let me set up my programme" order. The rail lists TDT4136 twice, the flagship credit line says "22,5 av 30 sp" when she has 15 sp, and the exam ribbon warns her in red that she has two exams the same day when she has one.
- Fix: src/lib/planner/store.ts `setProgramPlan`: drop manual entries whose code is already in the derived programme set — `const programCodes = new Set(courses.map(c => c.code)); const manual = plan.courses.filter(c => c.source === "manual" && !programCodes.has(c.code));`. Add the same one-line `Set` guard to the token loop in `parsePlanHash` (first token wins, so a `-`/`+` prefix on the first occurrence is honoured).
- Verifier: The auditor filed this as a minor hash-parsing nit reachable only by hand-editing a link, and I tried to kill it on exactly that ground. Instead I found the in-app path: I reproduced the duplicate with real UI clicks (course-page add, then studieinfo programme commit) with no seeded storage and no crafted hash, and the screenshot shows the damage is worse than a duplicate row — wrong credit total plus a false red exam collision, which is the one thing the product's honest-join promise cannot afford. Kept, re-anchored from `parsePlanHash` (store.ts:674) to `setProgramPlan` (store.ts:386), and raised minor → major.
### [tests-3] The suite now enforces the browsable course-page grade chart that PRODUCT.md D12 and ROADMAP.md:227 keep deleted
- Where: `e2e/flows.pw.ts:378` · product-conformance · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Verified every citation independently. docs/PRODUCT.md:419 (D12): "Grade stats only in the decision cell … Browsable grade trivia is DBH-mirror parasitism and violates the p4 discipline". docs/PRODUCT.md:252 asserts as present tense: "The browsable grade chart was removed from /emne/[code]/ per D12 (shipped) … The worker still serves /api/course/:code/grades — the data is reachable, no page shows it." docs/ROADMAP.md:226-227: "(D12: decision cells only — the browsable course-page grade chart stays deleted)". AUDIT-BRIEF.md's killed list repeats it. `git show --stat 94b5d9a` confirms the commit added src/components/site/gradeChart.ts, a Karakterer section in src/pages/emne/[code].astro, src/lib/planner/grades.ts, tests/planner/grades.test.ts and e2e/flows.pw.ts:378-390 "course page: the grade figure renders from DBH" — and touched exactly one doc, docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-course-page-and-week-density-design.md. I grepped that spec for `D12|PRODUCT|decision cell|parasit`: zero hits. PRODUCT.md and ROADMAP.md are unchanged, so line 252's "no page shows it" is now false. Live-verified the breach: `curl http://127.0.0.1:8788/emne/TDT4100/` returns one `grades-section` and one "Karakterer" heading; `curl /api/course/TDT4100/grades` returns the rows it renders.
- Failure: A maintainer reads D12 and ROADMAP.md:227, correctly deletes the browsable chart from /emne/[code]/, and runs the repo's own gates. `mise run check` fails 13 tests/planner/grades.test.ts cases and `mise run e2e` fails "course page: the grade figure renders from DBH". The suite reports the conformance fix as a regression, the fix is reverted, and the doc-violating feature is re-entrenched by the gate that exists to protect the docs.
- Fix: Adjudicate the conformance question first, then align the tests. If D12 stands: delete e2e/flows.pw.ts:378-390 and src/components/site/gradeChart.ts plus the Karakterer block in src/pages/emne/[code].astro; keep src/lib/planner/grades.ts and tests/planner/grades.test.ts (pure model, unreferenced by any page, wanted for the Phase 4 decision cell). If D12 is being overturned, record the reversal with a date in docs/PRODUCT.md §10 and update PRODUCT.md:252 + ROADMAP.md:227 + README — not in a superpowers spec doc that never names the decision it overrides.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as a mis-read of a deliberate decision. Checked whether D12 had been reversed anywhere: grepped PRODUCT.md and ROADMAP.md for D12/grade/karakter — every hit still says decision-cell-only and "stays deleted", and git shows neither file was touched by 94b5d9a. Checked whether the new section is a Phase-4 decision cell rather than the killed browsable chart: it is on /emne/[code]/, standalone under a "Karakterer" heading, with no verdict/shortlist context — i.e. exactly the browsable shape D12 names. Checked whether the commit argued the reversal: the message reasons at length about small-multiples vs stacked bars and palette ΔE, never about D12. Could not refute. Kept at major: this is the only finding in the dimension where a green suite actively punishes compliance with the definitive doc.
### [tests-4] reuseExistingServer is pinned true, so `mise run e2e` can skip the rebuild and certify a stale dist/
- Where: `playwright.config.ts:28` · test-coverage · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Confirmed by reading playwright.config.ts: webServer.command is `npm run build && npx wrangler dev --port 8788 --ip 127.0.0.1`, url is `/api/health`, reuseExistingServer: true (Playwright's own default for this option is `!process.env.CI`). worker/src/routes.ts's handleHealth returns `{ok:true}` unconditionally with no build reference, so ANY wrangler process answering on 8788 satisfies the probe and the `npm run build` half never runs. The repo's verification ritual rests entirely on this command: every recent commit message asserts a result from it (94b5d9a "mise run e2e: 26 passed", 665513f "mise run e2e: 23 passed"), and CLAUDE.md names `mise run e2e` as the only place navigation regressions are visible. CORRECTION to the auditor: the cited trigger is wrong — package.json's `preview` is bare `wrangler dev`, which binds wrangler's default port 8787 (README.md:44 says :8787), not 8788, so a running preview does NOT trigger reuse. The real triggers are a leaked wrangler from an interrupted prior e2e run and a manually started `wrangler dev --port 8788` — the second is exactly the state this audit runs in (AUDIT-BRIEF.md: "npx wrangler dev serving the real built dist/ ... on 8788, do not start another"), so it demonstrably occurs.
- Failure: A prior `mise run e2e` is Ctrl-C'd (or an agent's wrangler on :8788 is left up). The developer edits src/components/planner/grid.ts, introducing a pile-rendering regression, and runs `mise run e2e` to verify. Playwright probes /api/health, gets {ok:true} from the stale process, reuses it, never rebuilds, and drives Chromium against the pre-edit dist/. All 26 tests pass. The commit lands with "mise run e2e: 26 passed" in its message describing a run that never saw the committed code. CI cannot catch it if the diff also misses e2e.yml's globs (see tests-1).
- Fix: playwright.config.ts:28 — change `reuseExistingServer: true` to Playwright's idiomatic `!process.env.CI`, or better `process.env.PW_REUSE === "1"` so the default path always rebuilds and reuse is an explicit opt-in. Optionally make the readiness probe build-aware (have /api/health echo a build id) so a genuinely stale server fails the probe rather than satisfying it.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it three ways. (1) Checked whether the reuse path still rebuilds — it does not; Playwright skips the whole `command` when the url responds. (2) Checked whether /api/health could discriminate a stale build — read worker/src/routes.ts:98, it returns a constant, so it cannot. (3) Attacked the auditor's trigger and broke it: `npm run preview` is `wrangler dev` with no --port and wrangler.jsonc sets no dev port, so it serves 8787 while the e2e probe is 8788 — the README scenario the auditor leaned on does not fire. The finding survives on the weaker but demonstrated trigger (leaked/manually started server on 8788, which is the live state of this very audit). Kept at major because it is the only mechanism here that produces a FALSE GREEN in the command the repo's entire verification ritual cites; note it cannot ship to users, since release.yml re-runs everything in a clean runner.
### [ux-1] MTIØT's first-year week draws Matematikk 1 nine times under a green "ingen kollisjoner", with no note that they are alternatives — and the only escape hatch deletes half the lectures
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:772` · product-conformance · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live at /planlegger/#26h;MTIØT.2026; (1280×900): 10 blocks total, 9 of them TMA4400 — Mon 10:15 "Forelesning 1 MTBYGG, MTING, MTIØT…", Tue 08:15 "Forelesning 1 MTGEORT, MTINGGEO, MTENERG, MTIØT…", Tue 10:15 "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM", Wed 10:15 and 12:15 "Forelesning 2 …", Wed 14:15 Plenumsregning, Thu 08:15/10:15/10:15 "Forelesning 2 …". #planner-grid-status = "ingen kollisjoner"; #planner-grid-notes innerText = "" (empty). Traced: curl /api/course/TMA4400/timetable?year=2026 returns 21 entries; "MTIØT" appears in studyProgramKeys of 3 distinct "Forelesning 1" groups and 5 "Forelesning 2" groups, so entriesForProgram (src/lib/planner/schedule.ts:85) narrows nothing, and defaultLectureKeys (src/lib/planner/groups.ts:120-136) correctly declines to guess (looksLikeNumberedParallels at groups.ts:86 needs labels ending in digits; NTNU's end in programme codes) and returns []. grid.ts:772-783 builds the "velg gruppe" margin note ONLY for `!entry.isLecture` entries and only when revealOthers is true, so a lecture ambiguity can never produce one. I then attacked the auditor's own escape hatch: the block popover (#planner-popover) lists 13 radio options, none checked, none labelled "(din parallell)"; checking the first collapsed the week to TWO blocks — one TMA4400 "Forelesning 1" plus TIØ4105 — deleting the Forelesning-2 session and Plenumsregning, because popover.ts:200-206 models lecture groups as mutually-exclusive radios while F1 and F2 are sequential sessions the student attends both of.
- Failure: MTIØT kull 2026, Høst 2026: the very first week shows Matematikk 1 at Mon 10:15, Tue 08:15, Tue 10:15, Wed 10:15, Wed 12:15, Wed 14:15, Thu 08:15 and Thu 10:15 (twice), with the page asserting "ingen kollisjoner" and no text anywhere saying these are alternative parallels. If the student uses the block popover to narrow it, they end up with exactly one Matematikk 1 lecture per week and no Plenumsregning — a week now missing real sessions.
- Fix: Two parts, both small. (1) grid.ts:772-783 — extend the unpickedGroups collection to also cover LECTURE entries of a course that has more than one surviving lecture group and no explicit selection, ungated by `revealOthers`, so #planner-grid-notes renders "TMA4400: 8 forelesningsparalleller — velg din" as a button opening the existing picker. (2) popover.ts:200-206 — do not use a single radio group when the surviving lecture options are not one numbered-parallel family (reuse groups.ts's looksLikeNumberedParallels): render them as pre-checked checkboxes, so narrowing removes only what the student unticks and can never delete an unrelated weekly session. Do NOT route users into the current radio picker as-is — I verified it produces a materially incomplete week.
- Verifier: Tried to kill this three ways. (a) Checked whether groups.ts's no-guess branch is a documented deliberate decision — it is (groups.ts:110-119), but PRODUCT §0 addendum point 1 says "Parallels default to the programme's own", and the failure here is the silence, not the declining. (b) Checked the auditor's second example, MTDT kull 2026: REFUTED — entriesForProgram narrows MTDT to exactly one F1, one F2 and Plenumsregning, which is the correct week; I removed that claim. (c) Checked whether the block popover rescues the user: it does not — checking the first lecture radio collapsed the week from 9 TMA4400 blocks to 1, which makes the auditor's proposed fix actively harmful, so I rewrote it. Downgraded blocker→major: the flow completes, every block drawn is a real TMA4400 session that names MTIØT upstream, and the affected population is the programmes upstream over-tags (MTIØT clearly; MTENERG/MTNANO/MTKMB/MTGEORT partially), not every first-year.
### [ux-2] The provenance line is never recomposed after the timetable fetches resolve, so DR-8's "Fikk ikke hentet …" clause can never render and the line reports a state that is over
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1486` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: loadBundles() (plannerApp.ts:1463-1487) ends `renderCreditLine(); renderCourseRows(); renderGapLine(); renderGridAndExams();` — no renderProvenance(). grep confirms only three callsites (1460 renderAll, 1506 renderPlanDependents, 1668 the search-index .then), all of which run before the bundle promises settle. Decisive live repro, cleaner than the auditor's: /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026; with ONLY `**/api/course/TMA4400/timetable*` aborted (one flaky request, four other courses fine). After 8 s the week has 4 painted blocks and status "ingen kollisjoner"; the TMA4400 course row correctly reads "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Failed to fetch"; and #planner-provenance reads "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026) · studieplan for kull 2026. Uoffisiell." — still claiming a fetch is in flight, never "Timeplan hentet direkte fra NTNU nå", and with no "Fikk ikke hentet timeplan for TMA4400." clause, which lines 1411-1421 compose from state.bundle.errors. I also found a second shape of the same bug: on clean loads (#26h;MTIØT.2026;, #26h;MTDT.2024;, #26h;ÅENG.2026;) the line loses its first clause entirely — "eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026) · studieplan for kull 2026. Uoffisiell." — because the last render happened while every bundle was still null. PRODUCT.md:253 claims this surface is "Shipped on /planlegger/ (composed per-render from what actually happened: live timetable fetch … real per-course fetch failures)"; it is not.
- Failure: MTDT kull 2026, one timetable request fails. The week paints, the course rail says the fetch failed, and the one line whose entire job is to name the gaps says "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå" and nothing about TMA4400. On a fully clean load the same line never says where the timetable came from at all. The differentiator sentence (PRODUCT §1, DR-8) reports the opposite of what happened.
- Fix: Add `renderProvenance();` as the last statement of the post-await block in loadBundles (plannerApp.ts, after line 1486's renderGridAndExams()). That single line fixes both shapes: the source clause flips to "Timeplan hentet direkte fra NTNU nå" and the failures loop at 1411-1417 finally sees bundle.errors.
- Verifier: Tried to kill this by hunting for an indirect re-render: grepped every renderProvenance/renderAll/loadBundles callsite, checked whether loadPeriodCourses' renderPlanDependents (1500-1507) might land after the bundles and recompose the line, and whether store.onPlanChange (1606-1622) fires again post-load. None do reliably — proven empirically by four live runs (three clean, one partial-failure) where the final settled text was wrong in every case. The auditor's exact quoted string reproduces on the failure path; on clean loads the line is broken differently (no source clause at all), so I corrected the evidence. Kept at major, not blocker: the week itself is correct, only the honesty line is.
### [ux-3] A failed timetable fetch is misdiagnosed as an unanswered studieretning question, so the "Prøv igjen" retry is unreachable for every programme with a pending choice
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1279` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: plannerApp.ts:1278-1300 orders the fallback branches `if (question) → renderGridMessage(question.weekMessage)` BEFORE `else if (!published)` and `else if (failed) → "Fikk ikke hentet timeplanen." + "Prøv igjen"`, so a pending choice shadows a network failure; grid.ts:745-749 repeats the same shadowing internally (`rawEntries.length === 0` → `pending ? empty("pending-choice", pending) : …`). Reproduced live with a control: (A) /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024; with `**/api/course/*/timetable*` aborted → #planner-grid-frame innerText is exactly "Velg studieretning i studieinfo — ukeplanen fylles ut med en gang.", getByRole("button", {name: "Prøv igjen"}).count() === 0, while the course rows a few hundred px away both read "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Failed to fetch". (B) Control, same abort, /planlegger/#26h;ÅENG.2026; (a programme with no pendingChoice) → the frame correctly reads "Fikk ikke hentet timeplanen." with 1 "Prøv igjen" button. The only difference between A and B is the presence of a pending studieretning question.
- Failure: An MTDT/MTIØT/MIDT/MSIT/MLREAL student (every programme whose period carries a pendingChoice) loses connectivity mid-load. The week tells them to pick a studieretning. They pick one — the week is still empty, because the real problem was the network — and there is no retry button and no mention of the failure in the week frame. The identical page state for ÅENG offers a working "Prøv igjen".
- Fix: In renderGridAndExams (plannerApp.ts:1278-1300) test `failed` before `question` — a network failure is not a user-answerable question — and pass the failure state down into renderGrid so grid.ts:745-749 prefers the failure message over pendingChoiceMessage when every loaded bundle has `timetable === null`.
- Verifier: Attacked it by looking for a retry elsewhere on the page (there is none — count 0 across the whole document) and by running the ÅENG control to prove the failure branch itself works and that the pending-choice question is what shadows it. Also checked whether the ordering is a documented decision: the comment at grid.ts:718-727 defends showing the question over a BLANK week (B2), not over a FAILED one, and the comment at plannerApp.ts:1240-1244 shows the author explicitly cared about not misreporting failures (S6/T10) — so this ordering is an oversight, not an adjudicated call.
### [ux-4] A plan course whose timetable comes back successfully but EMPTY vanishes from the week with no note anywhere, under a green "ingen kollisjoner"
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:745` · data · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live: /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2024; (prefills TDT4136 + TMA4135). The week draws 2 blocks, both TDT4136; TMA4135 draws nothing. `curl 'http://127.0.0.1:8788/api/course/TMA4135/timetable?year=2026'` returns exactly `[]` — a successful fetch with zero rows, so bundle.errors is empty and no failure path fires. The catalog contradicts the emptiness: `curl /api/course/TMA4135` gives `"teachingStart":"Høst 2026"`, `"teachingDuration":"1 semester"`, and search-index has TMA4135 with offeredYears [2026,2025]. On screen: the course row reads "7,5 sp · fra programmet · Skriftlig skoleeksamen" with no marker, #planner-grid-status = "ingen kollisjoner", #planner-grid-notes = "", #planner-provenance mentions nothing. Traced why no marker: isOffSemester (plannerApp.ts:552-557) early-returns false for an empty array because `entriesForProgram([], code).length === 0`, so the "undervises ikke i valgt semester" tag never renders and the credits still count; grid.ts:745-749 only emits "Ingen timeplandata for emnene i planen ennå." when EVERY course is empty, never per course.
- Failure: An MTDT 3rd-year (kull 2024) opens Høst 2026 and sees one course in a week that should hold two. Nothing on the page — not the block area, not the course row, not the provenance line — says Matematikk 4D produced zero timetable rows. They plan around a Monday/Wednesday the site has silently left empty.
- Fix: In renderGridAndExams (plannerApp.ts:1221-1256) collect codes where `state.bundle !== null && state.bundle.timetable !== null && semesterEntries(state.bundle).length === 0`, and (a) push "Ingen timeplan funnet for TMA4135" into #planner-grid-notes through the same channel grid.ts's unpickedGroups uses, and (b) append them to renderProvenance's gaps list next to the "Fikk ikke hentet …" clause (which needs ux-2's fix to render at all).
- Verifier: Attacked the auditor's two examples separately. TMA4135 survives — I re-curled the endpoint (`[]`), re-curled the catalog (taught Høst 2026), re-ran the page and confirmed zero blocks, empty grid-notes, empty credit-note and silent provenance. The SPRÅK1201 example is REFUTED and I removed it: `curl /api/course/SPRÅK1201/timetable?year=2026` returns 3 entries, all titled "Dialog- og samarbeidsbasert undervisning"/"Formidling", which classifyActivity (activity.ts:128-136) deliberately calls "other" — hidden behind the øvinger/labber toggle by DR-1's documented asymmetric-risk design, not missing data. Also checked isOffSemester in case an existing marker covers TMA4135; it explicitly does not (its comment treats an absent timetable as unknown, and the `.length === 0` guard swallows the empty-array case with it).
### [ux-5] At 390 px the week's blocks are unreadable: course codes shatter into three fragments and the "guaranteed" start time is ellipsised away
- Where: `src/styles/planner-week.css:238` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Measured live at 390×844 on /planlegger/#26h;MTDT.2026; and read the frame screenshot back. Geometry: frame clientWidth 358, scrollWidth 384, .planner-grid computed min-width 336px (the @media (max-width: 40rem) 21rem rule, planner-week.css:66-70), grid box 336, document.body.scrollWidth 390 (no page-level scroll). Every .planner-block is 56 px wide; every .planner-block-code box is 29.6 px wide and 62.5 px TALL — three text rows for one course code. The screenshot shows "TMA4412" rendered as "TMA" / "■441" / "2", "EXPH0300" as "EXP" / "■H03" / "00", "TDT4109" as "TDT" / "■410" / "9" — the coloured dot sits inline inside the fractured code. The meta line renders visually as "R1 ·…": .planner-block-meta (planner-week.css:251-257) is `white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis`, so at 56 px the start time is cut, contradicting that rule's own comment "The guaranteed second line: room · start time". Course names all read "Forel…". One block (TDT4109) also reports scrollHeight 77 > clientHeight 72. The frame carries data-scroll="middle" with scrollLeft 24 and overflow of only 26 px, so the "Dra sidelengs for å se hele uken." hint (plannerApp.ts:1117) promises a horizontal reveal of 26 px; the code comment at plannerApp.ts:1099-1100 ("At 390 px the week frame is ~200 px narrower than its content") is stale.
- Failure: A student opens the planner on a phone and the week is a column of 56 px rectangles reading "TMA / ■441 / 2", "EXP / ■H03 / 00" with the second line cut to "R1 ·…" — no readable course code and no start time. Persona B's literal question ("when and where do I walk in") is unanswerable on the device most students will use, and the "drag sideways" hint moves the grid 26 px and reveals nothing new.
- Fix: NOT the auditor's fix — raising .planner-grid's phone min-width contradicts the documented A4 decision at planner-week.css:64-70 ("Five day names still have to fit on a phone, or the edge mask is hiding a column the student never knew existed"). Fix the block content instead, inside the @media (max-width: 40rem) block: (a) take .np-dot out of the code tag's inline flow (position it as a small marker on the block edge) so .planner-block-code (lines 238-243) gets the full column width and "TMA4412" wraps to at most two rows as its own comment intends; (b) drop the room prefix from .planner-block-meta at phone widths so the start time — the one thing the comment at line 250 guarantees — always survives instead of being ellipsised; (c) update the stale comment at plannerApp.ts:1099-1100 to the measured 26 px.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by checking whether the wrapping is deliberate: it partly is — the comment at planner-week.css:235-237 explicitly accepts "TDT4110 → two rows" and forbids an ellipsis on the code. But what I measured is three fragments with a dot wedged in, not two rows, and the ellipsis lands on the meta line the CSS itself calls guaranteed, so the shipped result is outside what the comments sanction. Also confirmed the 21rem min-width is a deliberate A4 decision, which invalidates the auditor's fix, so I replaced it. Kept at major: mobile is a primary case and the week is the primary surface.
### [ux-fail-2] An open studieretning question pre-empts both the pre-publish and the fetch-failed week states, producing a false promise and hiding "Prøv igjen"
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1279` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: Two live runs. (a) `/planlegger/#27v;MTDT.2024;` — banner correctly reads "kull 2024 · Vår 2027 · timeplan publiseres ~desember", yet #planner-grid-frame reads "Velg studieretning i studieinfo — ukeplanen fylles ut med en gang." (identical on 390×844, P-mobile-27v.png). I then answered the question via `#27v;MTDT.2024.MTDTDS-24;` and the same frame reads "Timeplan for Vår 2027 publiseres vanligvis i desember — kom tilbake da." — proving the earlier sentence was false. (b) With `page.route("**/api/course/**")` → 500 on `#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135` (I1-api-500.png): the week again reads "Velg studieretning i studieinfo — ukeplanen fylles ut med en gang.", the course rows read "fikk ikke hentet timeplan: Internal error", and `getByRole("button", {name: "Prøv igjen"}).count()` is **0** — state 3's retry affordance is unreachable. The chain at plannerApp.ts:1279-1291 tests `if (question)` before `else if (!published)` and `else if (failed)`, so the question always wins.
- Failure: MTDT kull 2024 planning Vår 2027 (the exact elective-planning window DR-2 exists for) is told the week fills in the moment they pick a studieretning; they pick one and get an empty week and a "come back in December" note instead. With the timetable API down, the same student is pointed at a studieretning picker while the only correct action — retry — is not rendered.
- Fix: In plannerApp.ts:1278-1315, order the branches by severity: `failed` first, then `!published`, then `question`. When a question coexists with an unpublished term or a failed fetch, compose both ("Timeplan for Vår 2027 publiseres vanligvis i desember. Velg studieretning i studieinfo nå, så er uken klar når den kommer.") rather than dropping the true half.

### [ux-fail-3] The DR-8 provenance line is a stale snapshot — it never re-renders after the fetches land, so it says "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå" forever and can omit real fetch failures
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1483` · correctness · UNVERIFIED · regression of UX-STUDY.md U9
- Evidence: Polled #planner-provenance every 400 ms for 12 s on a fully healthy plan (`#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135`). It settles on and stays at: "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026) · studieplan for kull 2024. Uoffisiell." — while the grid beside it is fully drawn with live blocks (TDT4136 VE1 · 14:15 Main lecture uke 34–46). Its first observed value was worse: "eksamensdatoer ikke publisert for Høst 2026 · …" (the index simply hadn't loaded). Cause: `loadBundles` (plannerApp.ts:1463-1487) ends with renderCreditLine/renderCourseRows/renderGapLine/renderGridAndExams and **not** renderProvenance; nothing else re-renders it once bundles resolve. Consequence for the gap-naming half of DR-8: on `#26h;-;+ZZZ9999` the course row prints "fikk ikke hentet detaljer: Not found" while the provenance line prints "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · eksamensdatoer fra katalogen (hentet 24. jul 2026). Uoffisiell." — the failure is never named there. It only appeared in my 500/abort runs because `loadPeriodCourses` happened to resolve last and call renderPlanDependents(); with no programme set that race never happens.
- Failure: Any student with a working plan reads a provenance line claiming the timetable is still being fetched, seconds after it arrived. Any student without a programme whose course details 404 gets a provenance line that names no gap at all — the "we show you exactly what we couldn't verify" promise silently doesn't fire.
- Fix: Add `renderProvenance();` to the post-await block in plannerApp.ts:1483-1486 (and to `retryBundles`), so the line always describes the settled state rather than whichever render happened to run last.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [ux-fail-4] Provenance asserts "studieplan for kull N" when the study plan 404'd, failed, or was silently substituted from another year
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1406` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: Three live cases, all reading the same clause. (1) `#26h;KNOAND.2024;` — KNOAND has no plan document at all (curl: 404 for year=2026, 2025 and 2024, matching REVIEW.md B8); `findProgramPlan` returns `{kind:"not-found"}` and `loadPeriodCourses` (plannerApp.ts:1538-1543) clears everything — yet the footer reads "Emnekatalogen hentet 24. jul 2026 fra NTNU · studieplan for kull 2024. Uoffisiell." (2) With `page.route("**/api/**")` → abort, i.e. every study-plan request refused: "… · studieplan for kull 2024. Uoffisiell." again. (3) `#26h;184BG.2026;` — /api/program/184BG/plan 404s for 2026 AND 2025 and only answers at 2024; the page prefills 5 courses, paints "30 av 30 sp" in the full-load accent green, and states "studieplan for kull 2026" (screenshot L-184BG-stepback.png). `findProgramPlan` returns the year it actually used (`{plan, year}`, programPlan.ts:150) and plannerApp.ts:1536 throws that `year` away. plannerApp.ts:1406 is unconditional: `if (plan.program) sources.push(...)`.
- Failure: A student on a shared link for 184BG kull 2026 sees a confident, green, full 30 sp semester and a provenance line naming a kull-2026 study plan that does not exist — the courses come from the 2024 curriculum. A KNOAND student is told we consulted a study plan we never received. The one line the product calls its moat is the line that lies.
- Fix: Track whether the plan fetch succeeded and which year it returned. In plannerApp.ts:1406 emit `studieplan for kull ${cohort}` only on a same-year hit, `studieplan for kull ${cohort} finnes ikke — viser kull ${result.year}` on a step-back, and `fant ingen studieplan for ${program.code}` on not-found/error (store the outcome in `loadPeriodCourses` alongside `periodMissing`).

### [ux-fail-5] B8 is still a dead end: the planner never says a programme has no study plan, and the modal's Lagre demands a kull it refuses to show
- Where: `src/components/planner/studieinfo.ts:586` · ux · UNVERIFIED · regression of REVIEW.md B8
- Evidence: Live modal run: opened studieinfo, typed KNOAND, picked it. Hint reads (correctly, honestly) "Fant ingen studieplan for dette programmet. Du kan fortsatt legge til emnene dine selv." and the kull section is hidden (`parentElement.hidden === true`). Pressing **Lagre** replaces that hint with "Velg kull for å lagre studieprogrammet." and leaves the dialog open — with no kull chips anywhere on screen (screenshot K-modal-after-save.png). The instruction is impossible to follow and it destroys the only honest explanation. studieinfo.ts:585-589 returns early whenever `program && cohort === null`, regardless of `programMissing`. On the planner side, arriving with such a programme (via the shared link `#26h;KNOAND.2024;`) shows the programme name in the banner, "Mangler 30 sp", and the generic "Legg til emner for å se ukeplanen." — no word that we have no plan for it. Sampling 31 of the 403 catalogued programmes, 8 have no 2026 plan and 5 of those have none at 2025/2024 either (KNOAND, MFIFILO, PHFY, MTPROD, PHAAR, KASP1-1-10, KSPA1-8-13 — including **MTPROD "Produktutvikling og produksjon", a five-year sivilingeniør programme**, so this is not a niche-programme problem).

Nothing saves; they either give up or press Avbryt and lose the explanation. If they instead land via a shared link, the planner shows their programme name over an empty week and never mentions that the study plan is missing.
- Fix: In studieinfo.ts:585, when `programMissing` is true skip the kull gate: commit the programme (cohort null is fine — nothing to classify) or, if PlanProgram must carry a cohort, drop the programme and commit the semester while keeping the hint. ### [ux-fail-7] If search-index.json fails to load, the exam section spins forever and the provenance falsely claims exam dates are unpublished
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:1338` · correctness · UNVERIFIED
- Evidence: `page.route("**/data/search-index.json")` → abort, on `#26h;MTDT.2024;TDT4136,TMA4135`. After 6 s (screenshot I3-index-fails.png): the grid renders normally with live blocks, but #planner-exam-status reads "henter eksamensdatoer …" and #planner-exam-list-host is empty — permanently, because `loadPlannerIndex().catch()` (plannerApp.ts:1670-1672) swallows the failure and leaves `plannerIndex === null`, while `examLoading = anyLoading || (states.length > 0 && plannerIndex === null)` (plannerApp.ts:1338) is therefore true forever. Worse, the footer reads "Henter timeplan fra NTNU nå · **eksamensdatoer ikke publisert for Høst 2026** · studieplan for kull 2024. Uoffisiell." — a false statement: exam dates for Høst 2026 exist (the working run lists "ma 7. des TDT4136"), we just failed to load our own 482 KB asset. plannerApp.ts:1400-1404 uses `indexCoversSemester(plannerIndex, …)`, which returns false for a null index, conflating "we couldn't load it" with "NTNU hasn't published it".
- Failure: On a flaky connection the exam column shows a spinner that never resolves, and the provenance line tells the student NTNU has not published Høst 2026 exam dates — so they stop looking for the exam clash that is actually there.
- Fix: Track a third index state (`null` = loading, `"failed"`). Set it in the `.catch` at plannerApp.ts:1670, make `examLoading` false when it is `"failed"`, render "Fikk ikke lastet emnekatalogen — eksamensdatoer mangler. Last siden på nytt." in the exam frame, and branch plannerApp.ts:1400-1404 on failed-vs-uncovered instead of on `indexCoversSemester` alone.
- Verifier: verifier never ran (session limit)
### [week-3] An all-muted pile block renders with a fully transparent background — an invisible clickable region of floating text
- Where: `src/styles/planner-week.css:187` · design-system · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live /planlegger/#26h;BIT.2026; with "Vis øvinger og labber" on: `getComputedStyle` of the pile returns `{cls:'planner-block planner-block-pile is-muted', backgroundColor:'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)', w:148, h:552}` while every sibling lab block resolves to a real `color(srgb …)` wash. Read the full-page screenshot back: Tuesday 14:15–18:00 is three lines of bare text — "2 emner · 14:15 / IT2805 / HMS0002" — with no fill and no edge, sitting beside four solid grey Laboratorieøvelse blocks on MAN/ONS/TOR/FRE at the same hour. Cause confirmed in source: `buildPileBlock` (grid.ts:567-600) never sets `--dot`, and `.planner-block.is-muted` (planner-week.css:186-189, specificity 0-2-0) beats `.planner-block-pile` (line 283, 0-1-0), so the pile resolves `color-mix(in srgb, var(--dot) var(--wash-course-muted), var(--card))` with `--dot` unset → invalid at computed-value time → transparent. `.planner-block-pile:hover` (0-2-0) likewise loses to `.planner-block.is-muted:hover` (0-3-0), so hover does not reveal it either.
- Failure: BIT kull 2026 student presses the one "Vis øvinger og labber" toggle — the flagship §0.4 flow — and Tuesday afternoon shows unboxed floating text with no surface and no hover feedback, next to four visually solid lab blocks. The pile does not read as a session or as clickable, so the popover naming its two courses is never opened.
- Fix: planner-week.css:283 — raise the pile's selectors above the muted ones (`.planner-block.planner-block-pile`, `.planner-block.planner-block-pile.is-muted`, and the matching `:hover`). Equivalent one-line alternative: `block.style.setProperty("--dot", "var(--fg)")` in `buildPileBlock` (grid.ts:~575) so nothing undefined is mixed.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by checking whether `--dot` might be inherited from an ancestor (it is not — the computed background is literally rgba(0,0,0,0)), whether the pile rule itself could be the invalid one (it is not — `color-mix(in srgb, var(--fg) 7%, var(--card))` is valid), and whether a border/box-shadow still bounds the block (none). Confirmed both in the computed-style dump and by reading the rendered screenshot. Survives.
### [week-5] MTIØT's default week draws nine TMA4400 lecture blocks for one course, with no note and "ingen kollisjoner"
- Where: `src/lib/planner/groups.ts:133` · ux · CONFIRMED · regression of UX-STUDY S5
- Evidence: Live /planlegger/#26h;MTIØT.2026; renders 10 blocks, 9 of them TMA4400: "Forelesning 1 MTBYGG, MTING, MTIØT…" (10:15), "Forelesning 1 MTGEORT, MTINGGEO, MTENERG, MTIØT…" (08:15), "Forelesning 1 MTDT, MTIØT, MTKOM" (10:15), "Forelesning 2 MTING, MTIØT, MTMART, MTDESIG", "Forelesning 2 MTIØT og MTMASKIN", "Forelesning 2 MTGEORT, MTINGGEO", "Plenumsregning", plus two 73 px side-by-side blocks ("Forelesning 2 MTENERG, MTIØT" and "Forelesning 2 MTIØT, MTKOM, MTDT") that report CLIP on both their meta and name spans. `#planner-grid-status` reads "ingen kollisjoner" and `#planner-grid-notes` is EMPTY — no affordance at all. Traced: `entriesForProgram` leaves several TMA4400 parallels naming MTIØT, and their labels end in a programme code, so `looksLikeNumberedParallels` is false and groups.ts:133 bails to no narrowing. The same course narrows to two blocks for MTDT, so the failure is programme-shaped. This is the exact rendering UX-STUDY S5 called a blocker ("renders parallels as simultaneous weekly slots … while the verdict line still reads 'ingen kollisjoner'"); the S5 fix simply does not reach this shape.
- Failure: MTIØT kull 2026 student opens /planlegger/ and sees Matematikk 1 apparently occupying five weekday slots (three mutually exclusive "Forelesning 1" alternatives and four "Forelesning 2" alternatives) for a course attended twice a week, with the verdict line claiming no collisions and no note explaining the duplication. They cannot tell which two blocks are theirs, and two of the nine are unreadable 73 px slivers.
- Fix: groups.ts:120-136 — when the programme filter leaves several *simultaneous or same-course* lecture groups, do not fail open silently. Draw the first as the provisional block and emit the affordance the øving layer already has (grid.ts's `.planner-note-groups`): "TMA4400 har N alternative forelesninger — velg din", opening the same popover picker. At minimum, emit that note in the lecture-only view too, where `#planner-grid-notes` is currently empty.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it as a restatement of the already-fixed UX-STUDY S5 — it is not: S5's fix landed (defaultLectureKeys + picker + notes) and works for MTDT, but this programme's data defeats it and the live week is still wrong. Also tried to kill it via the documented "leave ambiguous sets unresolved rather than guess" decision in the groups.ts docstring — that is a code comment, not an adjudicated product decision, and it produces exactly the state S5 forbade. Verified the notes list is empty in the default view, so the student gets no explanation. Survives at major.
## MINOR (82)
### [a11y-1] Picking a study programme hides the input that has focus, dropping activeElement to <body> with no announcement
- Where: `src/components/planner/studieinfo.ts:427` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live (empty localStorage, Studieinfo opened via `#studieinfo-chip`): initial focus `INPUT #studieinfo-program-input` (correct). Typed "Informatikk", pressed Enter → `document.activeElement` = BODY immediately and still BODY 2.5 s later after the plan resolves. Trace: keydown Enter → `pickProgram()` (line 330) → `renderProgramField()` → `programForm.hidden = true` (line 427), which hides the ancestor `<form>` of the focused input. `clearProgram()` (line 462) does the mirror-image move correctly (`programInput.focus()` at its end), so the pattern is established in the same file.
- Failure: Screen-reader student opens Studieinfo, types their programme, presses Enter. Nothing is spoken: the input they were on vanishes, focus is on body, and the newly-rendered Kull step is announced to nobody. Sighted keyboard users lose the focus ring for one keystroke.
- Fix: In `pickProgram` (330-339), after `renderProgramField()` move focus to the surviving control: `chipHost.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(".studieinfo-chip-remove")?.focus()`.
- Verifier: Reproduced with my own script. Downgraded from the auditor's `major`: I measured the recovery and it is one Tab, landing on `Kull 2026` — the semantically correct next step — because the dialog is `showModal()` so focus stays scoped to it (auditor's own log showed the same but the writeup implies the user is stranded). Real WCAG 2.4.3/4.1.3 gap for AT users, but survivable and self-correcting for sighted keyboard users. Not covered by any prior REVIEW.md item (A2/A5 are combobox semantics and skip link, both fixed).
### [a11y-2] Add-course dialog hides the "Legg til" button the moment it is pressed, so focus falls to <body> and the add is never announced
- Where: `src/components/planner/addCourse.ts:121` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live: dialog open, query "TDT4110", focus on `.np-btn.add-course-add | Legg til TDT4110 i planen` → Enter → `document.activeElement` = BODY, and the dialog's only live region (`.add-course-status`, aria-live=polite) still reads `1 treff.` — nothing about the add. Trace: click handler (addCourse.ts:213-216) → `sync()` → `setAddState()` → `button.hidden = inPlan` (line 121) hides the focused button; the "Lagt til ✓" confirmation is a plain `<span>` in no live region (line 206). The file's own comment at 105-112 shows the team already fixed the sibling case ("rebuilding the list on every add would throw focus back to `<body>` on the very button the student just pressed (mirrors emner's A5 fix)") — the hidden-button variant of the same bug survived.
- Failure: Screen-reader student searches TDT4110 in "Legg til emne" and presses Enter on "Legg til". The add succeeds but focus is on body and nothing is spoken; the only success signal is a visual ✓.
- Fix: Stop hiding the activated control: replace the add/added/remove triple with one persistent `<button aria-pressed>` flipping "Legg til" ⇄ "Fjern" (the pattern `src/pages/emner/index.astro` already ships), and write the confirmation into the existing `.add-course-status` live region ("TDT4110 lagt til i planen.").
- Verifier: Reproduced with my own script, including the live-region text. Downgraded from `major`: the dialog is `showModal()`, so focus stays scoped, and my Tab trace shows the very next Tab lands on `.add-course-remove | Fjern TDT4110 fra planen` — same screen position, and its accessible name does communicate the resulting state. The auditor's "may press Enter repeatedly" is overstated (Enter on body is a no-op). Real 4.1.3/2.4.3 gap, survivable.
### [a11y-5] Dark-mode --clash fails AA on the planner's collision sentence (4.42:1 on --bg) — a surface S11 never measured; the known-open S11 figure (3.62:1 on --clash-bg) is unchanged
- Where: `src/styles/tokens.css:192` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Measured from live computed styles in dark theme with ancestor-background compositing. (1) NEW: `.np-note-clash.planner-note-link` on /planlegger/ — text "Mandag 14:15–16:00 · IT1901 og TDT4160 kolliderer …", color rgb(209,77,65) = #d14d41 on composited bg rgb(16,15,15) = --bg, 13.44px/400 → 4.42:1 (AA needs 4.5:1 under 18.66px). Same token pair carries `.exam-gap.is-tight` (examList.ts:130) and `#emne-clash` (planClash.ts:179). (2) `.np-note-clash.home-proof-clash` on / — #d14d41 on rgb(62,23,21) = --clash-bg → 3.62:1. Light theme passes both (5.18:1 / 6.23:1). Independently recomputed the sRGB relative-luminance ratios: 4.42 and 3.62 exactly.
- Failure: A student on dark mode reads the single most consequential sentence the product produces — the margin note naming which two courses collide, when, and in which weeks — at 4.42:1, and the homepage proof-of-value clash line at 3.62:1. Low-vision users and anyone on a dim or glare-affected screen lose the collision warning.
- Fix: One token in tokens.css's `:root[data-theme="dark"]` block: lift `--clash` from #d14d41 to #e8705f. Verified by computation: 6.30:1 on --bg and 5.17:1 on --clash-bg, both clearing AA, with no change to --clash-bg, --clash-edge or the light theme.
- Verifier: Attacked on two fronts. (a) Prior-art: ROADMAP.md:57-61 lists "S11's dark-mode clash-text contrast figure" as **not touched, still open**, so half of this finding is a restatement of an acknowledged-open item and I stripped the auditor's `regression_of: S11` (nothing regressed — it was never fixed). (b) The other half survives: S11 measured only #d14d41 against the `is-clash` background; the --bg surface at 4.42:1 is genuinely un-measured, and it is the *planner's* collision sentence, not the homepage decoration. Also checked font-size/weight to be sure large-text AA (3:1) didn't apply — 13.44px/400, it does not. Downgraded to minor: the new surface misses by 0.08, and the fix is the same single token the already-tracked S11 needs.
### [a11y-6] Light theme: --accent-contrast on --accent computes 4.39:1, just under AA, on every accent-filled control including the Studieinfo "Lagre" primary
- Where: `src/styles/tokens.css:42` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Live tokens on /emner/ light: `--accent: #66800b`, `--accent-contrast: #fffcf0` → independently recomputed 4.39:1 (AA needs 4.5:1 below 18.66px). The pair is applied by `.np-btn[aria-pressed="true"]`/`.np-btn.is-active` (primitives.css:87-91), `.np-toggle[aria-pressed="true"]` (primitives.css:221-224) and `.studieinfo-save` (site.css:317-320, `background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-contrast)`), so it covers the Studieinfo primary at 13.44px/500, selected kull chips, the øvinger/labber toggle and /emner/'s selected city facets (all measured at 13.44px or 11.52px, weight 500). Dark theme passes at 6.12:1. DESIGN.md:45-48 mandates exactly this pair on accent fills and cites WCAG thresholds by number elsewhere, so it is a miss against the system's own stated standard.
- Failure: A student in light theme reads "Lagre" — the one button that commits the whole plan — and their selected kull chip at 4.39:1, repeated on every selected-state control sitewide.
- Fix: Darken the fill only, keeping the hue for links and rings: add `--accent-strong: #536907` (6.03:1 against the unchanged --accent-contrast, verified by computation) and use it in `.np-btn[aria-pressed="true"]`, `.np-toggle[aria-pressed="true"]` and `.studieinfo-save`.
- Verifier: Verified the token values from the live document (not the source), traced every named surface back to a real CSS rule that actually applies the pair (the auditor asserted `.studieinfo-save` uses it without citing the rule — site.css:317 confirms), checked font sizes/weights to rule out large-text AA, and recomputed the ratio myself. Also checked DESIGN.md doesn't adjudicate this pair as an accepted exception — it does not. Kept at minor rather than promoted: the shortfall is 0.11 and the fix is contained.
### [a11y-8] Studieinfo's zero-match and refused-save states are silent to assistive tech: a listbox reports expanded with zero options and a bare <li>, and the hint's text is written while its live region still carries hidden
- Where: `src/components/planner/studieinfo.ts:274` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Reproduced live. (1) Typing "zzzz" into the programme combobox: `{expanded:"true", aria-activedescendant:null, listbox role:"listbox", hidden:false, children:["LI/norole/Ingen treff."]}` — a role=listbox reporting itself expanded with zero role=option children, whose only child is a bare `<li>` that ARIA does not permit inside a listbox, in no live region (studieinfo.ts:273-279). (2) Pressing Lagre with a programme staged but no kull: dialog stays open, focus stays on `#studieinfo-save`, and the message lands in `#studieinfo-hint`. Verified `hint.hidden` was `true` before the press, and a MutationObserver recorded the mutations in exactly this order: `childList` (text set) **then** `attributes/hidden` (unhidden) — matching renderHint() lines 537-538, i.e. the content change happens while the live region is still `hidden` and therefore out of the accessibility tree. Also confirmed `#studieinfo-save` has no `aria-describedby`. The site's other live regions (`#planner-grid-status`, `#emner-status`) are always rendered and hidden by `:empty { display: none }` (index.astro:275) — the correct pattern.
- Failure: A screen-reader student mistypes their programme: the combobox says "expanded" with no options and nothing is spoken, so they keep typing into silence. Later they press Lagre without picking a kull: the dialog does not close, focus does not move, and "Velg kull for å lagre studieprogrammet." is rendered visually only — no feedback at all about why saving failed.
- Fix: (a) In the zero-match branch (273-279) either set `aria-expanded="false"` and move "Ingen treff." outside the listbox, or give the row `role="option" aria-disabled="true"` so the listbox stays valid. (b) In `renderHint()` (536-539) drop the `hidden` toggle and rely on `.studieinfo-hint:empty { display: none }` so the text mutation fires inside a live region already in the a11y tree; add `aria-describedby="studieinfo-hint"` to `#studieinfo-save`.
- Verifier: Tried to kill both halves. For (2) I did not trust the auditor's async MutationObserver instrumentation, so I re-ran it recording per-record order and separately confirmed `hint.hasAttribute("hidden")===true` immediately before the Lagre press — the record sequence [childList, attributes/hidden] proves textContent lands first, matching the source. For (1) I dumped the live listbox children and roles rather than reading the source. Checked whether the refusal is instead conveyed by focus or aria-describedby — neither. Kept at minor: both are AT-only, the visual path works.
### [app-3] The "link pointed at a semester we cannot plan" note is never cleared, so it keeps naming the wrong semester after the student deliberately switches
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:376` · copy · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: grep linkNote in plannerApp.ts: assigned only at :376 (inside planFromHash), cleared only at :1640 (the hashchange handler), printed unconditionally at :1450-1451 in renderAll. A semester switch goes store.setSemester -> savePlan -> PLAN_CHANGE_EVENT -> onPlanChange (:1606-1622), which calls syncHash() — and replaceState does not fire hashchange — so nothing on that path clears it. Reproduced live: loaded /planlegger/#25h;MTDT.2024; -> #planner-link-note "Lenken pekte på et semester vi ikke kan planlegge ennå — viser Høst 2026.", #planner-context-line "kull 2024 · Høst 2026". Opened studieinfo, selected 27h in #studieinfo-semester-select, clicked Lagre -> #planner-context-line becomes "kull 2024 · Høst 2027 · timeplan publiseres ~august", url becomes #27h;MTDT.2024;, and #planner-link-note still reads "…viser Høst 2026." with hidden=null and isVisible()=true.
- Failure: Student opens a shared 25h link, gets the honest fallback note, then switches to Høst 2027 in studieinfo. The banner, the context line and the grid all say Høst 2027 while a line directly above them insists the page is showing Høst 2026 — two contradictory statements about the resolved term on the same fold, on the surface (DR-8) whose whole value is that its disclaimers are true.
- Fix: In the store.onPlanChange callback (plannerApp.ts:1606), before `plan = next`, add `if (next.semesterId !== plan.semesterId) linkNote = null;` — the note only ever describes a semester substitution, so a deliberate semester change is exactly when it stops being true.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by looking for any other clear path (grep of every linkNote reference; checked whether the studieinfo save round-trips through hashchange — it does not, syncHash uses replaceState which fires no hashchange event), then reproduced the contradictory pair of strings live. Severity minor is right: cosmetic-but-dishonest, one line above an otherwise-correct page.
### [app-4] Dropp / Fjern / Legg tilbake drops keyboard focus to <body>: renderCourseRows() replaceChildren()s the button that was just activated
- Where: `src/components/planner/plannerApp.ts:894` · a11y · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: renderCourseRows starts with `elements.courseRows.replaceChildren()` (plannerApp.ts:894) and rebuilds every row + action button (905-960); the handlers at 930-935 call store.dropCourse/restoreCourse/removeCourse -> PLAN_CHANGE_EVENT -> onPlanChange -> renderAll -> renderCourseRows, destroying the focused button. Reproduced live: focused .planner-course-remove (aria-label "Dropp TDT4136"), document.activeElement's aria-label read "Dropp TDT4136" before; pressed Enter; after 800 ms document.activeElement.tagName = "BODY" and the rebuilt row's button reads "Legg tilbake TDT4136". No aria-live on #planner-course-rows either (grep aria-live: only #planner-grid-status, #planner-exam-status, addCourse.ts:91, studieinfo.ts:219), so nothing is announced. popover.ts:116-118 already solves the identical problem for its own inputs, so the in-repo pattern exists.
- Failure: A keyboard or screen-reader student tabs to "Dropp TDT4136" and presses Enter. The course greys out but focus is thrown to the top of the document with no announcement; to reach the "Legg tilbake" button that §0.3 promises is one tap away they must Tab back through topbar, nav, chip, theme toggle, region tabs and the whole week region.
- Fix: Mirror popover.ts: add `let pendingFocusCode: string | null = null;` in plannerApp.ts, set it to course.code in the three handlers at 930-935, and at the end of renderCourseRows re-focus `elements.courseRows.querySelector('.planner-course-remove[aria-label$="' + pendingFocusCode + '"]')` (the suffix still matches after the label flips to "Legg tilbake TDT4136") then clear it.
- Verifier: Tried to kill it by checking for any focus-restoration or aria-live compensation (grep across the planner: none on #planner-course-rows) and by reproducing with real keyboard activation rather than a synthetic click — activeElement was BODY afterwards. Severity minor stands: affects only keyboard/AT users and the page is still navigable.
### [astro-2] All 5470 statically generated course pages are undiscoverable: no sitemap, no robots.txt, and zero server-rendered links to /emne/
- Where: `src/pages/emner/index.astro:25` · product-conformance · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: Verified on disk and live: `grep -o 'href="/emne/[^"]*"' dist/index.html dist/404.html dist/emner/index.html dist/planlegger/index.html` → zero matches; the built catalog page ships an empty `<ul id="emner-results">` (source line 25) and `render()` deliberately returns before building any row while the query is empty (`src/pages/emner/index.astro:309-313`, live status "Skriv for å søke i 5470 emner."). `ls dist` shows no robots.txt and no sitemap; `curl -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}'` on /robots.txt, /sitemap.xml and /sitemap-index.xml all return 404; `grep -c sitemap package.json astro.config.mjs` → 0/0, so `@astrojs/sitemap` is not installed even though `astro.config.mjs:7` sets `site`. `find dist -name '*.html'` = 5474 of which 5470 are course pages. PRODUCT.md:177 (the corrected 2026-07-25 §4) states /emner/ is "still a real page for deep search and for search-engine landing"; grepping ROADMAP.md/PRODUCT.md for sitemap/robots/SEO turns up no deferral — Phase 3's deferred item is the *shared-plan* unfurl, a different thing.
- Failure: A crawler entering at / can reach only /planlegger/ and /emner/; /emner/ renders no rows without a typed query and there is no sitemap, so no course page is ever indexed. A student googling "TDT4100 timeplan" never sees the page that exists for exactly that query, and the 5470-page static build — 99.9% of the build cost and the entire reason `getStaticPaths` exists — is unreachable.
- Fix: `npm i @astrojs/sitemap`, add it to `astro.config.mjs`'s `integrations` (it walks getStaticPaths), add `public/robots.txt` with the Sitemap: line — and give a non-JS crawler one server-rendered entry: append a plain alphabetical `<ul>` of `<a href="/emne/{code}/">` to `src/pages/emner/index.astro` (split A–Z if 5470 anchors is too much for one document). Set the real hostname in `astro.config.mjs:7` first — it is still the placeholder `ntnu.martinsundal.no`.
- Verifier: Facts all re-verified independently (dist grep, three 404 curls, package.json grep). I tried to kill it as a deliberate deferral — grepped every doc for sitemap/robots/canonical/SEO and found only PRODUCT §4 asserting the opposite, and ROADMAP's Phase 3 "unfurl" item is about the shared plan hash, not crawlability. Downgraded major → minor: nothing here breaks for a live user, the site is not in production yet (placeholder `site` hostname, commented-out wrangler route), and the fix is additive. It stays because a stated product surface (search-engine landing) is 100% non-functional.
### [astro-4] /emner/ campus facets are dead controls until something is typed — aria-pressed latches, nothing on the page changes
- Where: `src/pages/emner/index.astro:309` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `render()` (src/pages/emner/index.astro:302-313) returns on an empty query *before* `activeCities` is consulted — the city filter lives at 316-317 inside the branch below — while the chip handler (433-438) toggles aria-pressed and calls `render()`. Reproduced live on a cold /emner/ at 1280×900: clicking the "Gjøvik" chip gave `{status: 'Skriv for å søke i 5470 emner.', rows: 0, pressed: 'true'}` — identical status text, identical (empty) list. Facets do work once a query exists ("matematikk" → "112 treff").
- Failure: A Gjøvik student lands on /emner/, taps "Gjøvik" to scope the catalog, sees the chip turn on and nothing else change — same count sentence, no rows, no note. The control reads as broken rather than as pending.
- Fix: In `src/pages/emner/index.astro`, apply the city filter above the empty-query early return and let the empty-query status reflect it: `const scoped = activeCities.size ? index.courses.filter(([,,loc]) => cities(loc).some(c => activeCities.has(c))) : index.courses;` then `Skriv for å søke i ${scoped.length} emner${activeCities.size ? ` i ${[...activeCities].join(", ")}` : ""}.`
- Verifier: Reproduced in Chromium exactly as claimed. Tried to refute it as the deliberate "no 200-row dump on an empty query" rule (REVIEW.md's /emner/ row, honoured at line 309-311) — that rule justifies showing no rows, but not a latched toggle with zero feedback; the count in the status line is the obvious cheap acknowledgement and is not shown. Held at minor: real, visible, survivable the moment the student types.
### [astro-5] Catalog search has no relevance ranking — results come back in raw index order (alphabetical by code), so the course being searched for is buried
- Where: `src/pages/emner/index.astro:315` · ux · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: `src/pages/emner/index.astro:315-323` is a bare `Array.filter` over `index.courses` then `slice(0, RESULT_CAP)` — no scoring, so output order is the crawler's (alphabetical by code). Re-ran the exact `fold()`+filter over the real 5470-row public/data/search-index.json myself: "matematikk" → 112 hits, TMA4100 ("Matematikk 1") at rank 77; "fysikk" → 103 hits, TFY4115 at 65; "programmering" → 41 hits, TDT4100 at 34; "statistikk" → 37 hits, TMA4240 at 31. Confirmed in the browser: typing "matematikk" gives status "112 treff" and first five codes `AR100919, BMA1010, BMA1020, BR100121, BR100131`; "fysikk" gives `AAR4335, BYGG2260, EP8001, ET8303, FI1004`. An exact name match earns no lift.
- Failure: A first-year types "matematikk" to add TMA4100 and gets AR100919 "Matematikk for økonomer", BMA1010, BMA1020, BR100121 … at the top; the course thousands of students take is row 77 of 112. Same for "fysikk" (64 rows before TFY4115) and "programmering" (33 before TDT4100).
- Fix: Score in `render()` before slicing: exact folded code match 0, code startsWith 1, name startsWith 2, name word-boundary 3, substring 4; `filtered.sort((a,b) => score(a)-score(b) || a[0].localeCompare(b[0],"nb"))`. ~10 lines, no new data, one sort over ≤2000 rows.
- Verifier: Recomputed the ranks myself rather than trusting the report (mine are 1-indexed; the auditor's were one lower, otherwise identical) and re-observed the first five codes live for three queries. Tried to refute it as acceptable because code searches dominate — an exact code query does return one row — but name searches are the documented use of the field ("Søk etter emnekode eller emnenavn") and they are the ones that fail. No doc defers ranking. Held at minor.
### [conf-3] Classifier misses the "Forlesning" misspelling and English combined titles ("Lecture and Lab exercise", "Lecture/Tutorial/Lab"), emptying whole courses' lecture layer
- Where: `src/lib/planner/activity.ts:64` · correctness · CONFIRMED
- Evidence: LECTURE_KEYWORDS covers one misspelling (`/forelesing/i`, line 64) but not the dropped-e form "Forlesning". Verified against the live API: LKRO001E 2026_HØST is titled "Forlesning/Øving sammen med MGLU2509" on every entry (Mon/Wed/Thu, weeks 34-38,40-41,45-47) — `isSlashCombinedLecture` splits it into ["Forlesning","Øving sammen med MGLU2509"], neither list matches "Forlesning", so `hasPlainLecturePart` is false and every entry is "other". Note the entry's `name` field is literally "Forelesning/Øving", which WOULD classify — but classifyActivity (line 129) only falls back to `name` when `title` is blank, so the correct signal sitting in the same record is never consulted. IT3708 2026_VÅR: 4 of its 5 entries are "2Forlesning/Øving" (Mon 08:15–10:00 and 12:15–14:00) → "other"; only the Thursday "1Forelesning" survives. IIK4100 2026_HØST: both entries are "Lecture and Lab exercise " — `\blab\b` fires, the title has no "/" so `isSlashCombinedLecture` (line 105) bails immediately → "other". AIS4004 2026_VÅR: its single entry "Lecture/Tutorial/Lab" hits the slash carve-out but `parts.every(...)` at line 115 fails on "Tutorial" → "other". My independent 120-course sample (shipped code, live data) puts 12/41 autumn-taught courses at zero lecture entries; several of those are genuinely signal-free (SPA1100 publishes only "Formidling" / "Dialog- og samarbeidsbasert undervisning" as titles, where no classifier could do better), but the four above are fixable string gaps.
- Failure: Student adds LKRO001E to an autumn plan. `renderGrid` finds zero lecture entries, `mutedLayerAutoRevealed` fires (grid.ts:756) and the week shows all 16 blocks muted with "Ingen aktiviteter er merket som forelesning i disse emnene"; `findConflicts` (grid.ts:786) receives an empty lecture set, so a genuine Monday 12:15–16:00 overlap with another plan course is never reported and the verdict line says no collisions. On /emne/LKRO001E/ the same gap surfaces as conf-1's outright falsehood.
- Fix: activity.ts: (a) add `/forlesning/i` alongside `/forelesing/i` in LECTURE_KEYWORDS; (b) add `/tutorial/i` to NON_LECTURE_KEYWORDS so "Lecture/Tutorial/Lab" passes the `every` guard; (c) let `isSlashCombinedLecture` also split on ` and `/` & ` so "Lecture and Lab exercise" is treated as the same combined session as "Forelesning/Øving". Add the four observed strings to tests/planner/activity.test.ts's `lectures` table. Cheaper alternative for LKRO001E specifically: when `title` yields "other" but `name` is a recognised combined-session string, fall back to `name` — but (a)–(c) are the narrower change.
- Verifier: Confirmed each string against the live API rather than trusting the report, and corrected two errors in it: the auditor wrote IT3708's titles as "2Forelesning/Øving" in one place (which WOULD classify as lecture) — the real title is "2Forlesning/Øving", and only 4 of 5 entries are affected, not the whole course; and SPA1100, which the auditor grouped with these, is not a classifier gap at all (its titles contain no activity kind, so "other" is the correct verdict). I downgraded major→minor because the direction of failure is the one DR-1 and activity.ts's header deliberately choose, and grid.ts:751-756 already ships an explicit B7a fallback that reveals the muted layer and says why — so on the flagship surface this is documented graceful degradation, not a wrong answer. What survives is a concrete, cheap keyword-list gap; the sharp edge belongs to conf-1, which is filed separately.
### [copy-1] Pile block counts distinct courses but labels them "emner" with no plural branch and "aktiviteter samtidig" in the aria-label — 17 blocks reading "1 emner" / "1 aktiviteter samtidig" on one course page
- Where: `src/components/planner/grid.ts:607` · copy · CONFIRMED
